Appendix A

Color Sorter Firmware

Colour-based product sorting conveyor, STM32F103C8T6

Enter steps through matches · Shift+Enter goes back

6,057 lines across 8 files Library and generated code excluded

What this listing contains

The whole of the application firmware for the sorting station: the main program and the three device drivers written for it. Files produced by STM32CubeMX and the STM32Cube HAL library are not reproduced, because none of them was written for this project.

Click any line number to put that line in the address bar, so a specific line can be cited or shared. Copy on each file header takes the whole file as plain text with straight quotation marks intact, which is the form a compiler accepts — a word processor that substitutes curly quotation marks will make the same code fail to build.

45/* ==========================================================================
46 * KIEN TRUC: HAI KHOI DOC LAP CHAY SONG SONG TRONG SUPERLOOP
47 * ==========================================================================
48 * KHOI A - CAM BIEN + DIEU KHIEN NGUON (chay tu luc CAP NGUON, VO DIEU KIEN)
49 * gy33_quet() : doc TCS34725 LIEN TUC, phat SU KIEN "XANH DUONG"
50 * ir_quet() : doc E18-D80NK LIEN TUC - CHI de HIEN THI va DEM
51 * nut_quet() : doc START/STOP - CHI dong/cat RELAY MOTOR
52 * KHOI B - CO CAU GAT (may trang thai gat_task), nghe DUY NHAT MOT dau vao:
53 * su kien XANH DUONG do khoi A phat ra.
54 *
55 * ⚠⚠ LIEN DONG BAT BIEN: khoi B KHONG CO duong tin hieu nao tu E18 va tu
56 * NUT NHAN; g_ir_vat tuyet doi khong duoc xuat hien trong gat_task(). Ly do:
57 * day tin hieu E18 chay song song day dong luc motor 24V nen bi kich gia
58 * thuong xuyen - neu E18 la diem VAO cua chu trinh thi moi lan kich gia la
59 * mot lan servo gat oan. Cat han duong nay thi nhieu tren PA0 chi lam sai
60 * BO DEM hien thi, khong the lam co cau chap hanh chuyen dong.
61 *
62 * TRINH TU PHOI XANH (co chang nang canh):
63 * xac nhan XANH DUONG XAC_NHAN_XANH lan lien tiep
64 * -> [G_NANG] truot 90 -> 180 do (nang canh), cho "den noi"
65 * -> [G_CHO_TROI] giu 180 do trong TRE_GAT_XANH_MS cho phoi troi toi
66 * canh gat (⚠ dem TU LUC TOI 180 do, khong phai tu luc thay mau)
67 * -> [G_DANG_GAT] truot 180 -> 0 do, cho "den noi"
68 * -> [G_GIU] dung yen tai 0 do trong GIU_XANH_MS
69 * -> [G_VE_HOME] truot 0 -> 90 do
70 * -> [G_KHOA] vu trang lai khi DU CA HAI: GY-33 da doc ra KHAC XANH it
71 * nhat mot lan, VA da qua NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS
72 *
73 * TRINH TU PHOI DO: goc cho = home va tre = 0 nen G_NANG/G_CHO_TROI di qua
74 * ngay, servo ra 0 do tuc thi, giu GIU_BAO_DO_MS roi ve home.
75 *
76 * Servo hoat dong DOC LAP voi bang tai: gat ke ca khi motor dang dung.
77 * ========================================================================== */
78 
79/* CHE DO DO MAU - bat len de DO LAI NGUONG MAU (vi du sau khi doi man che
80 anh sang). Khi = 1, firmware in LIEN TUC so lieu mau ra UART TX (PA9):
81 C | r g b | b-r b-g | ket luan. Cam FT232: PA9 -> RX, GND -> GND, 115200 8N1.
82 ⚠ Ban NoUART khong nhan duoc phim - day la kenh MOT CHIEU, chi doc.
83 ⚠ KHONG bat cung luc voi CHE_DO_BANG_LOG: hai luong in chen nhau, cot le
84 het. Bang log da co san so lieu mau; che do nay chi hon o cho in day hon
85 (5 dong/giay) va co ca so dem tho. */
86#define CHE_DO_DO_MAU 0u
87 
88/* KHOA CO CAU GAT: servo nam yen o home trong suot qua trinh do nguong, va
89 log khong bi cac dong cua may trang thai gat chen ngang.
90 ⚠⚠ PHAI DAT LAI VE 0 KHI CHAY THAT, neu khong servo se KHONG BAO GIO gat.
91 Trieu chung neu quen: cam bien nhan dung mau, log day du, den PC13 nhay
92 deu, nhung servo im hoan toan (g_tu_dong = 0 lam gat_task() thoat ngay
93 dong dau). Khong phai loi nguong mau. */
94#define CHE_DO_KHOA_SERVO 0u
95 
96/* Gian cach in mot dong so lieu o che do do (ms). O gain/ATIME mac dinh mot
97 phep do chi ton ~3ms nen neu in moi lan doc se ra ~190 dong/giay - khong
98 doc noi bang mat va UART thanh nut co chai. 200ms = 5 dong/giay, vua du
99 nhanh de thay phoi troi qua ma van doc duoc. */
100#define DO_MAU_CHU_KY_MS 200u
101 
102/* Cu bao nhieu dong thi in lai dong tieu de cot (kem nguong dang dung, muc
103 nen dang bam, gain/ATIME). De cuon man hinh len van biet cot nao la gi. */
104#define DO_MAU_SO_DONG_TD 20u
105 
106/* SO LAN THU TU DO NEN luc khoi dong, va gian cach giua hai lan (ms).
107 Xem khoi giai thich o cho goi TCS_HocSanToi() trong main(). Tong thoi gian
108 xau nhat = 5 x 2s = 10s, chi xay ra khi that su co van de. */
109#define HOC_NEN_SO_LAN_THU 5u
110#define HOC_NEN_CHO_MS 2000u
111 
112/* HOC LAI NEN SAU KHI MOTOR DA CHAY. TCS_HocSanToi() chay o cuoi main() khi
113 motor con DUNG (MOTOR_TAT o dau main() la yeu cau an toan), nen moc nen
114 luon duoc hoc o mot dieu kien chieu sang roi he lam viec o dieu kien khac:
115 do that cho b-r = +28 khi motor dung va +32 khi motor chay, sai so 4 don
116 vi do nam lai vinh vien trong moi nguong.
117 ⚠ BA CHOT chong hoc nham mot cai PHOI thanh nen: (1) chi dem nhung lan doc
118 DA KET LUAN LA TRONG nen phep hoc lai chi TINH CHINH trong vung nen; (2)
119 hai lan doc lien tiep lech qua 2 don vi la dem lai tu dau; (3) CHI MOT LAN
120 moi lan cap nguon. */
121#define HOC_LAI_SO_LAN 15u /* ~2.3s bang tai trong, on dinh */
122 
123/* Loc nhieu IR E18-D80NK: lay mau moi 10ms, can 3 mau giong nhau lien tiep
124 (30ms) moi doi trang thai - khong tin 1 canh don le. */
125#define IR_MAU_MS 10u
126/* 6 mau (60ms) chu khong phai 3: motor DC 24V dong ngat qua relay phat xung
127 nhieu manh, day tin hieu IR chay song song day dong luc rat de bi kich
128 gia, ma moi lan kich gia la mot chu trinh phan loai chay oan. Vat that che
129 tia IR lau hon 60ms rat nhieu nen tang nguong nay khong lam bo sot phoi. */
130#define IR_SO_MAU 6u
131 
132/* ================== THONG SO CO CAU GAT (KHOI B) ========================= */
133 
134/* GOC NGHI = goc servo ve ngay khi cap nguon. Servo_Init nap do rong xung
135 TRUOC khi bat PWM nen xung dau tien da dung goc nay - servo di thang ve
136 day. Home = 90 do => xung 1500us, dung diem GIUA tam co khi cua MG996R.
137 ⚠ CANH GAT PHAI DUOC LAP DUNG THEO GOC NAY: dua servo ve home roi moi lap
138 canh, neu khong canh se nghi giua duong bang tai va CHAN PHOI. MG996R 25
139 rang = 14.4 do/rang nen khong the bu bang cach lech rang.
140 ⚠ GOC_NGHI_DO va GOC_GAT_DO PHAI KHAC NHAU. */
141#define GOC_NGHI_DO 90u /* goc servo luc nghi (home) */
142 
143/* GOC CHO cua chu trinh XANH ("nang canh len"). Khi xac nhan phoi XANH,
144 servo truot len 180 do TRUOC, dung o do dem TRE_GAT_XANH_MS cho phoi troi
145 toi, roi gat mot mach 180 -> 0 do.
146 Y NGHIA VAT LY: nang canh gat len khoi mat bang tai de phoi troi vao dung
147 tam, roi quet TOAN DAI 180 do de hat phoi ra - luc gat co ca quang duong
148 lay da chu khong chi 90 do.
149 ⚠ Chu trinh DO KHONG dung goc nay (goc cho cua DO = home). */
150#define GOC_CHO_XANH_DO 180u /* goc servo cho phoi xanh troi toi */
151/* Goc gat = 0 do, tuc hanh trinh gat cua phoi XANH la TOAN DAI 180 do
152 (~800ms). Khong can sua timing: may trang thai CHO Servo_DenNoi() chu
153 khong doan thoi gian, va TIMEOUT_GAT_MS (15s) con thua rat nhieu.
154 ⚠ Quet toan dai keo dong dinh cao hon - nguon servo phai du khoe; thay MCU
155 reset luc gat thi do sut ap, khong phai code. */
156#define GOC_GAT_DO 0u /* goc servo luc gat */
157 
158/* CHAN O KHAU BIEN DICH. Servo_DatGoc() KEP moi gia tri > 180 ve 180, nen
159 mot goc dat sai (vd 190) van chay dung 2000us y het 180 - "sua xong khong
160 co tac dung gi" - ma cac dong thong bao lai in thang macro ra nen terminal
161 bao "truot 190 do", rat de truy oan. Tu day sai nguong khong bien dich duoc.
162 ⚠ Muon canh gat ra XA HON 2000us thi KHONG duoc nang so nay - phai do lai
163 chan co that cua servo roi nang SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US, hoac lech mot rang khop
164 tren truc (MG996R 25 rang = 14.4 do/rang). */
165#if (GOC_NGHI_DO > 180u) || (GOC_GAT_DO > 180u)
166#error "GOC_NGHI_DO / GOC_GAT_DO phai <= 180 - Servo_DatGoc() se kep am tham"
167#endif
168#if (GOC_NGHI_DO == GOC_GAT_DO)
169#error "GOC_NGHI_DO va GOC_GAT_DO PHAI KHAC NHAU - canh gat se khong di chuyen"
170#endif
171/* Chan cung cho goc CHO cua chu trinh XANH */
172#if (GOC_CHO_XANH_DO > 180u)
173#error "GOC_CHO_XANH_DO phai <= 180 - Servo_DatGoc() se kep am tham"
174#endif
175#if (GOC_CHO_XANH_DO == GOC_GAT_DO)
176#error "GOC_CHO_XANH_DO trung GOC_GAT_DO - chang gat XANH se khong di chuyen"
177#endif
178 
179/* TRE VAN CHUYEN cua chu trinh XANH: servo giu nguyen tai 180 do bao lau
180 truoc khi bat dau gat. Y NGHIA VAT LY: thoi gian phoi troi tu tam nhin cam
181 bien toi canh gat.
182 ⚠ MOC DEM: 2 giay nay dem TU LUC SERVO TOI 180 DO, khong phai tu luc xac
183 nhan mau. Tong tu luc thay phoi xanh den luc canh gat cham phoi:
184 ~400ms (nang 90->180) + 2000ms (dung yen) + ~800ms (gat 180->0) = ~3.2s
185 ⚠ Canh gat danh HUT phia sau phoi thi HA con so nay xuong ~1550 (KHONG
186 phai tang toc do servo); danh TRUOC mat phoi thi tang. */
187#define TRE_GAT_XANH_MS 2000u
188 
189/* Giu yen tai GOC_GAT_DO bao lau roi moi tra ve home (ms).
190 Nghi o day de servo DUNG HAN roi moi doi chieu - doi chieu ngay luc con
191 quan tinh chinh la cai lam servo GIAT va rut dong dot bien. */
192#define GIU_XANH_MS 1000u
193 
194/* ==================== CHU TRINH BAO PHOI DO ==============================
195 Thay phoi DO thi servo tu HOME 90 do ve 0 do NGAY LAP TUC, giu 5 giay roi
196 ve home (khong di qua 180 do - chi chu trinh XANH nang canh).
197 "DO" lay bang TCS_MAU_KHAC, tuc MOI vat khong phai xanh duong; day chuyen
198 chi co hai loai phoi nen khong can lop nhan dien DO rieng. ⚠ He qua: phoi
199 trang / xanh la / vat la cung kich chu trinh nay.
200 ⚠ GOC_BAO_DO = 0 = DUNG BANG GOC_GAT_DO cua phoi XANH, tuc VI TRI GAT THAT
201 SU: canh gat ra do ngay khi phoi con o cho cam bien roi nam yen 5 giay, ma
202 phoi mat ~2 giay de troi toi => PHOI DO RAT CO THE BI GAT RA LUON. Khong
203 muon the thi ha GIU_BAO_DO_MS xuong duoi ~1500ms, hoac tra GOC_BAO_DO ve 90. */
204#define GOC_BAO_DO 0u /* goc servo khi bao phoi DO */
205#define TRE_BAO_DO_MS 0u /* 0 = truot ngay, khong cho phoi troi */
206#define GIU_BAO_DO_MS 5000u /* giu tai GOC_BAO_DO 5 giay roi ve home */
207 
208#if (GOC_BAO_DO > 180u)
209#error "GOC_BAO_DO phai <= 180 - Servo_DatGoc() se kep am tham"
210#endif
211#if (GOC_BAO_DO == GOC_NGHI_DO)
212#error "GOC_BAO_DO trung GOC_NGHI_DO - canh gat se khong di chuyen"
213#endif
214 
215/* So lan doc MAU KHAC LIEN TIEP moi kich chu trinh bao do.
216 ⚠ De THAP thi co rui ro bo sot phoi xanh: luc phoi XANH vua troi VAO tam
217 nhin, cam bien thay mot phan phoi + mot phan nen nen van mau nam giua nen
218 va xanh, co the ra MAU KHAC vai lan doc TRUOC khi du dieu kien xanh. Neu
219 may lan do du kich chu trinh DO thi servo ve 0 do va khoa 5 giay, phoi
220 XANH that di qua trong luc khoa => bo sot hoan toan. Gap trieu chung do
221 thi NANG so nay len 3-4 - do la cho sua DAU TIEN.
222 ⚠ Doi so nay KHONG lam do nhanh len: dieu kien kich do la HAI VE AND (so
223 lan doc VA TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS), ve thoi gian luon chan truoc. */
224#define XAC_NHAN_DO 1u
225 
226/* THOI GIAN ON DINH cua chu trinh DO - chong loi "phoi XANH cung chay chu
227 trinh DO". Khong co no thi hai chu trinh dua nhau khong can suc: XANH phai
228 cho 2000ms moi truot, con DO truot NGAY. Luc phoi XANH vua vao tam nhin,
229 van mau chuyen tiep ra MAU_KHAC vai lan doc la DO kich ngay, servo ra 0 do
230 va khoa 5 giay, phoi xanh that di qua trong luc khoa.
231 CACH CHUA: bat DO phai thay MAU_KHAC LIEN TUC du mot khoang THOI GIAN; nhanh
232 XANH van duoc xet truoc moi vong lap nen phoi xanh kip thang, con chuyen
233 tiep mep phoi chi vai chuc ms nen bi loai het.
234 ⚠ Dung THOI GIAN THAT chu khong dem so lan doc - toc do vong lap thay doi.
235 ⚠ DANH DOI: phoi DO nam trong tam nhin ngan hon so nay se bi bo qua (bang
236 tai hien tai cho phoi qua ~0.5-1s nen 250ms con an toan). Nguoc lai, chuyen
237 tiep mep phoi XANH keo dai hon 250ms thi loi tren quay lai - dau hieu la
238 phoi xanh ra log "PHOI DO" va servo giu 5 giay thay vi 1 giay. */
239#define TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS 250u
240 
241/* ==================== TOC DO SERVO THEO TUNG CHANG =======================
242 So us do rong xung doi moi khung PWM 20ms; ca dai 180 do = 1000us nen
243 25us -> ~800ms (EM) va 40us -> ~500ms (NHANH).
244 VI SAO CHIA THEO CHANG: chay 40us thi servo bi lenh 360 do/giay, sat kha
245 nang thuc cua MG996R o 6V (~430 do/giay khong tai) - het bien khi co tai
246 canh gat nen chieu phai KEO NGUOC (ve home) bi giat, con chieu di gat duoc
247 trong luc giup nen van bam kip. Vi vay chi DO di 0 do chay NHANH (bao hieu
248 dut khoat), ba chang con lai chay EM. */
249#define BUOC_EM 25u /* ~800ms ca dai 180 do */
250#define BUOC_NHANH 40u /* ~500ms ca dai 180 do */
251 
252/* BUOC_XANH_NANG - toc do chang 90 -> 180 do. Chay EM vi day dung la chieu
253 PHAI KEO NGUOC TAI, tuc chieu de lo ra loi "giat nhe" khi chay nhanh.
254 BANG THOI GIAN THUC TE (home = 90 do):
255 XANH nang 90 -> 180 : 500us / 25us = ~400ms
256 XANH gat 180 -> 0 : 1000us / 25us = ~800ms (toan dai)
257 XANH ve 0 -> 90 : 500us / 25us = ~400ms
258 DO di 90 -> 0 : 500us / 40us = ~260ms (nhanh)
259 DO ve 0 -> 90 : 500us / 25us = ~400ms */
260#define BUOC_XANH_NANG BUOC_EM
261#define BUOC_XANH_DI BUOC_EM
262#define BUOC_XANH_VE BUOC_EM
263#define BUOC_DO_DI BUOC_NHANH
264#define BUOC_DO_VE BUOC_EM
265 
266/* SO LAN DOC XANH DUONG LIEN TIEP moi duoc coi la CHAC va cho gat.
267 Moi lan doc ton mot chu ky tich phan (154ms) cong ~20ms cua so phuc vu,
268 nen 2 lan doi hoi phoi nam trong tam nhin ~350ms LIEN TUC. Muc dich: chan
269 mot lan doc don le bi sai do nhieu tu motor 24V danh vao bus I2C (day la
270 lop chan THU HAI - driver da co bo loc on dinh 2 lan doc ben trong).
271 ⚠ DANH DOI: cang cao cang chac nhung phoi phai o trong tam nhin cang lau.
272 1 = kich ngay (mot lan doc sai la gat oan) | 2 = dang dung | 3 = chac nhat.
273 ⚠ Moc thoi gian tren tinh theo ATIME = 0xC0; doi ATIME thi phai xet lai. */
274#define XAC_NHAN_XANH 2u
275 
276/* BIEN AN TOAN TANG UNG DUNG - chong loi "servo gat oan khi chua co phoi".
277 Nguong cua DRIVER mot minh khong du: bien giua nen va nguong xanh cua
278 driver co luc chi con vai don vi, nhieu vai don vi tren d_br la ra XANH
279 GIA => servo gat lien tuc du chua co phoi nao.
280 BA LOP DOC LAP (khong lop nao dung tin hieu E18):
281 Lop 1: bien nay - phai co d_br >= nen + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN moi tinh la
282 xanh "chac"; tu dong bam theo nen ke ca khi nen tu bam doi moc.
283 Lop 2: phai co XAC_NHAN_XANH lan doc LIEN TIEP dat lop 1.
284 Lop 3: canh len - truoc do phai thay bang tai TRONG on dinh, nen mot cai
285 nen dung yen mai mai khong bao gio tu kich duoc.
286 ⚠ CHINH SO NAY THE NAO - so do that tren rig CO MAN CHE:
287 DO b-r = +20 | NEN b-r = +28 (dao dong 0) | XANH b-r = +38
288 Voi bien 6 thi nguong gat = +34: cach NEN 6 don vi, cach XANH 4 don vi.
289 ⚠⚠ KHONG nang qua 9 (= 37 - 28): nguong gat vuot qua phoi xanh thi
290 la_xanh_chac() luon tra ve 0 va SERVO KHONG BAO GIO GAT (log bao thay xanh,
291 LED PC13 nhay binh thuong, servo im, g_dem_xanh_yeu tang).
292 ⚠ Bien nay MONG - chi an toan vi nhieu bang KHONG khi bang tai dung. Gat
293 oan luc MOTOR CHAY thi cach chua DUNG la siet vong co chan sang, khong
294 phai nang so nay. Bo sot phoi -> GIAM, toi thieu 5. */
295#define BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN 6
296 
297/* So lan doc TRONG lien tiep de cong nhan "bang tai da trong that su".
298 Dung cho lop 3 (canh len) va cho dieu kien vu trang lai sau moi lan gat. */
299#define XAC_NHAN_TRONG 3u
300 
301/* NGHI TOI THIEU sau khi servo ve toi home moi duoc gat lan ke tiep.
302 Day la MOT trong HAI dieu kien vu trang lai (dieu kien con lai la GY-33
303 phai doc ra KHAC XANH it nhat mot lan). Can ca hai de mot phoi xanh dung
304 yen truoc cam bien khong lam servo gat lap di lap lai. */
305#define NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS 800u
306 
307/* ===================== MOTOR BANG TAI + NUT NHAN ========================= */
308/* Relay kich muc CAO (jumper High Level Trigger tren module) */
309#define MOTOR_BAT() HAL_GPIO_WritePin(RELAY_MOTOR_GPIO_Port, RELAY_MOTOR_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET)
310#define MOTOR_TAT() HAL_GPIO_WritePin(RELAY_MOTOR_GPIO_Port, RELAY_MOTOR_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET)
311 
312/* Nut nhan: POLLING + loc mau, KHONG dung EXTI.
313 Ly do: moi truong co motor DC 24V, xung nhieu tu motor tung kich nham EXTI
314 lam motor tu tat - loi da gap that o khoi Motor_Start_Stop. */
315#define BTN_MAU_MS 10u /* lay mau moi 10ms */
316/* 20 mau (200ms) chu khong phai 5 hay 10: da xac nhan thuc te rang voi 10
317 mau (100ms) nhieu tu motor VAN kich duoc nham nut STOP - tuc no giu chan o
318 muc CAO lien tuc hon 100ms chu khong phai xung nhon. Nguoi bam nut binh
319 thuong giu 200-300ms nen nguong nay van bat duoc moi lan bam that.
320 ⚠ Day la gioi han cua loc phan mem. Neu 200ms van bi kich nham thi BAT
321 BUOC phai sua phan cung (tro keo xuong ngoai + tu 104). */
322#define BTN_SO_MAU 20u
323#define BTN_KHOA_MS 1000u /* khoa 1s giua 2 lenh - bao ve tiep diem */
324 
325/* CUA SO CAM LUC MOI CAP NGUON. Module nut 3 chan co nguon rieng, luc moi co
326 dien ngo ra vong len muc CAO vai chuc ms truoc khi on dinh - bo loc mau
327 khong du de loai nen firmware nhan nham la co nguoi bam START va bang tai
328 chay ngay khi cap nguon. Hai lop chan:
329 1) trong BTN_KHOI_DONG_MS dau tien: khong nhan lenh nao;
330 2) sau do van phai THAY NUT O MUC THAP it nhat mot lan roi moi cong nhan
331 lan bam dau tien, nen nut dang bi giu / dang chap khong tu chay duoc. */
332#define BTN_KHOI_DONG_MS 1500u
333 
334/* Encoder JGB37-545: hop so 1:44, encoder 11 xung/vong truc dong co,
335 TIM4 dem 4 canh (TI12) => 11 x 4 x 44 = 1936 xung / 1 vong truc ra. */
336#define ENCODER_GEAR 44u
337#define ENCODER_PPR 11u
338#define ENCODER_X4 4u
339#define XUNG_MOI_VONG (ENCODER_PPR * ENCODER_X4 * ENCODER_GEAR)
340#define CUA_SO_RPM_MS 100u /* cua so do toc do */
341 
342/* BANG LOG TONG HOP - gop encoder + cam bien mau + servo + so dem thanh MOT
343 dong duy nhat, in dinh ky moi DASH_CHU_KY_MS:
344 t(s) | encoder (xung/vong/rpm/mm-s) | cam bien mau | servo | dem | motor
345 Can no vi cho duy nhat in gia tri encoder ra la lenh 'e' go qua UART, ma
346 ban NoUART tat han duong nhan nen trinh bien dich xoa ca khoi switch(c).
347 ⚠ KHOI NAY CHI DOC VA IN => khong the gay hoi quy; nghi no gay loi thi dat
348 = 0, dich lai, loi con nghia la cho khac.
349 ⚠ KHONG bat cung luc voi CHE_DO_DO_MAU - hai luong in xen ke, cot le het. */
350#define CHE_DO_BANG_LOG 1u
351 
352/* Gian cach in mot dong bang log (ms).
353 500ms = 2 dong/giay: du nhanh de thay phoi troi qua tam nhin (~0.5-1s) va
354 thay so dem nhay, nhung nguoi xem video van kip doc tung dong. Mot phep do
355 mau ton ~154ms nen 500ms la ~3 phep do/dong - so lieu tuoi. */
356#define DASH_CHU_KY_MS 500u
357 
358/* Cu bao nhieu dong thi in lai dong tieu de cot. 20 dong x 500ms = 10 giay
359 moi lan - vua du de nguoi xem video tua toi doan nao cung thay ten cot. */
360#define DASH_SO_DONG_TD 20u
361 
362/* ⚠⚠ SO NAY PHAI DO THAT ROI SUA - hien dang la GIA DINH.
363 Duong kinh PULY KEO (con lan chu dan mat bang), tinh bang mm, do phan
364 TIEP XUC voi day bang tai. Sai so nay chi lam sai cot mm/s va mm; cot
365 xung / vong / rpm KHONG phu thuoc no nen van dung tuyet doi. */
366#define ENC_DUONG_KINH_MM 30u
367 
368/* Chu vi x10 (0.1mm). 3142 = pi x1000, chia 100 de ra don vi 0.1mm.
369 D=30mm -> 942 (= 94.2mm moi vong truc ra). */
370#define ENC_CHU_VI_X10 ((3142u * ENC_DUONG_KINH_MM) / 100u)
371 
372/* He so doi (xung / ms) -> rpm x10, LAM TRON thay vi cat cut:
373 rpm_x10 = xung * 600000 / (dt_ms * XUNG_MOI_VONG)
374 = xung * ENC_HS_RPMX10 / dt_ms
375 Voi 1936 xung/vong -> 310. Sai so 0.03%. */
376#define ENC_HS_RPMX10 ((600000u + (XUNG_MOI_VONG / 2u)) / XUNG_MOI_VONG)
377 
378/* Motor DANG CHAY (relay dong) ma bo dem KHONG NHUC MOT XUNG trong khoang
379 nay => bao dong. 1.5s: dai hon moi qua do khoi dong cua JGB37 (~200ms)
380 nhung van bat duoc su co gan nhu tuc thi. */
381#define ENC_KET_CANH_BAO_MS 1500u
382#define ENC_BAO_KET_LAP_MS 5000u /* gian cach in lai canh bao ket */
383 
384/* Chong ket co cau chap hanh: mot buoc CO CHUYEN DONG treo qua lau (servo
385 khong bao "den noi" vi ket co khi / mat nguon servo) thi danh ve home va
386 vu trang lai, khong de may trang thai chet cung. */
387#define TIMEOUT_GAT_MS 15000u
388 
389/* SO LAN THU KET NOI CAM BIEN MAU LUC KHOI DONG.
390 ⚠ KHONG duoc cho cam bien bang mot vong while khong co loi thoat: cam bien
391 chua bat tay duoc thi firmware ket vinh vien o do va khong bao gio vao
392 vong lap chinh, ma gat_task() nam SAU vong nay nen toan bo trinh tu phan
393 loai khong chay mot nhip nao (trieu chung: LED PC13 nhay rat nhanh lien
394 tuc, servo dung im, nut START chet).
395 Nay thu dung so lan nay roi VAN VAO vong lap chinh voi g_cam_bien_ok = 0;
396 gy33_quet() da xu ly san truong hop doc that bai nen cam bien cam lai luc
397 nao la he chay tiep luc do, khong can khoi dong lai board. */
398#define KHOI_DONG_SO_LAN_THU 3u
399 
400/* Gian cach TOI THIEU giua hai lan in canh bao "mat lien lac cam bien".
401 3 giay du de nguoi van hanh biet he dang loi ma khong lam ngap terminal. */
402#define MAT_LIEN_LAC_IN_MS 3000u
403/* ========================================================================== */
404 
405/* ==================== HANG DOI THONG BAO (event queue) ====================
406 Mot phep do mau ton tron mot chu ky tich phan (154ms), va GY-33 quet lien
407 tuc, nen he gan nhu luc nao cung dang cho I2C => nut nhan va IR BAT BUOC
408 phai duoc quet ben trong vong cho do, neu khong bo loc 20 mau x 10ms cua
409 nut se khong bao gio dem du.
410 NHUNG ir_quet()/nut_quet() khong duoc IN ra UART: HAL_UART_Transmit chan
411 toi 200ms, goi trong vong cho se lam HET HAN AVALID va phep do that bai.
412 CACH GIAI: hai ham do chi DAT CO su kien; vong lap chinh xa hang doi va
413 in ra khi rong. */
414#define SK_IR_VAT_DEN 0x01u
415#define SK_IR_VAT_DI 0x02u
416#define SK_NUT_START 0x04u
417#define SK_NUT_STOP 0x08u
418 
419/* Cac buoc cua CO CAU GAT (khoi B). Dau vao DUY NHAT: su kien xanh duong. */
420typedef enum
421{
422 G_SAN_SANG = 0, /* da vu trang - cho GY-33 xac nhan XANH DUONG */
423 G_NANG, /* servo truot HOME 90 -> goc cho (XANH: 180) */
424 G_CHO_TROI, /* servo GIU tai goc cho, dem g_tre_ms (phoi troi) */
425 G_DANG_GAT, /* servo dang truot goc cho -> goc gat (0 do) */
426 G_GIU, /* dung yen tai 0 do, dem g_giu_ms */
427 G_VE_HOME, /* servo dang truot 0 -> 90 do (ve home) */
428 G_KHOA /* cho phoi roi khoi tam nhin + het NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS */
429} Gat_t;

448TCS_ChanDoan_t g_cd; /* ket qua kiem tra ket noi gan nhat */
449uint32_t g_so_lan_doc; /* dem so chu ky da doc */

451uint8_t g_dang_quet; /* 1 = dang in lien tuc mau doc duoc */
452uint8_t g_ir_vat; /* 1 = IR dang thay vat (da loc 30ms) */
453uint32_t g_ir_so_lan_vat; /* dem so lan vat DI QUA cam bien */
454/* Module GY-33 cua rig nay KHONG co chan LED (den sang thuong truc) nen
455 khong con bien/lenh dieu khien den. */
456 
457/* --- KHOI B: co cau gat --- */
458Gat_t g_gat_buoc; /* buoc hien tai cua co cau gat */
459/* 1 = khoi gat lam viec. CHI cac lenh HIEU CHINH ('b'/'c'/'d'/'w') moi ha
460 co nay xuong 0 trong luc do, va TU dua lai 1 khi do xong. Khong con lenh
461 UART nao tam dung khoi gat vo thoi han nua. */
462uint8_t g_tu_dong = 1u;
463uint8_t g_xanh_lien_tiep; /* so lan doc XANH "CHAC" lien tiep */
464uint8_t g_do_lien_tiep; /* so lan doc MAU KHAC lien tiep */
465uint32_t g_do_moc; /* tick luc BAT DAU chuoi MAU KHAC hien
466 tai - de do do DAI THOI GIAN cua chuoi,
467 xem TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS */
468/* --- Tham so cua chu trinh servo DANG chay. Dat mot lan luc kich, roi ba
469 trang thai giua (CHO_TROI / DANG_GAT / GIU) dung chung. Nho vay phoi xanh
470 va phoi do di chung mot may trang thai, khong phai nhan doi. */
471uint8_t g_goc_cho = GOC_CHO_XANH_DO; /* goc DUNG CHO phoi troi
472 (XANH=180, DO=home) */
473uint8_t g_buoc_nang = BUOC_XANH_NANG; /* toc do chang HOME->goc cho */
474uint8_t g_goc_dich = GOC_GAT_DO; /* goc servo se truot toi */
475uint32_t g_tre_ms = TRE_GAT_XANH_MS; /* cho truoc khi truot */
476uint32_t g_giu_ms = GIU_XANH_MS; /* giu tai goc dich bao lau */
477uint8_t g_buoc_di = BUOC_XANH_DI; /* toc do chang DI ra goc gat */
478uint8_t g_buoc_ve = BUOC_XANH_VE; /* toc do chang VE home */
479const char *g_ten_ct = "XANH"; /* ten chu trinh - chi de in log */
480uint32_t g_dem_bao_do; /* so lan da chay chu trinh bao do */
481uint8_t g_trong_lien_tiep; /* so lan doc TRONG lien tiep */
482uint8_t g_da_roi_xanh; /* 1 = da thay bang tai TRONG on dinh */
483int16_t g_br_min = 32767; /* dai dao dong cua d_br - de chan doan */
484int16_t g_br_max = -32768;
485uint32_t g_dem_xanh_yeu; /* driver bao XANH nhung KHONG du bien */
486 
487/* HAI BO DEM CHAN DOAN: phoi co kip dat XAC_NHAN_XANH lan doc khong.
488 XAC_NHAN_XANH = 2 doi hoi phoi nam trong tam nhin ~350ms lien tuc (2 x
489 154ms tich phan + 2 x 20ms cua so phuc vu). Bang tai chay nhanh la phoi
490 troi qua truoc khi du so lan => BO SOT PHOI.
491 g_xanh_max : chuoi xanh DAI NHAT tung dat duoc. Chay ca buoi ma so nay
492 khong bao gio toi XAC_NHAN_XANH thi phai ha nguong xac nhan.
493 g_dem_hut : so lan chuoi xanh DUT GIUA CHUNG truoc khi du. Moi lan tang
494 la mot phoi xanh rat co the da bi bo sot. */
495uint8_t g_xanh_max;
496uint32_t g_dem_hut;
497uint32_t g_dem_gat; /* so lan da gat (= so phoi xanh duong) */
498uint32_t g_dem_khac; /* so lan ket luan MAU KHAC (cho di thang) */
499uint32_t g_dem_bo_qua; /* thay xanh nhung dang khoa -> khong gat */
500uint32_t g_moc_ve_home; /* thoi diem servo ve toi home lan gan nhat */
501/* g_khoa_nut da bi bo: nut vat ly khong duoc phep khoa bang lenh UART */
502uint8_t g_mat_lien_lac;/* 1 = dang mat lien lac I2C voi cam bien mau */
503uint32_t g_moc_in_mat; /* moc lan in canh bao mat lien lac gan nhat */
504 
505/* Hang doi thong bao: ir_quet/nut_quet dat co, vong lap chinh in ra.
506 volatile vi duoc ghi tu ben trong vong cho I2C (qua ham phuc vu). */
507volatile uint8_t g_su_kien;
508 
509/* --- motor bang tai + nut nhan + encoder --- */
510uint8_t g_motor_chay; /* 1 = relay dang dong, motor chay */
511uint32_t g_so_lan_start;/* dem so lan nhan START hop le */
512uint32_t g_so_lan_stop; /* dem so lan nhan STOP hop le */
513int32_t g_encoder_cnt; /* so xung encoder tich luy (co dau) */
514int32_t g_vong_x100; /* so vong truc ra x100 */
515int32_t g_rpm; /* toc do truc ra (vong/phut) */
516/* Bon bien cua khoi doc encoder. Tat ca deu la gia tri DUONG (da lay tri
517 tuyet doi) tru g_encoder_cnt: chieu quay cua bang tai co dinh nen dau am
518 chi noi len thu tu hai day A/B dang nguoc, khong phai loi. */
519int32_t g_rpm_x10; /* toc do truc ra x10 (46.3 rpm -> 463) */
520int32_t g_mm_s; /* toc do dai mat bang tai (mm/giay) */
521int32_t g_quang_duong_mm;/* quang duong mat bang da chay (mm) */
522uint8_t g_enc_ket; /* 1 = relay dang dong ma encoder dung im */
523uint32_t g_enc_so_lan_ket;/* dem so lan roi vao trang thai tren */

537/* Private user code ---------------------------------------------------------*/
538/* USER CODE BEGIN 0 */
539 
540/* Gui 1 chuoi ra USART1 (PA9 = TX) -> FT232 -> terminal PC (115200 8N1) */
541static void uart_in(const char *s)
542{
543 /* HAN THOI GIAN PHAI THEO DO DAI CHUOI, khong duoc de co dinh.
544 HAL_UART_Transmit lay moc thoi gian MOT LAN o dau ham roi kiem tra tich
545 luy cho CA CHUOI. O 115200 thi 200ms chi phat duoc ~2300 ky tu, ma mot
546 chuoi 1882 ky tu da ton ~163ms - chi can vai ngat chen vao la ham TRA VE
547 GIUA CHUNG va cat cut chuoi (banner mat mot mang, hai chuoi dinh nhau).
548 Han = 50ms + 1ms moi 8 ky tu, du gap ~1.4 lan toc do that.
549 ⚠ Van co han (khong dung HAL_MAX_DELAY) de loi phan cung UART khong lam
550 treo vinh vien vong lap chinh. */
551 uint16_t n = (uint16_t)strlen(s);
552 
553 HAL_UART_Transmit(&huart1, (uint8_t *)s, n, (uint32_t)(50u + (n / 8u)));
554}
555 
556/* QUET CAM BIEN IR E18-D80NK (PA0, active-low) voi loc 60ms.
557 ⚠⚠ HAM NAY CHI CAP NHAT SO LIEU HIEN THI. Ket qua (g_ir_vat,
558 g_ir_so_lan_vat) KHONG duoc phep dieu khien bat cu co cau chap hanh nao -
559 day la lien dong bat bien cua he, xem giai thich o dau file. Them tinh
560 nang moi thi TUYET DOI khong duoc doc g_ir_vat ben trong gat_task().
561 KHONG IN RA UART o day: ham duoc goi ca tu ben trong vong cho I2C, ma
562 HAL_UART_Transmit chan toi 200ms se lam hong phep do mau. Chi dat co,
563 vong lap chinh se in. */
564static void ir_quet(uint32_t now)
565{
566 static uint32_t tick_mau = 0;
567 static uint8_t dem = 0;
568 uint8_t muc_moi;
569 
570 if ((now - tick_mau) < IR_MAU_MS) { return; }
571 tick_mau = now;
572 
573 /* Open collector + pull-up: khong vat = CAO, co vat = keo xuong THAP */
574 muc_moi = (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(IR_E18_GPIO_Port, IR_E18_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_RESET) ? 1u : 0u;
575 
576 if (muc_moi == g_ir_vat) { dem = 0; return; } /* khong doi -> thoi */
577 if (++dem < IR_SO_MAU) { return; } /* chua du 6 mau -> cho */
578 dem = 0;
579 g_ir_vat = muc_moi;
580 
581 if (g_ir_vat)
582 {
583 g_ir_so_lan_vat++;
584 g_su_kien |= SK_IR_VAT_DEN;
585 }
586 else
587 {
588 g_su_kien |= SK_IR_VAT_DI;
589 }
590}
591 
592/* ===================== MOTOR BANG TAI + NUT NHAN ========================= */
593 
594/* QUET 2 NUT START/STOP bang POLLING co loc mau.
595 Quy tac: START chi BAT, STOP chi TAT; nhan ca hai => UU TIEN STOP.
596 ⚠ TAM DIEU KHIEN: hai nut nay CHI dong/cat RELAY MOTOR. Chung KHONG
597 dung/chay khoi gat - servo lam viec doc lap voi bang tai.
598 KHONG IN RA UART o day (goi tu trong vong cho I2C) - chi dat co. */
599static void nut_quet(uint32_t now)
600{
601 static uint32_t tick_mau = 0;
602 static uint32_t tick_khoa = 0;
603 static uint8_t dem_start = 0, dem_stop = 0;
604 /* Khoi tao = 1: PHAI thay nut o muc THAP mot lan roi moi cong nhan lan bam
605 dau tien. Nho vay ngo ra vong cao luc module nut moi co dien khong bi
606 hieu la co nguoi bam START. */
607 static uint8_t giu_start = 1, giu_stop = 1;
608 /* Chot "da qua cua so khoi dong" - xem giai thich ngay duoi. */
609 static uint8_t qua_khoi_dong = 0;
610 uint8_t muc_start, muc_stop;
611 
612 /* Cam tuyet doi trong cua so khoi dong - luc nay moi thu con dang on ap.
613 ⚠ Dung CHOT MOT CHIEU chu KHONG so sanh thang "now < BTN_KHOI_DONG_MS":
614 HAL_GetTick() la uint32_t, tran ve 0 sau ~49.7 ngay chay lien tuc, va
615 dung luc tran nut START/STOP se bi cam them 1.5 giay mot cach vo co.
616 Chot chi bat len MOT LAN roi khong bao gio xet lai. */
617 if (qua_khoi_dong == 0u)
618 {
619 if (now < BTN_KHOI_DONG_MS) { return; }
620 qua_khoi_dong = 1u;
621 }
622 
623 if ((now - tick_mau) < BTN_MAU_MS) { return; }
624 tick_mau = now;
625 
626 /* Module nut 3 chan, tro keo xuong => nhan = muc CAO */
627 muc_start = (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(BTN_START_GPIO_Port, BTN_START_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) ? 1u : 0u;
628 muc_stop = (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(BTN_STOP_GPIO_Port, BTN_STOP_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) ? 1u : 0u;
629 
630 /* Dem so mau CAO lien tiep; nha nut thi xoa co giu */
631 if (muc_start) { if (dem_start < BTN_SO_MAU) { dem_start++; } }
632 else { dem_start = 0; giu_start = 0; }
633 if (muc_stop) { if (dem_stop < BTN_SO_MAU) { dem_stop++; } }
634 else { dem_stop = 0; giu_stop = 0; }
635 
636 /* ⚠ KHONG co bat ky duong nao khoa duoc nut vat ly. Nut START/STOP la
637 dau vao dieu khien DUY NHAT cua bang tai va phai song vo dieu kien. */
638 
639 /* Con trong cua so khoa thi bo qua (lan dau sau boot khong bi chan) */
640 if ((tick_khoa != 0u) && ((now - tick_khoa) < BTN_KHOA_MS)) { return; }
641 
642 /* --- STOP xet TRUOC START: nhan ca hai thi dung, khong chay --- */
643 if ((dem_stop >= BTN_SO_MAU) && (giu_stop == 0u))
644 {
645 giu_stop = 1u;
646 if (g_motor_chay)
647 {
648 MOTOR_TAT();
649 g_motor_chay = 0u;
650 g_so_lan_stop++;
651 tick_khoa = now;
652 g_su_kien |= SK_NUT_STOP;
653 }
654 return; /* lenh trung trang thai: khong ton khoa */
655 }
656 
657 if ((dem_start >= BTN_SO_MAU) && (giu_start == 0u))
658 {
659 giu_start = 1u;
660 if (g_motor_chay == 0u)
661 {
662 MOTOR_BAT();
663 g_motor_chay = 1u;
664 g_so_lan_start++;
665 tick_khoa = now;
666 g_su_kien |= SK_NUT_START;
667 }
668 }
669}
670 
671/* Xa hang doi thong bao - CHI goi tu VONG LAP CHINH, khong bao gio goi tu
672 ben trong vong cho I2C (vi ham nay in ra UART, chan toi 200ms). */
673static void xa_su_kien(void)
674{
675 uint8_t sk = g_su_kien;
676 
677 if (sk == 0u) { return; }
678 g_su_kien = 0u; /* xoa truoc khi in - khong mat su kien moi */
679 
680 if (sk & SK_NUT_STOP)
681 {
682 uart_in("\r\n>> NUT STOP (PA2): bang tai DUNG\r\n"
683 " Neu ban KHONG he bam nut ma van thay dong nay => nhieu tu\r\n"
684 " motor dang kich nham PA2. Xem huong dan chong nhieu.\r\n");
685 }
686 if (sk & SK_NUT_START) { uart_in("\r\n>> NUT START: bang tai CHAY\r\n"); }
687 /* IR chi la thong tin tham khao - ghi ro de nguoi van hanh khong hieu
688 nham rang no dang dieu khien servo. */
689 if (sk & SK_IR_VAT_DEN) { uart_in(" [IR] vat den (chi dem, KHONG kich gat)\r\n"); }
690 if (sk & SK_IR_VAT_DI) { uart_in(" [IR] vat di (chi dem, KHONG kich gat)\r\n"); }
691}
692 
693/* Doc encoder TIM4: quang duong tich luy + toc do truc ra.
694 TIM4 dem 16-bit tu quay vong, nen phai cong don theo HIEU so voi lan truoc. */
695static void encoder_quet(uint32_t now)
696{
697 static uint32_t tick = 0;
698 static uint16_t cnt_truoc = 0;
699 static uint8_t lan_dau = 1;
700 uint16_t cnt_nay;
701 int16_t delta;
702 
703 cnt_nay = (uint16_t)__HAL_TIM_GET_COUNTER(&htim4);
704 
705 if (lan_dau)
706 {
707 cnt_truoc = cnt_nay;
708 lan_dau = 0u;
709 tick = now;
710 return;
711 }
712 
713 /* Ep ve int16 => tu xu ly dung ca truong hop bo dem tran qua 0 */
714 delta = (int16_t)(cnt_nay - cnt_truoc);
715 cnt_truoc = cnt_nay;
716 g_encoder_cnt += delta;
717 
718 /* CHONG TRAN int32: chia trong ra truoc roi moi nhan phan du.
719 Neu lam thang (g_encoder_cnt * 100) / XUNG_MOI_VONG thi phep nhan tran
720 khi g_encoder_cnt > 21.4 trieu (~11000 vong truc ra = ~4 gio chay lien
721 tuc o 46 rpm), so vong nhay sang am va ca mm/s lan quang duong thanh
722 rac. Cach nay khong bao gio tran, ket qua y het. */
723 {
724 int32_t vong_tron = g_encoder_cnt / (int32_t)XUNG_MOI_VONG;
725 int32_t phan_du = g_encoder_cnt % (int32_t)XUNG_MOI_VONG;
726 int32_t v;
727 
728 g_vong_x100 = (vong_tron * 100) + ((phan_du * 100) / (int32_t)XUNG_MOI_VONG);
729 
730 v = g_vong_x100;
731 if (v < 0) { v = -v; }
732 g_quang_duong_mm = (v * (int32_t)ENC_CHU_VI_X10) / 1000;
733 }
734 
735 if ((now - tick) >= CUA_SO_RPM_MS)
736 {
737 static int32_t cnt_moc = 0;
738 int32_t xung = g_encoder_cnt - cnt_moc;
739 uint32_t dt = now - tick;
740 
741 cnt_moc = g_encoder_cnt;
742 tick = now;
743 
744 /* CHIA THEO THOI GIAN THUC, KHONG THEO DANH NGHIA.
745 encoder_quet() duoc goi tu vong lap chinh VA tu phuc_vu_trong_khi_do();
746 mot phep do mau ton 154ms nen khoang cach thuc te giua hai lan vao day
747 la 100-180ms. Dung hang so 60000/CUA_SO_RPM_MS (= dinh ninh cua so
748 dung 100ms) thi gom xung cua 180ms ma van chia cho 100 => RPM BAO CAO
749 HON THUC. Lay dt that thi so dung o moi nhip vong lap. */
750 if (xung < 0) { xung = -xung; }
751 if (dt == 0u) { dt = 1u; }
752 
753 g_rpm_x10 = (xung * (int32_t)ENC_HS_RPMX10) / (int32_t)dt;
754 g_rpm = g_rpm_x10 / 10; /* giu bien cu cho cac cho dang dung */
755 
756 /* mm/s = (rpm_x10 / 10) x (chu_vi_x10 / 10) / 60 = tich / 6000 */
757 g_mm_s = (g_rpm_x10 * (int32_t)ENC_CHU_VI_X10) / 6000;
758 }
759}
760 
761#if (CHE_DO_BANG_LOG != 0u)
762/* ==========================================================================
763 BANG LOG TONG HOP - mot dong = mot khoanh khac cua CA he thong, chi mot
764 nguon in duy nhat nen cot khong bao gio le.
765 BA VIEC: (1) in tieu de cot moi DASH_SO_DONG_TD dong; (2) in mot dong so
766 lieu moi DASH_CHU_KY_MS; (3) giam sat - relay dang dong ma bo dem khong
767 nhuc mot xung qua ENC_KET_CANH_BAO_MS thi canh bao.
768 ⚠ Goi tu VONG LAP CHINH, TUYET DOI khong goi tu phuc_vu_trong_khi_do():
769 uart_in() chan toi 200ms, du de lam HET HAN co AVALID cua cam bien.
770 ⚠ Viec (3) CHI CANH BAO, KHONG cat relay - bang tai dung dot ngot giua chu
771 trinh gat co the ket phoi vao canh gat.
772 ⚠ CA KHOI CHI DOC BIEN => khong the gay hoi quy. */
773 
774/* Ten mau NGAN (TCS_TenMau() tra ve chuoi 30 ky tu, qua dai cho mot cot). */
775static const char *ten_mau_ngan(TCS_Mau_t m)
776{
777 switch (m)
778 {
779 case TCS_XANH_DUONG: return "XANH ";
780 case TCS_MAU_KHAC: return "KHAC ";
781 case TCS_KHONG_RO: return "BAOHOA";
782 default: return "TRONG ";
783 }
784}
785 
786/* Ten buoc gat NGAN - ten_buoc() day du dai toi 35 ky tu. */
787static const char *ten_buoc_ngan(Gat_t b)
788{
789 switch (b)
790 {
791 case G_SAN_SANG: return "SANSANG";
792 case G_NANG: return "NANG ";
793 case G_CHO_TROI: return "CHOTROI";
794 case G_DANG_GAT: return "DANGGAT";
795 case G_GIU: return "GIU ";
796 case G_VE_HOME: return "VEHOME ";
797 default: return "KHOA ";
798 }
799}
800 
801/* Goc servo DANG PHAT ra (khong phai goc dich): suy nguoc tu do rong xung
802 trong thanh ghi CCR. Ban nay khong co co SERVO_DAO_CHIEU nen anh xa
803 thang: 1000us = 0 do ... 2000us = 180 do. */
804static uint8_t servo_goc_hien_tai(void)
805{
806 uint16_t us = Servo_XungHienTai();
807 
808 if (us <= SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US) { return 0u; }
809 if (us >= SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US) { return 180u; }
810 
811 return (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)(us - SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US) * 180u) /
812 (uint32_t)(SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US - SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US));
813}
814 
815/* ==== Dong tieu de cot ====================================================
816 ⚠ DO RONG TUNG O PHAI KHOP TUYET DOI voi format cua bang_log_in(), neu
817 khong bang se le cot. Khoa so do o day de khoi phai dem lai bang tay:
818 o1 t(s) 9 | o2 encoder 28 | o3 mau 36 | o4 servo 13 | o5 dem 9 | o6 motor
819 ⚠ ten_mau_ngan() phai luon dai DUNG 6 ky tu, ten_buoc_ngan() DUNG 7.
820 Chia lam ba lan goi uart_in(): mot chuoi qua dai co the bi cat giua chung. */
821static void dash_tieu_de(void)
822{
823 char b[200];
824 
825 uart_in("\r\n"
826 " t(s) | xung vong rpm mm/s "
827 "| C r g b b-r b-g mau "
828 "| goc buoc | DO/XANH | MOTOR\r\n");
829 uart_in("---------+----------------------------"
830 "+------------------------------------"
831 "+-------------+---------+------\r\n");
832 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
833 " (r,g,b = ti le chuan hoa, r+g+b=255. GAT khi b-r >= %+d, %u lan lien tiep.)\r\n",
834 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN), (unsigned)XAC_NHAN_XANH);
835 uart_in(b);
836}
837 
838static void bang_log_in(uint32_t now)
839{
840 static uint8_t lan_dau = 1u;
841 static uint32_t tick_in = 0u; /* moc in dinh ky */
842 static uint32_t tick_ket = 0u; /* moc lan cuoi CON THAY xung */
843 static uint32_t tick_bao = 0u; /* moc in lai canh bao ket */
844 static int32_t cnt_ket = 0; /* so xung tai tick_ket */
845 static uint8_t dem_dong = 0u; /* dem de in lai tieu de cot */
846 char b[200];
847 char s_vong[16]; /* so vong da dung san co dau */
848 int32_t v;
849 const char *dau;
850 
851 if (lan_dau)
852 {
853 lan_dau = 0u;
854 tick_in = now;
855 tick_ket = now;
856 tick_bao = now;
857 cnt_ket = g_encoder_cnt;
858 dem_dong = 0u;
859 dash_tieu_de();
860 }
861 
862 /* ---------- 1) GIAM SAT KET ---------- */
863 if (g_encoder_cnt != cnt_ket)
864 {
865 cnt_ket = g_encoder_cnt; /* con xung -> lam tuoi moc */
866 tick_ket = now;
867 if (g_enc_ket != 0u)
868 {
869 g_enc_ket = 0u;
870 uart_in(" [ENC] Da co xung tro lai - truc dong co dang quay.\r\n");
871 }
872 }
873 else if (g_motor_chay == 0u)
874 {
875 tick_ket = now; /* motor dung thi dung im la DUNG */
876 }
877 else if ((g_enc_ket == 0u) && ((now - tick_ket) >= ENC_KET_CANH_BAO_MS))
878 {
879 g_enc_ket = 1u;
880 if (g_enc_so_lan_ket < 0xFFFFFFFFu) { g_enc_so_lan_ket++; }
881 tick_bao = now - ENC_BAO_KET_LAP_MS; /* ep in ngay lan dau */
882 }
883 else
884 {
885 /* dang ket va chua toi han in lai - khong lam gi */
886 }
887 
888 if ((g_enc_ket != 0u) && ((now - tick_bao) >= ENC_BAO_KET_LAP_MS))
889 {
890 tick_bao = now;
891 uart_in("\r\n[!] ENCODER KHONG DEM trong khi RELAY DANG DONG.\r\n"
892 " Ba nguyen nhan, kiem tra theo dung thu tu nay:\r\n"
893 " 1) Bang tai/truc bi KET hoac tuot day dai - motor khong quay\r\n"
894 " that. Nghe tieng motor: co u u ma khong quay = ket co khi.\r\n");
895 uart_in(" 2) Encoder chua co dien hoac chua dau tin hieu: day encoder\r\n"
896 " cua JGB37 can rieng +5V va GND, hai day A/B vao PB6/PB7.\r\n"
897 " Dau hieu: xung dung dung 0 tu luc cap nguon toi gio.\r\n"
898 " 3) Relay dong nhung 24V khong len - do dien ap 2 dau motor.\r\n");
899 }
900 
901 /* ---------- 2) IN TIEU DE CO CHU KY ---------- */
902 if ((now - tick_in) < DASH_CHU_KY_MS) { return; }
903 tick_in = now;
904 
905 if (dem_dong >= (uint8_t)DASH_SO_DONG_TD)
906 {
907 dem_dong = 0u;
908 dash_tieu_de(); /* nhac lai ten cot + nguong gat dang dung */
909 }
910 dem_dong++;
911 
912 /* ---------- 3) MOT DONG SO LIEU ---------- */
913 /* ⚠ SO VONG PHAI DUNG SAN THANH CHUOI TRUOC, khong nhet thang vao format
914 chinh. Hai ly do:
915 1) "%ld.%02ld" voi so am cho ra "-1.-50" (dau am in ra HAI lan).
916 2) Neu de "%s%3ld.%02ld" thi o nay rong 6 hay 7 ky tu TUY dau am co hay
917 khong => ca phan con lai cua dong bi day lech mot cot. Dung %7s voi
918 chuoi da co dau thi be rong CO DINH. */
919 v = g_vong_x100;
920 dau = "";
921 if (v < 0) { v = -v; dau = "-"; }
922 snprintf(s_vong, sizeof(s_vong), "%s%ld.%02ld",
923 dau, (long)(v / 100), (long)(v % 100));
924 
925 /* ⚠ MOT LAN snprintf DUY NHAT cho ca dong. Neu chia lam nhieu lan
926 uart_in() thi giua hai lan goi co the bi mot dong SU KIEN (PHOI XANH,
927 nut bam) chen vao giua - dong so lieu se bi be doi. */
928 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
929 " %4lu.%lu |%8ld %7s %3ld.%ld %4ld "
930 "| %4u %3u %3u %3u %+5d %+4d %s "
931 "| %3u %s | %2lu / %2lu | %s%s\r\n",
932 /* --- thoi gian tu luc cap nguon --- */
933 (unsigned long)(now / 1000u), (unsigned long)((now % 1000u) / 100u),
934 /* --- encoder / bang tai --- */
935 (long)g_encoder_cnt,
936 s_vong,
937 (long)(g_rpm_x10 / 10), (long)(g_rpm_x10 % 10),
938 (long)g_mm_s,
939 /* --- cam bien mau --- */
940 (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c, (unsigned)g_kq.r, (unsigned)g_kq.g,
941 (unsigned)g_kq.b, (int)g_kq.d_br, (int)g_kq.d_bg,
942 ten_mau_ngan(g_kq.mau),
943 /* --- co cau gat --- */
944 (unsigned)servo_goc_hien_tai(), ten_buoc_ngan(g_gat_buoc),
945 /* --- hai bo dem san pham (dung so DAY DU, khong % 10 nhu LED) --- */
946 (unsigned long)g_dem_bao_do, (unsigned long)g_dem_gat,
947 /* --- bang tai --- */
948 (g_motor_chay != 0u) ? "CHAY" : "DUNG",
949 (g_cam_bien_ok != 0u) ? "" : " [MAT I2C]");
950 uart_in(b);
951}
952#endif /* CHE_DO_BANG_LOG */
953 
954/* BAN NoUART: ham nay LUON tra ve 0. Duong nhan da bi tat han o USER CODE
955 BEGIN 2 (UART_MODE_TX + PA10 pull-up) nen phan cung khong nhan duoc byte
956 nao. Tra ve 0 ngay tu day de moi khoi `switch (c)` ben duoi KHONG BAO GIO
957 chay - ke ca hai vong `while (uart_doc_lenh() == 0u)` trong lenh 'c'/'d',
958 hai vong do neu vao duoc se treo VINH VIEN.
959 Than ham goc giu trong khoi `#if 0` ngay duoi de doi chieu khi can dung
960 lai ban co UART (`#if 0` chu khong de thanh ham tinh khong ai goi - AC5 se
961 bao warning #177-D, ma quy uoc du an la 0 Error 0 Warning). */
962static uint8_t uart_doc_lenh(void)
963{
964 return 0u;
965}
966 
967#if 0 /* ===== BAN GOC, chi de tham khao - xem khoi comment o tren ===== */
968static uint8_t uart_doc_lenh_ban_cu(void)
969{
970 if (__HAL_UART_GET_FLAG(&huart1, UART_FLAG_ORE)) /* xoa loi tran neu co */
971 {
972 __HAL_UART_CLEAR_OREFLAG(&huart1);
973 }
974 if (__HAL_UART_GET_FLAG(&huart1, UART_FLAG_RXNE))
975 {
976 return (uint8_t)(huart1.Instance->DR & 0xFFu);
977 }
978 return 0u;
979}

982/* In 1 dong ket qua kiem tra: [so] ten ... OK / LOI + goi y sua */
983static void in_dong_kt(int stt, const char *ten, uint8_t ok, const char *chi_tiet)
984{
985 char b[180];
986 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "[%d] %-26s : %s %s\r\n", stt, ten, ok ? "OK " : "LOI", chi_tiet);
987 uart_in(b);
988}

990/* Quet bus I2C va in danh sach dia chi tim duoc.
991 ⚠ CONG CU QUAN TRONG NHAT khi cam bien khong noi chuyen duoc: phan biet
992 duoc "dut day" (khong co dia chi nao) voi "cam nham cap chan GY-33"
993 (thay 0x5A cua MCU phu thay vi 0x29 cua chip TCS34725). */
994static void quet_bus_va_in(void)
995{
996 char b[140];
997 uint8_t i;
998 uint8_t thay_29 = 0u, thay_5a = 0u;
999 
1000 uart_in("\r\n--- QUET BUS I2C2 (PB10=SCL, PB11=SDA) ---\r\n");
1001 TCS_QuetBus(&g_cd);
1002 
1003 if (g_cd.so_tb == 0u)
1004 {
1005 uart_in(" KHONG co thiet bi nao tra loi.\r\n"
1006 " -> Kiem tra: GY-33 co dien 3.3V chua, GND CHUNG voi STM32,\r\n"
1007 " chan SCL cua GY-33 vao PB10, SDA vao PB11 (rat de cam\r\n"
1008 " nguoc 2 day nay), va tro keo len 4.7k tu SCL/SDA len 3.3V\r\n"
1009 " neu module KHONG co san tro.\r\n");
1010 return;
1011 }
1012 
1013 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Tim thay %u thiet bi:\r\n", (unsigned)g_cd.so_tb);
1014 uart_in(b);
1015 for (i = 0u; (i < g_cd.so_tb) && (i < 8u); i++)
1016 {
1017 const char *ghi_chu = "";
1018 if (g_cd.dia_chi[i] == TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT)
1019 {
1020 ghi_chu = "<== chip TCS34725 - DUNG CAI NAY";
1021 thay_29 = 1u;
1022 }
1023 if (g_cd.dia_chi[i] == TCS_DIA_CHI_GY33)
1024 {
1025 ghi_chu = "<== MCU phu cua GY-33 (cap chan CT/DR)";
1026 thay_5a = 1u;
1027 }
1028 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " 0x%02X %s\r\n", (unsigned)g_cd.dia_chi[i], ghi_chu);
1029 uart_in(b);
1030 }
1031 
1032 /* GY-33 co HAI duong I2C: SCL/SDA di thang vao chip 0x29, con CT/DR di vao
1033 MCU phu 0x5A. Chi ket luan duoc khi biet dang thay dia chi nao. */
1034 if ((thay_29 == 0u) && thay_5a)
1035 {
1036 uart_in(" [!] CHI thay MCU phu (0x5A), KHONG thay chip TCS34725 (0x29).\r\n"
1037 " Dang cam nham vao cap chan CT/DR cua GY-33.\r\n"
1038 " => Chuyen 2 day sang cap chan ghi SCL va SDA tren module.\r\n");
1039 }
1040 else if (thay_29 && thay_5a)
1041 {
1042 uart_in(" [!] Thay CA HAI dia chi => MCU phu cua GY-33 dang cung nam\r\n"
1043 " tren bus nay. Firmware van chay duoc, nhung neu bo dem\r\n"
1044 " 'cuu bus' (go 't') tang deu thi chinh no dang tranh bus:\r\n"
1045 " luc do phai cat/nhac chan MCU phu ra khoi bus.\r\n");
1046 }
1047}
1048 
1049/* Chay tu kiem tra ket noi va in bao cao ra terminal */
1050static uint8_t kiem_tra_ket_noi(void)
1051{
1052 char b[190];
1053 
1054 uart_in("\r\n--- KIEM TRA KET NOI TCS34725 ---\r\n");
1055 TCS_KiemTraKetNoi(&g_cd);
1056 
1057 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "So thiet bi tren bus=%u | ID=0x%02X | C=%u R=%u G=%u B=%u (tran %u)\r\n",
1058 (unsigned)g_cd.so_tb, (unsigned)g_cd.id, (unsigned)g_cd.c,
1059 (unsigned)g_cd.r, (unsigned)g_cd.g, (unsigned)g_cd.b,
1060 (unsigned)g_cd.tran);
1061 uart_in(b);
1062 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "Gain=%s | Tich phan=%ums (ATIME=0x%02X)\r\n",
1063 TCS_TenGain(TCS_LayGain()), (unsigned)TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms(),
1064 (unsigned)TCS_LayTichPhan());
1065 uart_in(b);
1066 
1067 in_dong_kt(1, "Bus I2C2 - dia chi 0x29", g_cd.ok_bus,
1068 g_cd.ok_bus ? "chip TCS34725 tra ACK"
1069 : "-> go 'p' quet bus (co the dang cam nham CT/DR)");
1070 in_dong_kt(2, "Thanh ghi ID (0x44/0x4D)", g_cd.ok_id,
1071 g_cd.ok_id ? "dung chip TCS34725"
1072 : "-> co thiet bi o 0x29 nhung khong phai TCS34725");
1073 in_dong_kt(3, "Doc duoc so lieu moi", g_cd.ok_doc,
1074 g_cd.ok_doc ? "AVALID len, doc du 4 kenh"
1075 : "-> chip khong hoan tat chu ky do (kiem tra nguon 3.3V)");
1076 in_dong_kt(4, "Muc sang", g_cd.ok_sang,
1077 g_cd.ok_sang ? "du sang"
1078 : "-> qua toi: bat den module (lenh 'l'), tang gain ('a')");
1079 in_dong_kt(5, "Khong bao hoa", g_cd.ok_khong_bh,
1080 g_cd.ok_khong_bh ? "con cho de phoi sang hon"
1081 : "-> qua sang: ha gain ('a') hoac ha tich phan ('n')");
1082 in_dong_kt(6, "Tach duoc 3 kenh", g_cd.ok_tach_mau,
1083 g_cd.ok_tach_mau ? "R,G,B khac nhau"
1084 : "-> 3 kenh dinh nhau: cam bien bi che kin / du lieu rac");
1085 
1086 if (g_cd.du_dieu_kien)
1087 {
1088 if (g_cd.ok_sang && g_cd.ok_khong_bh && g_cd.ok_tach_mau)
1089 {
1090 uart_in("=> KET NOI TOT. San sang quet mau.\r\n");
1091 }
1092 else
1093 {
1094 uart_in("=> Bus + chip OK, muc 4-5-6 chi la CANH BAO (phu thuoc vat dang\r\n"
1095 " dat truoc cam bien). Van quet duoc; go 'b' de do lai nen.\r\n");
1096 }
1097 }
1098 else
1099 {
1100 uart_in("=> CHUA DAT. Sua day theo goi y o tren roi go 'k' de kiem tra lai.\r\n");
1101 quet_bus_va_in();
1102 }
1103 return g_cd.du_dieu_kien;
1104}
1105 
1106static void in_tro_giup(void)
1107{
1108 uart_in("\r\n=== UART CHI DE XEM SO LIEU VA HIEU CHINH ===\r\n"
1109 " Moi DIEU KHIEN deu do STM32 quyet dinh:\r\n"
1110 " - BANG TAI : chi nut START/STOP vat ly (PA1/PA2)\r\n"
1111 " - SERVO GAT: chi cam bien mau TCS34725 duoc phep kich\r\n"
1112 " KHONG con lenh UART nao bat duoc motor hay kich duoc servo.\r\n"
1113 "\r\nLENH (go trong Hercules, khong can Enter):\r\n"
1114 " k = kiem tra lai ket noi\r\n"
1115 " p = QUET BUS I2C - in ra moi dia chi tim duoc\r\n"
1116 " q = bat/tat quet mau lien tuc\r\n"
1117 " r = doc 1 lan roi in ket qua\r\n"
1118 " b = HOC NEN (bo het phoi ra roi go 'b') -> do van mau CUA NEN\r\n"
1119 " roi tu dat nguong xanh nam TREN no. LAM DAU TIEN!\r\n"
1120 " c = HOC NGUONG XANH tu chinh phoi xanh that (dat dung khoang\r\n"
1121 " cach lam viec roi go 'c')\r\n"
1122 " d = DO THU lien tuc - dua phoi tu xa lai gan, xem tu khoang cach\r\n"
1123 " nao thi doi ket luan\r\n"
1124 " a = doi GAIN 1x -> 4x -> 16x -> 60x -> 1x (nen giu 60x)\r\n"
1125 " n = doi THOI GIAN TICH PHAN 2.4 / 24 / 50 / 103 / 154 ms\r\n"
1126 " y = bat/tat NEN TU BAM (tu bu troi anh sang / xe dich sensor)\r\n"
1127 " 0 = DAT LAI MAC DINH tat ca nguong (nut thoat hiem)\r\n"
1128 " w = DO DAI NHIEU CUA NEN (bo phoi ra roi go 'w') - CHAN DOAN\r\n"
1129 " QUAN TRONG NHAT khi servo gat oan luc chua co phoi\r\n"
1130 " x = TU CUU BUS I2C bang tay + xoa bo dem loi\r\n"
1131 " i = xem trang thai IR E18-D80NK hien tai\r\n"
1132 " j = DO MUC NHIEU tren PA0/PA1/PA2 (AN NUT START vat ly trong\r\n"
1133 " luc do de xem chan nao bi nhieu) - CHAN DOAN QUAN TRONG\r\n"
1134 " t = xem trang thai khoi gat + bo dem\r\n"
1135 " e = xem encoder: so xung, so vong, toc do rpm\r\n"
1136 " h = hien lai bang lenh nay\r\n");
1137}
1138 
1139/* HAM PHUC VU - driver cam bien goi lien tuc trong suot 154ms cho do xong.
1140 Khong co no thi ca he thong dung hinh moi lan do mau: servo dung giua hanh
1141 trinh (GIAT rat ro), nut nhan bi bo sot, IR khong duoc quet.
1142 PHAI ngan va TUYET DOI khong duoc goi lai ham TCS_* nao (de quy). */
1143static void phuc_vu_trong_khi_do(void)
1144{
1145 /* PHAI QUET CA NUT VA IR O DAY, khong chi truot servo + doc encoder.
1146 GY-33 quet LIEN TUC nen he gan nhu luc nao cung dang nam trong vong cho
1147 AVALID nay. Khong quet nut o day thi bo loc 20 mau x 10ms cua nut se mat
1148 3-4 giay moi dem du => bam START/STOP nhu khong an.
1149 AN TOAN: ca bon ham duoi day deu KHONG in UART va KHONG goi TCS_* nao
1150 (ir_quet/nut_quet chi dat co su kien, vong lap chinh moi in). */
1151 uint32_t t = HAL_GetTick();
1152 
1153 Servo_Task();
1154 encoder_quet(t);
1155 nut_quet(t); /* START/STOP phai an lien tay ke ca khi dang do mau */
1156 ir_quet(t); /* chi cap nhat bo dem hien thi */
1157}
1158 
1159/* In dong tieu de cot cho che do quet lien tuc, kem BON CON SO QUYET DINH
1160 cua khoi gat. Can phan soi khoi gat vi chuoi mau co the DA DU DIEU KIEN
1161 GAT ma servo van dung im, luc do bang do chi in so lieu CAM BIEN nen khong
1162 phan biet duoc (a) g_tu_dong == 0 hay (b) may trang thai ket o G_KHOA.
1163 ⚠ ten_buoc() dinh nghia mai tan duoi nen phai khai bao truoc o day, neu
1164 khong AC5 bao loi #20 "identifier undefined". */
1165static const char *ten_buoc(Gat_t b);
1166 
1167static void in_soi_khoi_gat(void)
1168{
1169 char b[190];
1170 uint32_t nay = HAL_GetTick();
1171 uint32_t da_nghi = nay - g_moc_ve_home;
1172 
1173 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " [KHOI GAT: %s | khoi gat=%s]\r\n",
1174 ten_buoc(g_gat_buoc),
1175 (g_tu_dong != 0u) ? "DANG LAM VIEC" : "BI KHOA (g_tu_dong=0)");
1176 uart_in(b);
1177 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
1178 " [xanh lien tiep=%u/%u | da roi xanh=%s | nghi %lu/%lums]\r\n",
1179 (unsigned)g_xanh_lien_tiep, (unsigned)XAC_NHAN_XANH,
1180 g_da_roi_xanh ? "co" : "CHUA",
1181 (unsigned long)((da_nghi > 99999u) ? 99999u : da_nghi),
1182 (unsigned long)NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS);
1183 uart_in(b);
1184 
1185 /* Hai loi khuyen DUNG voi ban NoUART - khong go duoc lenh nao. */
1186 if (g_tu_dong == 0u)
1187 {
1188 uart_in(" [!!] KHOI GAT DANG BI KHOA -> dat CHE_DO_KHOA_SERVO = 0\r\n"
1189 " trong main.c roi NAP LAI. Day KHONG phai loi nguong mau.\r\n");
1190 }
1191 else if ((g_gat_buoc == G_KHOA) && (g_da_roi_xanh == 0u))
1192 {
1193 uart_in(" [!!] Dang KHOA va CHUA thay bang tai TRONG du 3 lan lien tiep\r\n"
1194 " -> chua vu trang duoc. Don sach phoi khoi tam nhin cam bien.\r\n");
1195 }
1196}

1198static void in_tieu_de_cot(void)
1199{
1200 int16_t nbr, nbg;
1201 char b[200];
1202 
1203 TCS_LayNguongXanh(&nbr, &nbg);
1204 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n [XANH khi (b-r)>=%+d va (b-g)>=%+d | NEN o b-r=%+d b-g=%+d]\r\n",
1205 nbr, nbg, TCS_LayDbrNen(), TCS_LayDbgNen());
1206 uart_in(b);
1207 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " [%s %ums x%u mau | bam nen: %s (%u lan)]\r\n",
1208 TCS_TenGain(TCS_LayGain()), (unsigned)TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms(),
1209 (unsigned)TCS_LaySoMauLoc(),
1210 TCS_LayBamNen() ? "BAT" : "tat",
1211 (unsigned)TCS_LaySoLanBamNen());
1212 uart_in(b);
1213 /* Hai so nay cho biet firmware dang lay moc nen o dau, va PHAI in DUNG
1214 chieu dang bien dich (xem TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN) - in sai chieu thi ca
1215 buoi do se doc nham dau la vat, dau la nen. */
1216#if (TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG == 0u)
1217 /* Dang nhan vat bang VAN MAU, khong phai do sang. Con so C ben duoi chi de
1218 THEO DOI, KHONG tham gia quyet dinh - phai noi ro, neu khong buoi do sau
1219 lai di chinh nham mot con so vo tac dung. */
1220 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " [TRONG khi b-r trong %+d..%+d VA b-g trong %+d..%+d <== TRUC QUYET DINH]\r\n",
1221 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() - TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR),
1222 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR),
1223 (int)(TCS_LayDbgNen() - TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG),
1224 (int)(TCS_LayDbgNen() + TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG));
1225 uart_in(b);
1226 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " [do sang C=%u chi de THEO DOI - khong tham gia quyet dinh]\r\n",
1227 (unsigned)TCS_LayCNen());
1228#elif (TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN != 0u)
1229 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " [CO VAT khi C < %u (nen dang bam C=%u, %u%% - vat TOI hon nen)]\r\n",
1230 (unsigned)TCS_LayNguongCoVat(), (unsigned)TCS_LayCNen(),
1231 (unsigned)TCS_PT_CO_VAT);
1232#else
1233 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " [CO VAT khi C > %u (nen dang bam C=%u, %u%% - vat SANG hon nen)]\r\n",
1234 (unsigned)TCS_LayNguongCoVat(), (unsigned)TCS_LayCNen(),
1235 (unsigned)TCS_PT_CO_VAT_SANG);
1236#endif
1237 uart_in(b);
1238 /* Bo dem "phai doc lai tung byte". Khac 0 = cum 8 byte dang tra ve so vo
1239 ly ve vat ly => duong I2C hong that, phai xu ly PHAN CUNG chu khong
1240 phai chinh nguong. */
1241 {
1242 uint32_t doc_lai = TCS_LaySoLanDocLai();
1243 
1244 if (doc_lai != 0u)
1245 {
1246 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
1247 " [!! I2C HONG: %lu lan cum 8 byte tra ve so vo ly, da phai doc lai tung byte]\r\n",
1248 (unsigned long)doc_lai);
1249 uart_in(b);
1250 }
1251 }
1252 
1253 in_soi_khoi_gat(); /* soi ben trong khoi gat - xem giai thich o tren */
1254 
1255 /* Ba cot SO DEM THO. Tieu de phai khop DUNG do rong cua in_dong_quet()
1256 (%5u | %5u %5u %5u | %3u %3u %3u | %+4d %+4d | ...). */
1257 uart_in(" C | r_tho g_tho b_tho | r g b | b-r b-g | IR | KET LUAN\r\n"
1258 " -----+--------------------+-------------+-----------+---------+----------------\r\n");
1259}
