1261/* Doc cam bien 1 lan va in 1 dong ket qua */
1262static void doc_va_in(void)
1263{
1264 char dong[190];
1265 
1266 g_cam_bien_ok = TCS_Doc(&g_kq);
1267 g_so_lan_doc++;
1268 
1269 if (g_cam_bien_ok == 0u)
1270 {
1271 uart_in("!! MAT LIEN LAC I2C - driver da tu cuu bus nhung van khong doc duoc.\r\n"
1272 " Go 'p' de quet bus, hoac 'x' de cuu bus lai bang tay.\r\n");
1273 return;
1274 }
1275 snprintf(dong, sizeof(dong),
1276 " %5u | %3u %3u %3u | %+4d %+4d | %-7s | %s%s\r\n",
1277 (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c,
1278 g_kq.r, g_kq.g, g_kq.b, g_kq.d_br, g_kq.d_bg,
1279 g_ir_vat ? "CO VAT" : "TRONG", TCS_TenMau(g_kq.mau),
1280 g_kq.bao_hoa ? " [BAO HOA]" : "");
1281 uart_in(dong);
1282}
1283 
1284/* In 1 dong tu ket qua DA CO SAN trong g_kq - KHONG doc lai cam bien.
1285 Dung o che do quet lien tuc ('q'): vong lap chinh da doc mot lan bang
1286 gy33_quet() roi, doc them lan nua chi ton them 154ms cho khong.
1287 Cot IR o day thuan tuy la thong tin tham khao, khong tham gia dieu khien. */
1288static void in_dong_quet(void)
1289{
1290 char dong[190];
1291 
1292 /* IN CA SO DEM THO r/g/b, khong chi so da chuan hoa.
1293 Ba cot tho la thu DUY NHAT cho biet cam bien that su thu duoc bao nhieu
1294 photon. Mot dong kieu "C=2 | 0 0 254 | +254 +254" neu khong co so tho
1295 thi phai giai nguoc he phuong trinh chuan hoa moi biet kenh B tho ~510
1296 trong khi C chi co 2, tu do moi ket luan duoc la BYTE HONG. */
1297 snprintf(dong, sizeof(dong),
1298 " %5u | %5u %5u %5u | %3u %3u %3u | %+4d %+4d | %-7s | %s%s\r\n",
1299 (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c,
1300 (unsigned)g_kq.raw_r, (unsigned)g_kq.raw_g, (unsigned)g_kq.raw_b,
1301 g_kq.r, g_kq.g, g_kq.b, g_kq.d_br, g_kq.d_bg,
1302 g_ir_vat ? "CO VAT" : "TRONG", TCS_TenMau(g_kq.mau),
1303 g_kq.bao_hoa ? " [BAO HOA]" : "");
1304 uart_in(dong);
1305}
1306 
1307#if (CHE_DO_DO_MAU != 0u)
1308/* IN SO LIEU O CHE DO DO MAU - co TIET CHE theo thoi gian.
1309 Khac in_dong_quet() o cho khong in moi vong lap (se ra ~190 dong/giay,
1310 khong doc noi va lam nghen UART) ma DO_MAU_CHU_KY_MS moi in mot dong, cu
1311 DO_MAU_SO_DONG_TD dong lai in tieu de cot. Dung lai g_kq da co san. */
1312static void do_mau_in(uint32_t now)
1313{
1314 static uint32_t tick = 0u;
1315 static uint8_t dem = 0u;
1316 static uint8_t lan_dau = 1u;
1317 
1318 if (lan_dau) /* in tieu de ngay dong dau tien */
1319 {
1320 lan_dau = 0u;
1321 dem = 0u;
1322 in_tieu_de_cot();
1323 }
1324 
1325 if ((now - tick) < DO_MAU_CHU_KY_MS) { return; }
1326 tick = now;
1327 
1328 if (dem >= (uint8_t)DO_MAU_SO_DONG_TD)
1329 {
1330 dem = 0u;
1331 in_tieu_de_cot(); /* nhac lai nguong + muc nen dang bam */
1332 }
1333 dem++;
1334 
1335 in_dong_quet();
1336}
1337#endif /* CHE_DO_DO_MAU */
1338 
1339/* HOC LAI NEN MOT LAN sau khi motor da chay - xem HOC_LAI_SO_LAN.
1340 Goi tu vong lap chinh, NGAY SAU gy33_quet() (can g_kq da moi).
1341 ⚠ KHONG duoc goi tu trong vong cho I2C: ham nay co the goi TCS_HocSanToi()
1342 von ton mot chu ky tich phan (154ms). */
1343static void hoc_lai_nen_khi_chay(void)
1344{
1345 static uint8_t da_hoc = 0u; /* mot lan duy nhat moi lan cap nguon */
1346 static uint8_t dem = 0u;
1347 static int16_t br_truoc = 0;
1348 
1349 if (da_hoc != 0u) { return; }
1350 if (g_motor_chay == 0u) { dem = 0u; return; } /* chi hoc khi dang chay */
1351 
1352 /* CHOT 1: chi dem lan doc da ket luan TRONG (xem giai thich o HOC_LAI_*) */
1353 if (g_kq.mau != TCS_TRONG)
1354 {
1355 dem = 0u;
1356 return;
1357 }
1358 
1359 /* CHOT 2: phai dung yen - lech qua 2 don vi la dem lai tu dau */
1360 if (dem > 0u)
1361 {
1362 int16_t lech = (int16_t)(g_kq.d_br - br_truoc);
1363 
1364 if (lech < 0) { lech = (int16_t)(-lech); }
1365 if (lech > (int16_t)TCS_NEN_BAM_DAO_DONG) { dem = 0u; }
1366 }
1367 br_truoc = g_kq.d_br;
1368 if (dem < 255u) { dem++; }
1369 
1370 if (dem >= (uint8_t)HOC_LAI_SO_LAN)
1371 {
1372 TCS_KetQua_t k;
1373 char b[190];
1374 int16_t br_cu = TCS_LayDbrNen();
1375 
1376 da_hoc = 1u; /* CHOT 3: chi mot lan */
1377 
1378 if (TCS_HocSanToi(&k) != 0u)
1379 {
1380 int16_t nbr, nbg;
1381 
1382 TCS_LayNguongXanh(&nbr, &nbg);
1383 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
1384 "\r\n>> HOC LAI NEN voi MOTOR DANG CHAY: b-r %+d -> %+d (b-g %+d)\r\n",
1385 br_cu, TCS_LayDbrNen(), TCS_LayDbgNen());
1386 uart_in(b);
1387 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
1388 " Nguong moi: XANH khi b-r >= %+d | GAT khi b-r >= %+d | TRONG %+d..%+d\r\n",
1389 nbr, (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN),
1390 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() - TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR),
1391 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR));
1392 uart_in(b);
1393 }
1394 else
1395 {
1396 uart_in("\r\n>> Hoc lai nen THAT BAI (loi I2C) - giu moc cu.\r\n");
1397 }
1398 }
1399}
1400 
1401/* ======================================================================== */
1402/* KHOI A - QUET GY-33 LIEN TUC (nguon kich DUY NHAT cua he) */
1403/* ======================================================================== */
1404 
1405/* Doc cam bien mau MOT chu ky va cap nhat cac co trang thai cua he. Goi LIEN
1406 TUC tu vong lap chinh, KHONG phu thuoc trang thai bang tai / IR / servo.
1407 Tra ve 1 = doc duoc, 0 = loi I2C. Hai co ma khoi B doc:
1408 g_xanh_lien_tiep : so lan doc XANH DUONG LIEN TIEP (ve 0 khi doc ra
1409 mau khac); >= XAC_NHAN_XANH la du chac
1410 g_da_roi_xanh : bat len khi doc ra KHAC xanh - dieu kien vu trang
1411 lai sau mot lan gat */
1412/* LOP 1 chong gat oan: ngoai viec driver ket luan XANH DUONG, con BAT BUOC
1413 d_br phai vuot NEN it nhat BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN don vi. Doc nen bang
1414 TCS_LayDbrNen() nen bien nay TU DONG bam theo moc nen. */
1415static uint8_t la_xanh_chac(const TCS_KetQua_t *k)
1416{
1417 int16_t muc_toi_thieu;
1418 
1419 if (k->mau != TCS_XANH_DUONG) { return 0u; }
1420 
1421 muc_toi_thieu = (int16_t)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN);
1422 return (uint8_t)((k->d_br >= muc_toi_thieu) ? 1u : 0u);
1423}
1424 
1425static uint8_t gy33_quet(void)
1426{
1427 TCS_KetQua_t k;
1428 
1429 if (TCS_Doc(&k) == 0u)
1430 {
1431 g_cam_bien_ok = 0u;
1432 return 0u; /* loi I2C: GIU NGUYEN cac bo dem lien
1433 tiep - mot lan mat lien lac khong duoc
1434 tinh la bang chung cua bat cu dieu gi */
1435 }
1436 
1437 g_kq = k;
1438 g_cam_bien_ok = 1u;
1439 g_so_lan_doc++;
1440 
1441 /* Dai dao dong cua d_br - so lieu chan doan cho lenh 't' va 'w'.
1442 Neu g_br_max cham toi nguong xanh trong luc bang tai TRONG thi chinh
1443 cai nen dang tu kich - phai tang BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN. */
1444 if (k.d_br < g_br_min) { g_br_min = k.d_br; }
1445 if (k.d_br > g_br_max) { g_br_max = k.d_br; }
1446 
1447 /* --- LOP 1 + LOP 2: bien an toan, roi dem so lan LIEN TIEP --- */
1448 if (la_xanh_chac(&k))
1449 {
1450 if (g_xanh_lien_tiep < 255u) { g_xanh_lien_tiep++; }
1451 if (g_xanh_lien_tiep > g_xanh_max) { g_xanh_max = g_xanh_lien_tiep; }
1452 }
1453 else
1454 {
1455 /* Chuoi xanh vua DUT. Neu dut khi chua du xac nhan thi rat co the mot
1456 phoi xanh vua troi qua ma khong kip ket luan - dem lai de biet co
1457 phai dang dat XAC_NHAN_XANH cao qua so voi toc do bang tai khong. */
1458 if ((g_xanh_lien_tiep > 0u) && (g_xanh_lien_tiep < XAC_NHAN_XANH))
1459 {
1460 g_dem_hut++;
1461 }
1462 if (k.mau == TCS_XANH_DUONG)
1463 {
1464 /* Driver bao xanh nhung KHONG du bien => coi nhu chua ket luan duoc.
1465 Dem lai: con so nay lon nghia la BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN dang dat cao qua
1466 so voi phoi that (bo sot phoi), hoac nen dang troi len. */
1467 g_dem_xanh_yeu++;
1468 }
1469 g_xanh_lien_tiep = 0u;
1470 }
1471 
1472 /* --- LOP 3: chi TRONG (bang tai rong) moi duoc coi la "da roi xanh" ---
1473 ⚠ Neu chi can "khac xanh" la du thi MAU KHAC cung thoa, va mot vat khong
1474 phai xanh nam giua hai lan doc xanh gia van mo duong cho lan gat ke
1475 tiep. Bang tai PHAI trong tro lai roi moi nhan phoi moi. */
1476 if (k.mau == TCS_TRONG)
1477 {
1478 if (g_trong_lien_tiep < 255u) { g_trong_lien_tiep++; }
1479 if (g_trong_lien_tiep >= XAC_NHAN_TRONG) { g_da_roi_xanh = 1u; }
1480 }
1481 else
1482 {
1483 g_trong_lien_tiep = 0u;
1484 if (k.mau == TCS_MAU_KHAC) { g_dem_khac++; }
1485 }
1486 
1487 /* --- Dem MAU KHAC LIEN TIEP cho chu trinh BAO DO. Dem rieng, khong dung
1488 chung g_dem_khac vi con so kia dem TONG so lan doc chu khong phai chuoi
1489 lien tiep. */
1490 if (k.mau == TCS_MAU_KHAC)
1491 {
1492 /* Ghi lai thoi diem BAT DAU chuoi - chuoi bi dut thi lan sau tinh lai
1493 tu dau. Chinh cho nay chan duoc chuyen tiep mep phoi xanh: no chi
1494 keo dai vai chuc ms nen khong bao gio du TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS. */
1495 if (g_do_lien_tiep == 0u) { g_do_moc = HAL_GetTick(); }
1496 if (g_do_lien_tiep < 255u) { g_do_lien_tiep++; }
1497 }
1498 else
1499 {
1500 g_do_lien_tiep = 0u;
1501 }
1502 return 1u;
1503}
1504 
1505/* ======================================================================== */
1506/* KHOI B - MAY TRANG THAI CO CAU GAT (chi nghe GY-33) */
1507/* ======================================================================== */
1508 
1509static const char *ten_buoc(Gat_t b)
1510{
1511 switch (b)
1512 {
1513 case G_SAN_SANG: return "SAN SANG (cho GY-33 bao xanh duong)";
1514 case G_NANG: return "SERVO DANG NANG CANH RA GOC CHO";
1515 case G_CHO_TROI: return "CHO PHOI TROI TOI CANH GAT";
1516 case G_DANG_GAT: return "SERVO DANG RA GOC GAT";
1517 case G_GIU: return "GIU TAI GOC GAT";
1518 case G_VE_HOME: return "SERVO DANG VE HOME";
1519 default: return "KHOA (cho phoi roi + het nghi)";
1520 }
1521}
1522 
1523static void doi_buoc(Gat_t buoc_moi)
1524{
1525 g_gat_buoc = buoc_moi;
1526}
1527 
1528/* Dua co cau ve trang thai an toan roi BAT PHAI qua buoc KHOA - con cau
1529 phai thay phoi roi khoi tam nhin + het thoi gian nghi moi gat tiep. */
1530static void gat_ve_an_toan(void)
1531{
1532 Servo_DatTocDo(BUOC_EM); /* ve home theo duong an toan: luon di EM */
1533 Servo_DatGoc(GOC_NGHI_DO);
1534 g_xanh_lien_tiep = 0u;
1535 g_do_lien_tiep = 0u;
1536 g_trong_lien_tiep = 0u;
1537 g_da_roi_xanh = 0u;
1538 g_moc_ve_home = HAL_GetTick();
1539 doi_buoc(G_KHOA);
1540}
1541 
1542/* VAO TRANG THAI KHOA - dung luc KHOI DONG va sau cac lenh hieu chinh.
1543 ⚠ CO Y KHONG vao thang G_SAN_SANG. Day chinh la LOP 3 (canh len) chong loi
1544 "vua cap nguon servo da gat": he BUOC phai quan sat duoc bang tai TRONG on
1545 dinh XAC_NHAN_TRONG lan lien tiep roi moi tu vu trang. Mot cai nen dung
1546 yen - du van mau cua no co nam sat nguong den dau - cung khong bao gio tu
1547 kich duoc mot lan gat nao. */
1548static void gat_vu_trang(void)
1549{
1550 Servo_DatTocDo(BUOC_EM); /* ve home theo duong an toan: luon di EM */
1551 Servo_DatGoc(GOC_NGHI_DO);
1552 g_xanh_lien_tiep = 0u;
1553 g_do_lien_tiep = 0u;
1554 g_trong_lien_tiep = 0u;
1555 g_da_roi_xanh = 0u;
1556 g_moc_ve_home = HAL_GetTick();
1557 doi_buoc(G_KHOA);
1558}
1559 
1560/* LED PC13 BAO TRANG THAI - KENH CHAN DOAN DUY NHAT CUA BAN NoUART (khong go
1561 lenh duoc, va binh thuong chay khong cam FT232).
1562 ⚠ PC13 tren Blue Pill la ACTIVE-LOW: GPIO_PIN_RESET = SANG, SET = TAT.
1563 QUY UOC (uu tien tu tren xuong):
1564 Nhay RAT NHANH (5 lan/giay) : MAT LIEN LAC I2C voi cam bien mau
1565 SANG LIEN TUC : dang chay chu trinh gat (xanh HOAC do)
1566 Nhay NGAN moi 1 giay : bang tai DANG CHAY, san sang nhan phoi
1567 Nhay NGAN moi 3 giay : bang tai DANG DUNG, cho nut START
1568 Dung `now % chu_ky` nen khong can bien trang thai rieng va khong troi nhip
1569 du vong lap chay nhanh cham khac nhau. */
1570static void led_trang_thai(uint32_t now)
1571{
1572 uint32_t chu_ky;
1573 uint32_t sang;
1574 
1575 if (g_mat_lien_lac != 0u)
1576 {
1577 chu_ky = 200u; sang = 100u; /* nhay rat nhanh */
1578 }
1579 else if ((g_gat_buoc != G_SAN_SANG) && (g_gat_buoc != G_KHOA))
1580 {
1581 chu_ky = 1u; sang = 1u; /* now%1 = 0 < 1 => SANG LIEN */
1582 }
1583 else if (g_motor_chay != 0u)
1584 {
1585 chu_ky = 1000u; sang = 80u; /* nhay ngan moi 1 giay */
1586 }
1587 else
1588 {
1589 chu_ky = 3000u; sang = 80u; /* nhay ngan moi 3 giay */
1590 }
1591 
1592 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(LED_GPIO_Port, LED_Pin,
1593 ((now % chu_ky) < sang) ? GPIO_PIN_RESET : GPIO_PIN_SET);
1594}
1595 
1596/* Chay 1 nhip cua co cau gat. Goi lien tuc trong vong lap chinh.
1597 ⚠⚠ KIEM TRA LIEN DONG khi doc/sua ham nay: trong toan bo ham KHONG duoc
1598 xuat hien g_ir_vat, IR_E18_Pin, g_motor_chay, BTN_START_Pin hay
1599 BTN_STOP_Pin. Dau vao hop le DUY NHAT la g_xanh_lien_tiep va g_da_roi_xanh
1600 - ca hai do gy33_quet() sinh ra. */
1601static void gat_task(void)
1602{
1603 static uint32_t moc = 0; /* moc thoi gian cua buoc hien tai */
1604 uint32_t now = HAL_GetTick();
1605 char b[150];
1606 
1607 if (g_tu_dong == 0u) /* dang tam dung bang lenh 's' */
1608 {
1609 return;
1610 }
1611 
1612 /* Chong ket CO CAU CHAP HANH: chi giam sat cac buoc CO CHUYEN DONG hoac
1613 CO DEM GIO. G_SAN_SANG va G_KHOA duoc phep o lai vo han (bang tai co
1614 the trong ca chuc phut) nen KHONG bi timeout. */
1615 if ((g_gat_buoc != G_SAN_SANG) && (g_gat_buoc != G_KHOA) &&
1616 ((now - moc) > TIMEOUT_GAT_MS))
1617 {
1618 uart_in("!! TIMEOUT co cau gat - servo khong bao 'den noi'.\r\n"
1619 " Kiem tra nguon 6V cua servo va co khi canh gat co bi ket.\r\n"
1620 " Danh servo ve home va vu trang lai.\r\n");
1621 gat_ve_an_toan();
1622 moc = now;
1623 return;
1624 }
1625 
1626 switch (g_gat_buoc)
1627 {
1628 /* ---- 1. SAN SANG: cho GY-33 xac nhan XANH DUONG ---- */
1629 case G_SAN_SANG:
1630 if (g_xanh_lien_tiep >= XAC_NHAN_XANH)
1631 {
1632 g_dem_gat++;
1633 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n===== PHOI XANH #%lu: xac nhan %u lan lien tiep =====\r\n",
1634 (unsigned long)g_dem_gat, (unsigned)g_xanh_lien_tiep);
1635 uart_in(b);
1636 /* In DU BANG CHUNG da dung de quyet dinh - de khi gat oan con truy
1637 duoc no gat vi con so nao. */
1638 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " b-r=%+d (nen %+d, can >= %+d) b-g=%+d C=%u\r\n",
1639 g_kq.d_br, TCS_LayDbrNen(),
1640 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN),
1641 g_kq.d_bg, (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c);
1642 uart_in(b);
1643 /* Nap tham so cho chu trinh XANH roi dung chung cac trang thai giua.
1644 Chu trinh XANH co them chang NANG CANH 90 -> 180 do. */
1645 g_goc_cho = GOC_CHO_XANH_DO;
1646 g_buoc_nang = BUOC_XANH_NANG;
1647 g_goc_dich = GOC_GAT_DO;
1648 g_tre_ms = TRE_GAT_XANH_MS;
1649 g_giu_ms = GIU_XANH_MS;
1650 g_buoc_di = BUOC_XANH_DI;
1651 g_buoc_ve = BUOC_XANH_VE;
1652 g_ten_ct = "XANH";
1653 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [XANH]: nang canh %u do -> %u do (buoc %uus)\r\n",
1654 (unsigned)GOC_NGHI_DO, (unsigned)g_goc_cho,
1655 (unsigned)g_buoc_nang);
1656 uart_in(b);
1657 Servo_DatTocDo(g_buoc_nang); /* PHAI dat TRUOC Servo_DatGoc */
1658 Servo_DatGoc(g_goc_cho);
1659 doi_buoc(G_NANG);
1660 moc = HAL_GetTick(); /* lay lai tick: uart_in vua ton thoi gian */
1661 }
1662 /* ---- PHOI DO (= MAU KHAC): bao hieu, KHONG cham vao phoi ----
1663 Xet SAU xanh trong cung mot lan goi, nen mot lan doc da ket luan
1664 xanh thi khong bao gio roi vao day. Chu trinh do khong co tre van
1665 chuyen vi 90 do chi la bao hieu. */
1666 else if ((g_do_lien_tiep >= XAC_NHAN_DO) &&
1667 ((now - g_do_moc) >= TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS))
1668 {
1669 g_dem_bao_do++;
1670 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n===== PHOI DO #%lu: %u lan lien tiep, on dinh %lums =====\r\n",
1671 (unsigned long)g_dem_bao_do, (unsigned)g_do_lien_tiep,
1672 (unsigned long)(now - g_do_moc));
1673 uart_in(b);
1674 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " b-r=%+d b-g=%+d C=%u (nguong co vat %u)\r\n",
1675 g_kq.d_br, g_kq.d_bg, (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c,
1676 (unsigned)TCS_LayNguongCoVat());
1677 uart_in(b);
1678 /* Chu trinh DO KHONG nang canh. Dat goc cho = GOC_NGHI_DO thi trang
1679 thai G_NANG di qua NGAY (servo dang dung o home nen Servo_DenNoi()
1680 dung ngay lan goi dau), roi g_tre_ms = 0 lam G_CHO_TROI cung di
1681 qua ngay => servo ra 0 do TUC THI, dung y do "bao hieu dut khoat". */
1682 g_goc_cho = GOC_NGHI_DO;
1683 g_buoc_nang = BUOC_DO_DI; /* khong dung toi - chang dai 0 do */
1684 g_goc_dich = GOC_BAO_DO;
1685 g_tre_ms = TRE_BAO_DO_MS;
1686 g_giu_ms = GIU_BAO_DO_MS;
1687 g_buoc_di = BUOC_DO_DI;
1688 g_buoc_ve = BUOC_DO_VE;
1689 g_ten_ct = "DO";
1690 Servo_DatTocDo(g_buoc_nang);
1691 Servo_DatGoc(g_goc_cho); /* = home, servo khong di chuyen */
1692 doi_buoc(G_NANG);
1693 moc = HAL_GetTick();
1694 }
1695 break;
1696 
1697 /* ---- 1b. NANG CANH: servo truot HOME -> g_goc_cho ----
1698 XANH: 90 -> 180 do, mat ~400ms.
1699 DO : g_goc_cho = 90 = dung home nen Servo_DenNoi() dung ngay, di qua
1700 trong cung mot lan goi ke tiep - khong ton them thoi gian.
1701 ⚠ Trang thai nay NAM TRONG dien giam sat TIMEOUT_GAT_MS. */
1702 case G_NANG:
1703 if (Servo_DenNoi())
1704 {
1705 if (g_tre_ms > 0u)
1706 {
1707 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [%s]: da toi %u do (xung %uus), giu yen %lums cho phoi troi\r\n",
1708 g_ten_ct, (unsigned)Servo_GocDich(),
1709 (unsigned)Servo_XungHienTai(), (unsigned long)g_tre_ms);
1710 uart_in(b);
1711 }
1712 doi_buoc(G_CHO_TROI);
1713 /* MOC dem TRE_GAT_XANH_MS bat dau tu DAY - tuc tu luc servo TOI goc
1714 cho, khong tinh tu luc xac nhan mau. */
1715 moc = HAL_GetTick();
1716 }
1717 break;
1718 
1719 /* ---- 2. CHO TROI: servo NAM YEN o g_goc_cho dung g_tre_ms ----
1720 XANH: nam yen o 180 do (goc cho) du 2 giay cho phoi troi toi canh gat.
1721 DO : g_tre_ms = 0 nen di thang qua day ngay lan goi ke tiep.
1722 ⚠ HOME va GOC CHO la hai goc KHAC NHAU o chu trinh XANH nen phai in
1723 g_goc_cho, khong duoc in GOC_NGHI_DO. */
1724 case G_CHO_TROI:
1725 if ((now - moc) >= g_tre_ms)
1726 {
1727 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [%s]: truot %u do -> %u do (buoc %uus)\r\n",
1728 g_ten_ct, (unsigned)g_goc_cho, (unsigned)g_goc_dich,
1729 (unsigned)g_buoc_di);
1730 uart_in(b);
1731 Servo_DatTocDo(g_buoc_di); /* PHAI dat TRUOC Servo_DatGoc */
1732 Servo_DatGoc(g_goc_dich);
1733 doi_buoc(G_DANG_GAT);
1734 moc = HAL_GetTick();
1735 }
1736 break;
1737 
1738 /* ---- 3. Servo dang truot ra goc gat ---- */
1739 case G_DANG_GAT:
1740 if (Servo_DenNoi())
1741 {
1742 /* In XUNG THAT dang phat kem theo goc: neu mot ngay nao do goc va
1743 xung khong khop nhau nua thi nhin la thay ngay, khong phai doan. */
1744 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [%s]: da toi %u do (xung %uus), giu yen %lums\r\n",
1745 g_ten_ct, (unsigned)Servo_GocDich(), (unsigned)Servo_XungHienTai(),
1746 (unsigned long)g_giu_ms);
1747 uart_in(b);
1748 doi_buoc(G_GIU);
1749 moc = HAL_GetTick();
1750 }
1751 break;
1752 
1753 /* ---- 4. Giu yen tai goc gat ----
1754 Nghi o day cho servo DUNG HAN roi moi doi chieu. Doi chieu ngay luc
1755 con quan tinh chinh la cai lam servo giat va rut dong dot bien. */
1756 case G_GIU:
1757 if ((now - moc) >= g_giu_ms)
1758 {
1759 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [%s]: ve HOME - truot %u do -> %u do (buoc %uus)\r\n",
1760 g_ten_ct, (unsigned)g_goc_dich, (unsigned)GOC_NGHI_DO,
1761 (unsigned)g_buoc_ve);
1762 uart_in(b);
1763 Servo_DatTocDo(g_buoc_ve); /* PHAI dat TRUOC Servo_DatGoc */
1764 Servo_DatGoc(GOC_NGHI_DO);
1765 doi_buoc(G_VE_HOME);
1766 moc = HAL_GetTick();
1767 }
1768 break;
1769 
1770 /* ---- 5. Servo dang truot ve home ---- */
1771 case G_VE_HOME:
1772 if (Servo_DenNoi())
1773 {
1774 /* Chi bat dau dem NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS TU LUC NAY - khong phai tu luc bat
1775 dau gat. Va xoa g_da_roi_xanh de BAT BUOC phai co it nhat mot lan
1776 doc "khac xanh" MOI (sau khi ve home) thi moi vu trang lai. */
1777 g_da_roi_xanh = 0u;
1778 g_xanh_lien_tiep = 0u;
1779 g_trong_lien_tiep = 0u;
1780 g_moc_ve_home = HAL_GetTick();
1781 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> XONG. Da gat: %lu | Mau khac cho qua: %lu | Bo qua: %lu\r\n",
1782 (unsigned long)g_dem_gat, (unsigned long)g_dem_khac,
1783 (unsigned long)g_dem_bo_qua);
1784 uart_in(b);
1785 doi_buoc(G_KHOA);
1786 moc = g_moc_ve_home;
1787 }
1788 break;
1789 
1790 /* ---- 6. KHOA: chong gat lap lai tren CUNG MOT phoi ----
1791 Can DU CA HAI dieu kien moi vu trang lai:
1792 (1) g_da_roi_xanh : GY-33 da doc ra KHAC xanh it nhat mot lan
1793 => phoi that su da troi ra khoi tam nhin
1794 (2) het NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS ke tu luc servo ve toi home
1795 Thieu (1) thi mot phoi xanh dung yen truoc cam bien se bi gat lap di
1796 lap lai. Thieu (2) thi so doc nhap nhay o ranh gioi nguong co the cho
1797 gat hai lan lien nhau tren cung mot phoi. */
1798 default:
1799 {
1800 /* Dem so PHOI bi bo qua, KHONG phai so lan doc. Khong co co chot nay
1801 thi vong lap chay ~6 lan/giay se lam bo dem nhay vo nghia. */
1802 static uint8_t da_dem_bo_qua = 0u;
1803 
1804 if (g_xanh_lien_tiep >= XAC_NHAN_XANH)
1805 {
1806 if (da_dem_bo_qua == 0u)
1807 {
1808 da_dem_bo_qua = 1u;
1809 g_dem_bo_qua++;
1810 uart_in(" [!] Thay XANH nhung dang KHOA -> khong gat.\r\n"
1811 " Neu day la phoi MOI bi bo sot: cac phoi dang di qua\r\n"
1812 " gan nhau hon NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS, hay gian chung ra.\r\n");
1813 }
1814 }
1815 else
1816 {
1817 da_dem_bo_qua = 0u; /* het xanh -> san sang dem phoi ke tiep */
1818 }
1819 
1820 /* g_da_roi_xanh chi bat len khi da doc TRONG du XAC_NHAN_TRONG lan
1821 lien tiep (xem gy33_quet) => bang tai that su da rong tro lai. */
1822 if (g_da_roi_xanh && ((now - g_moc_ve_home) >= NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS))
1823 {
1824 da_dem_bo_qua = 0u;
1825 doi_buoc(G_SAN_SANG);
1826 moc = now;
1827 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " >> Bang tai TRONG (b-r=%+d, nen %+d) - SAN SANG nhan phoi.\r\n",
1828 g_kq.d_br, TCS_LayDbrNen());
1829 uart_in(b);
1830 }
1831 break;
1832 }
1833 }
1834}
1835 
1836/* USER CODE END 0 */
1837 
1838/**
1839 * @brief The application entry point.
1840 * @retval int
1841 */
1842int main(void)
1843{
1844 
1845 /* USER CODE BEGIN 1 */
1846 
1847 /* USER CODE END 1 */
1848 
1849 /* MCU Configuration--------------------------------------------------------*/
1850 
1851 /* Reset of all peripherals, Initializes the Flash interface and the Systick. */
1852 HAL_Init();
1853 
1854 /* USER CODE BEGIN Init */
1855 
1856 /* USER CODE END Init */
1857 
1858 /* Configure the system clock */
1859 SystemClock_Config();
1860 
1861 /* USER CODE BEGIN SysInit */
1862 
1863 /* USER CODE END SysInit */
1864 
1865 /* Initialize all configured peripherals */
1866 MX_GPIO_Init();
1867 MX_I2C2_Init();
1868 MX_TIM3_Init();
1869 MX_TIM4_Init();
1870 MX_USART1_UART_Init();
1871 /* USER CODE BEGIN 2 */
1872 /* HAI VIEC NAY PHAI LA VIEC DAU TIEN, TRUOC CA BANNER.
1873 Neu Servo_Init nam SAU banner thi trong ~150ms dau tien (banner ~95ms +
1874 HAL_Delay(50) cua relay) chan PA6 khong phat xung PWM nao. Servo RC
1875 khong co xung thi khong giu vi tri: no tha troi hoac giu cho cu tu lan
1876 chay truoc roi GIAT ve home khi xung bat dau co - nhin ra ngoai dung la
1877 "servo quay loan luc cap nguon".
1878 MOTOR_TAT cung dua len day: cat relay som nhat co the, khong de bang tai
1879 chay mot nhip nao truoc khi firmware kip kiem soat. */
1880 Servo_Init(&htim3, TIM_CHANNEL_1, GOC_NGHI_DO);
1881 MOTOR_TAT();
1882 g_motor_chay = 0u;
1883 
1884 /* KHOI HIEN THI SO DEM - 2 board 74HC595, moi board 1 LED 7 doan 1 chu so.
1885 Dat NGAY SAU Servo_Init/MOTOR_TAT va TRUOC banner de hai den hien "0"
1886 ngay tu dau; de sau phan do cam bien (co the ton toi 3 x 2s) thi suot
1887 thoi gian do hai den van giu noi dung RAC cua lan chay truoc, nhin ra
1888 ngoai giong het he bi treo.
1889 ⚠ PHAI goi SAU MX_GPIO_Init() vi ham nay ghi vao GPIOB->CRL/CRH.
1890 LED595_TuKiemTra() chan ~1.7 giay de mat thuong xac nhan ngay luc cap
1891 nguon rang den co sang, dung chieu phan cuc va dung thu tu board. */
1892 LED595_Init();
1893 LED595_TuKiemTra();
1894 
1895 /* TAT HAN DUONG NHAN UART - DAY LA LY DO SINH RA BAN NoUART. Trieu chung:
1896 he chi chay on dinh khi CO cam FT232, rut ra la bang tai va cac module
1897 hoat dong lung tung. NGUYEN NHAN: PA10 (USART1_RX) duoc CubeMX cau hinh
1898 GPIO_NOPULL, tuc THA NOI - rut FT232 ra thi PA10 hung nhieu tu motor 24V
1899 => USART1 nhan byte RAC => vong lap chinh thuc thi LENH NGAU NHIEN (bang
1900 lenh co ca 'b' hoc lai nen, '0' reset nguong, 'a'/'n' doi gain).
1901 Hai lop chua: (1) dat USART1 sang UART_MODE_TX, bo thu bi tat han;
1902 (2) keo len PA10 de no khong tha noi. Duong phat PA9 giu nguyen.
1903 ⚠ HAL_UART_Init() goi lai HAL_UART_MspInit() va no dat PA10 ve NOPULL,
1904 nen buoc keo len PHAI lam SAU - khong duoc doi thu tu hai buoc nay. */
1905 huart1.Init.Mode = UART_MODE_TX;
1906 if (HAL_UART_Init(&huart1) != HAL_OK)
1907 {
1908 Error_Handler();
1909 }
1910 {
1911 GPIO_InitTypeDef gpio_rx = {0};
1912 
1913 gpio_rx.Pin = GPIO_PIN_10;
1914 gpio_rx.Mode = GPIO_MODE_INPUT;
1915 gpio_rx.Pull = GPIO_PULLUP;
1916 gpio_rx.Speed = GPIO_SPEED_FREQ_LOW;
1917 HAL_GPIO_Init(GPIOA, &gpio_rx);
1918 }
1919 
1920 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(LED_GPIO_Port, LED_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET); /* LED PC13 tat */
1921 
1922 uart_in("\r\n");
1923 uart_in("==============================================\r\n");
1924 uart_in(" BangTai_PhanLoai_V2_1_NoUART - HE THONG\r\n");
1925 uart_in(" BAN NoUART: UART1 CHI PHAT (PA9=TX) 115200 8N1\r\n");
1926 uart_in(" Duong NHAN da TAT - khong go lenh duoc, chay khong can FT232\r\n");
1927 uart_in(" Trang thai xem bang LED PC13:\r\n");
1928 uart_in(" nhay rat nhanh = mat I2C | sang lien = dang gat\r\n");
1929 uart_in(" nhay 1s = bang tai chay | nhay 3s = dang dung\r\n");
1930 uart_in(" GY-33 (TCS34725): SCL=PB10 SDA=PB11 (I2C2)\r\n");
1931 uart_in(" dia chi 0x29 | CT/DR va S0/S1 DE TRONG | den luon sang\r\n");
1932 uart_in(" IR E18=PA0 | SERVO=PA6(TIM3) | RELAY=PA8\r\n");
1933 uart_in(" NUT START=PA1 STOP=PA2 | ENCODER=PB6/PB7(TIM4)\r\n");
1934 uart_in(" Cam bien dat NGANG, cach vat 10-15cm, do nhay toi da\r\n");
1935 uart_in("----------------------------------------------\r\n");
1936 uart_in(" KICH BAN VAN HANH:\r\n");
1937 uart_in(" * MOTOR : chi chay khi an START, dung khi an STOP.\r\n");
1938 uart_in(" Cap nguon xong bang tai DUNG YEN.\r\n");
1939 uart_in(" * E18 + GY-33: quet LIEN TUC ngay tu khi cap nguon,\r\n");
1940 uart_in(" khong phu thuoc nut nhan hay trang thai motor.\r\n");
1941 uart_in(" * SERVO : HOME = 90 do. CHI GY-33 duoc phep kich gat.\r\n");
1942 uart_in(" XANH: 90->180 do -> giu 2s -> gat ve 0 do\r\n");
1943 uart_in(" -> giu 1s -> ve home 90 do.\r\n");
1944 uart_in(" DO : 90->0 do ngay -> giu 5s -> ve home 90 do.\r\n");
1945 uart_in(" * E18 CHI DE DEM/HIEN THI - TUYET DOI khong kich servo\r\n");
1946 uart_in(" (cat han duong nay de nhieu tu motor 24V tren\r\n");
1947 uart_in(" PA0 khong the lam servo gat oan).\r\n");
1948 uart_in("==============================================\r\n");
1949 
1950 /* AN TOAN KHI BAT NGUON: motor phai DUNG.
1951 Danh RELAY XUONG NHIEU LAN va giu mot lat: neu vua co mot lan RESET (sut
1952 ap do motor/servo) thi relay co the dang dong san, va trong luc STM32
1953 reset chan PA8 tha noi nen module relay giu nguyen trang thai cu. */
1954 MOTOR_TAT();
1955 g_motor_chay = 0u;
1956 HAL_Delay(50);
1957 MOTOR_TAT();
1958 uart_in(">> AN TOAN: da danh RELAY VE TAT. Bang tai phai DUNG luc nay.\r\n");
1959 
1960 /* BAO NGUYEN NHAN LAN KHOI DONG NAY.
1961 Rat quan trong de phan biet "bat nguon binh thuong" voi "vua bi RESET
1962 giua chung do sut ap". Neu thay dong nay in ra GIUA luc dang chay thi
1963 board da reset - do la loi NGUON, khong phai loi logic firmware. */
1964 {
1965 char b[190];
1966 const char *ly_do = "khong ro";
1967 
1968 if (__HAL_RCC_GET_FLAG(RCC_FLAG_LPWRRST)) { ly_do = "LPWR (che do ngu)"; }
1969 else if (__HAL_RCC_GET_FLAG(RCC_FLAG_WWDGRST)) { ly_do = "WWDG (watchdog cua so)"; }
1970 else if (__HAL_RCC_GET_FLAG(RCC_FLAG_IWDGRST)) { ly_do = "IWDG (watchdog doc lap)"; }
1971 else if (__HAL_RCC_GET_FLAG(RCC_FLAG_SFTRST)) { ly_do = "PHAN MEM"; }
1972 else if (__HAL_RCC_GET_FLAG(RCC_FLAG_PORRST)) { ly_do = "POR/PDR - BAT NGUON hoac SUT AP"; }
1973 else if (__HAL_RCC_GET_FLAG(RCC_FLAG_PINRST)) { ly_do = "chan NRST (nut reset)"; }
1974 
1975 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> LY DO KHOI DONG: %s\r\n", ly_do);
1976 uart_in(b);
1977 uart_in(" Neu dong nay in ra GIUA luc dang chay (banner hien 2 lan) thi\r\n"
1978 " board DA RESET => loi NGUON, khong phai loi logic firmware.\r\n");
1979 __HAL_RCC_CLEAR_RESET_FLAGS();
1980 }
1981 
1982 /* Servo DA duoc cap xung tu dong dau tien cua USER CODE BEGIN 2, tuc no da
1983 bat dau di ve home tu truoc khi banner duoc in. Cho not phan hanh trinh

1985 trang thai da bat dau lam viec. */

2007 kieu "while (...) {}" khong dieu kien thoat: no khoa chet ca he thong. */

2010 uint8_t ket_noi = 0u;
2011

2013 Nho thu tu nay, moi lan TCS_Init() deu duoc danh gia bang dung mot lan
2014 kiem_tra_ket_noi() ngay sau no - khong con lan khoi tao cuoi bi bo
2015 khong nhu ban truoc. */
2016 for (lan_thu = 0u; lan_thu < KHOI_DONG_SO_LAN_THU; lan_thu++)
2017 {
2018 if (lan_thu > 0u)
2019 {
2020 uint32_t t0 = HAL_GetTick();
2021 while ((HAL_GetTick() - t0) < 2000u)
2022 {
2023 uint8_t c = uart_doc_lenh();
2024 if ((c == 'k') || (c == 'K'))
2025 {
2026 break; /* kiem tra lai ngay khong doi het 2s */
2027 }
2028 if ((c == 'p') || (c == 'P')) { quet_bus_va_in(); }
2029 nut_quet(HAL_GetTick()); /* van cho phep bat/tat bang tai bang nut */
2030 Servo_Task();
2031 HAL_GPIO_TogglePin(LED_GPIO_Port, LED_Pin); /* nhay nhanh = dang loi */
2032 HAL_Delay(10);
2033 }
2034 TCS_Init(&hi2c2); /* thu khoi tao lai sau khi nguoi dung sua day */
2035 }
2036

2038 {
2039 ket_noi = 1u;
2040 break;
2041 }
2042 }

2045 o CA HAI nhanh (thanh cong lan 2/3 cung da toggle roi), neu khong
2046 quy uoc "LED sang = dang gat" cua vong lap chinh se sai ngay tu dau. */

2272 {

2277

2285 else { uart_in("\r\n>> TAT quet mau\r\n"); }
2286 break;

2290 break;

2325 break;

2331 don vi) - nguong co dinh 12 se chan het ca hai mau. */

2337

2339

2344

2347 {
2348 if (TCS_Doc(&k) == 0u) { continue; }
2349 tong_br += k.d_br;
2350 tong_bg += k.d_bg;
2351 n++;
2352 }
2353 if (n == 0u)
2354 {
2355 uart_in(" Khong doc duoc - go 'k' de kiem tra ket noi\r\n");
2356 g_tu_dong = 1u;
2357 break;

2360 d_bg = (int16_t)(tong_bg / n);
2361 
2362 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Do duoc tren phoi: b-r = %+d | b-g = %+d | C = %u\r\n",
2363 d_br, d_bg, (unsigned)k.raw_c);

2392 gat_vu_trang();
2393 break;
2394 }
2395 
2396 case 'd': case 'D':
2397 {
2398 /* DO THU lien tuc - cong cu kiem tra tam xa lap dat. */
2399 int16_t nbr, nbg;
2400 int16_t br_manh = -999;
2401 uint8_t da_xanh = 0u, da_khac = 0u;
2402 char b[200];
2403 
2404 TCS_LayNguongXanh(&nbr, &nbg);
2405 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n>> DO THU: XANH khi (b-r)>=%+d | NEN o b-r=%+d\r\n",
2406 nbr, TCS_LayDbrNen());
2407 uart_in(b);
2408 uart_in(" Cam phoi dua tu XA lai GAN, xem tu khoang cach nao thi doi\r\n"
2409 " ket luan. Go phim bat ky de dung.\r\n");
2410 
2411 g_tu_dong = 0u; /* tam dung day chuyen khi do */
2412 while (uart_doc_lenh() == 0u)
2413 {
2414 TCS_KetQua_t k;
2415 if (TCS_Doc(&k) == 0u) { ir_quet(HAL_GetTick()); Servo_Task(); continue; }
2416 if (k.d_br > br_manh) { br_manh = k.d_br; }
2417 if (k.mau == TCS_XANH_DUONG) { da_xanh = 1u; }
2418 if (k.mau == TCS_MAU_KHAC) { da_khac = 1u; }
2419 
2420 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " C=%5u | r=%3u g=%3u b=%3u | b-r=%+4d b-g=%+4d | %s%s\r\n",
2421 (unsigned)k.raw_c, k.r, k.g, k.b, k.d_br, k.d_bg,
2422 TCS_TenMau(k.mau), k.bao_hoa ? " [BAO HOA]" : "");
2423 uart_in(b);
2424 ir_quet(HAL_GetTick());
2425 Servo_Task();
2426 }
2427 g_tu_dong = 1u;
2428 gat_vu_trang();
2429 
2430 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> Dung. b-r cao nhat=%+d | da thay XANH:%s | da thay KHAC:%s\r\n",
2431 br_manh, da_xanh ? "co" : "KHONG", da_khac ? "co" : "KHONG");
2432 uart_in(b);
2433 
2434 if ((da_xanh == 0u) && (da_khac == 0u))
2435 {
2436 uart_in(" [!] Luon ra NEN/TRONG => cam bien khong tach duoc phoi\r\n"
2437 " khoi nen. Dua phoi gan hon, hoac go 'b' do lai nen.\r\n");
2438 }
2439 else
2440 {
2441 uart_in(" => DAT. Khoang cach hien tai phan biet duoc. Chay thu di.\r\n");
2442 }
2443 break;
2444 }
2445 
2446 case 'a': case 'A':
2447 {
2448 char b[130];
2449 uint8_t g = (uint8_t)((TCS_LayGain() + 1u) & 0x03u);
2450 TCS_DatGain(g);
2451 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n>> GAIN = %s (tran dem %u)\r\n",
2452 TCS_TenGain(g), (unsigned)TCS_TranDem());
2453 uart_in(b);
2454 uart_in(" Sang qua (bao hoa) thi ha, toi qua thi tang.\r\n"
2455 " Doi xong PHAI go 'b' de dat lai nguong nen theo gain moi.\r\n");
2456 break;
2457 }
2458 
2459 case 'n': case 'N':
2460 {
2461 /* Vong qua cac muc tich phan thong dung */
2462 static const uint8_t ds[5] = { 0xFFu, 0xF6u, 0xEBu, 0xD5u, 0xC0u };
2463 static uint8_t idx = 1u; /* mac dinh 0xF6 = 24ms */
2464 char b[150];
2465 
2466 idx = (uint8_t)((idx + 1u) % 5u);
2467 TCS_DatTichPhan(ds[idx]);
2468 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n>> TICH PHAN = %ums (ATIME=0x%02X, tran dem %u)\r\n",
2469 (unsigned)TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms(), (unsigned)ds[idx],
2470 (unsigned)TCS_TranDem());
2471 uart_in(b);
2472 uart_in(" Dai hon = nhay hon nhung phoi tren bang tai di duoc xa hon\r\n"
2473 " trong luc do. Doi xong PHAI go 'b' de dat lai nguong nen.\r\n");
2474 break;
2475 }
2476 
2477 case 'j': case 'J':
2478 {
2479 /* DO MUC NHIEU tren 3 chan dau vao - CHI DOC, khong dieu khien gi.
2480 Cach dung: go 'j', roi AN NUT START VAT LY cho bang tai chay va
2481 nhin cot %CAO. Chan nao %CAO nhay len khi motor chay la chan do
2482 dang bi nhieu - so lieu de quyet dinh sua phan cung o dau.
2483 ⚠ nut_quet() VAN duoc goi trong vong do nay. */
2484 uint32_t n = 0, cao_ir = 0, cao_start = 0, cao_stop = 0;
2485 uint32_t t_in = HAL_GetTick();
2486 char b[190];
2487 
2488 uart_in("\r\n>> DO NHIEU CHAN DAU VAO. AN NUT START VAT LY de bat\r\n"
2489 " bang tai roi nhin cot %CAO. Go phim bat ky de dung.\r\n");
2490 uart_in(" PA0 IR: binh thuong CAO (khong vat). PA1/PA2 nut: binh\r\n"
2491 " thuong THAP (khong bam) => %CAO phai la 0.\r\n");
2492 
2493 for (;;)
2494 {
2495 if (uart_doc_lenh() != 0u) { break; }
2496 
2497 if (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(IR_E18_GPIO_Port, IR_E18_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) { cao_ir++; }
2498 if (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(BTN_START_GPIO_Port, BTN_START_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) { cao_start++; }
2499 if (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(BTN_STOP_GPIO_Port, BTN_STOP_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) { cao_stop++; }
2500 n++;
2501 
2502 nut_quet(HAL_GetTick()); /* nut vat ly VAN song trong luc do */
2503 Servo_Task();
2504 
2505 if ((HAL_GetTick() - t_in) >= 500u) /* in moi 0.5 giay */
2506 {
2507 t_in = HAL_GetTick();
2508 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " %5lu mau | PA0 IR:%3lu%% | PA1 START:%3lu%% | PA2 STOP:%3lu%% | motor:%s\r\n",
2509 (unsigned long)n,
2510 (unsigned long)((cao_ir * 100u) / n),
2511 (unsigned long)((cao_start * 100u) / n),
2512 (unsigned long)((cao_stop * 100u) / n),
2513 g_motor_chay ? "CHAY" : "dung");
2514 uart_in(b);
2515 n = 0; cao_ir = 0; cao_start = 0; cao_stop = 0;
2516 }
2517 }
2518 uart_in(">> Dung do nhieu.\r\n"
2519 " PA1/PA2 khac 0% khi motor CHAY => nhieu that, phai gan tro\r\n"
2520 " keo xuong 1k-10k va tu 104 tu chan do ve GND.\r\n");
2521 break;
2522 }
2523 
2524 case 'w': case 'W':
2525 {
2526 /* DO DAI NHIEU CUA NEN - chan doan quyet dinh cho loi gat oan.
2527 Doc lien tuc mot loat khi bang tai TRONG, roi bao cao:
2528 - dai dao dong that cua d_br (min..max)
2529 - so lan cham nguong cua DRIVER (se gat neu khong co bien an
2530 toan tang ung dung)
2531 - so lan cham nguong CO BIEN AN TOAN (van con gat oan)
2532 Tu do biet phai dat BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN bao nhieu la du. */
2533 const uint16_t SO_LAN = 30u;
2534 int16_t br_min = 32767, br_max = -32768;
2535 int16_t bg_min = 32767, bg_max = -32768;
2536 uint16_t i, n = 0u, cham_driver = 0u, cham_bien = 0u;
2537 uint16_t so_trong = 0u, so_khac = 0u, so_xanh = 0u;
2538 int16_t nbr, nbg, nen;
2539 char b[200];
2540 
2541 TCS_LayNguongXanh(&nbr, &nbg);
2542 nen = TCS_LayDbrNen();
2543 
2544 uart_in("\r\n===== DO DAI NHIEU CUA NEN =====\r\n"
2545 "BO HET PHOI ra khoi tam nhin cam bien truoc khi do!\r\n");
2546 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "Doc %u lan (~%u giay). Nen=%+d | nguong driver=%+d | co bien=%+d\r\n",
2547 (unsigned)SO_LAN,
2548 (unsigned)((SO_LAN * TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms()) / 1000u),
2549 nen, nbr, (int)(nen + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN));
2550 uart_in(b);
2551 
2552 g_tu_dong = 0u; /* tam dung khoi gat trong luc do */
2553 
2554 for (i = 0u; i < SO_LAN; i++)
2555 {
2556 TCS_KetQua_t k;
2557 if (TCS_Doc(&k) == 0u) { continue; }
2558 n++;
2559 if (k.d_br < br_min) { br_min = k.d_br; }
2560 if (k.d_br > br_max) { br_max = k.d_br; }
2561 if (k.d_bg < bg_min) { bg_min = k.d_bg; }
2562 if (k.d_bg > bg_max) { bg_max = k.d_bg; }
2563 if (k.d_br >= nbr) { cham_driver++; }
2564 if (k.d_br >= (int16_t)(nen + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN)) { cham_bien++; }
2565 if (k.mau == TCS_TRONG) { so_trong++; }
2566 else if (k.mau == TCS_XANH_DUONG) { so_xanh++; }
2567 else { so_khac++; }
2568 }
2569 
2570 if (n == 0u)
2571 {
2572 uart_in(" Khong doc duoc lan nao - go 'k' de kiem tra ket noi.\r\n");
2573 g_tu_dong = 1u;
2574 gat_vu_trang();
2575 break;
2576 }
2577 
2578 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n Doc thanh cong %u/%u lan\r\n", (unsigned)n, (unsigned)SO_LAN);
2579 uart_in(b);
2580 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " d_br: min=%+d max=%+d DAI DAO DONG=%d don vi\r\n",
2581 br_min, br_max, (int)(br_max - br_min));
2582 uart_in(b);
2583 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " d_bg: min=%+d max=%+d\r\n", bg_min, bg_max);
2584 uart_in(b);
2585 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Ket luan: TRONG=%u | MAU KHAC=%u | XANH=%u\r\n",
2586 (unsigned)so_trong, (unsigned)so_khac, (unsigned)so_xanh);
2587 uart_in(b);
2588 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Cham nguong DRIVER (%+d): %u lan | cham nguong CO BIEN (%+d): %u lan\r\n",
2589 nbr, (unsigned)cham_driver,
2590 (int)(nen + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN), (unsigned)cham_bien);
2591 uart_in(b);
2592 
2593 uart_in("\r\n --- KET LUAN ---\r\n");
2594 if (cham_bien > 0u)
2595 {
2596 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " [!!] NEN VAN CHAM NGUONG CO BIEN %u lan => SE CON GAT OAN.\r\n",
2597 (unsigned)cham_bien);
2598 uart_in(b);
2599 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Phai TANG BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN len it nhat %d (dang %d).\r\n",
2600 (int)(br_max - nen + 2), (int)BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN);
2601 uart_in(b);
2602 uart_in(" Neu con so do vuot qua (b-r cua phoi xanh - nen) thi\r\n"
2603 " PHAN CUNG khong du tach bach: dua cam bien lai gan\r\n"
2604 " hon, hoac them den LED trang roi thang vao phoi.\r\n");
2605 }
2606 else if (cham_driver > 0u)
2607 {
2608 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " [OK] Bien an toan dang lam viec: nen cham nguong driver %u lan\r\n"
2609 " nhung KHONG lan nao vuot nguong co bien. Day chinh la\r\n"
2610 " so lan servo SE gat oan neu bo BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN di.\r\n",
2611 (unsigned)cham_driver);
2612 uart_in(b);
2613 }
2614 else
2615 {
2616 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " [OK] Nen rat on dinh (cach nguong %d don vi). Khong co\r\n"
2617 " nguy co gat oan tu nhieu nen.\r\n",
2618 (int)(nbr - br_max));
2619 uart_in(b);
2620 }
2621 uart_in(" Buoc tiep theo: dat phoi XANH that vao roi go 'd' de xem\r\n"
2622 " b-r cua no cao hon nen bao nhieu - phai CAO HON dai nhieu.\r\n");
2623 
2624 g_tu_dong = 1u;
2625 gat_vu_trang();
2626 break;
2627 }
2628 
2629 case 'y': case 'Y':
2630 {
2631 /* Bat/tat NEN TU BAM - tu bu troi anh sang / xe dich cam bien */
2632 char b[190];
2633 TCS_BatBamNen((uint8_t)(!TCS_LayBamNen()));
2634 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n>> NEN TU BAM = %s (da tu doi moc %u lan) | moc: b-r=%+d\r\n",
2635 TCS_LayBamNen() ? "BAT" : "TAT",
2636 (unsigned)TCS_LaySoLanBamNen(), TCS_LayDbrNen());
2637 uart_in(b);
2638 uart_in(" TAT neu phoi hay dung yen truoc cam bien nhieu giay lien.\r\n");
2639 break;
2640 }
2641 
2642 case '0':
2643 {
2644 /* Nut thoat hiem: dat lai toan bo nguong ve mac dinh */
2645 int16_t nbr, nbg;
2646 char b[190];
2647 TCS_DatLaiMacDinh();
2648 TCS_LayNguongXanh(&nbr, &nbg);
2649 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n>> DA DAT LAI MAC DINH: XANH khi (b-r)>=%+d | NEN o %+d\r\n",
2650 nbr, TCS_LayDbrNen());
2651 uart_in(b);
2652 break;
2653 }

2669 uart_in(b);
2670 break;
2671 }
2672

2699 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN),
2700 g_da_roi_xanh ? "co" : "CHUA");
2701 uart_in(b);

2703 g_br_min, g_br_max, (int)(g_br_max - g_br_min));
2704 uart_in(b);

2706 (unsigned long)g_dem_xanh_yeu);
2707 uart_in(b);
2708 /* HAI CON SO QUYET DINH viec co phai ha XAC_NHAN_XANH hay khong */
2709 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Chuoi xanh DAI NHAT tung dat=%u (can %u) | so lan DUT giua chung=%lu\r\n",
2710 (unsigned)g_xanh_max, (unsigned)XAC_NHAN_XANH,
2711 (unsigned long)g_dem_hut);
2712 uart_in(b);
2713 if ((g_dem_hut > 0u) && (g_xanh_max < XAC_NHAN_XANH))
2714 {
2715 uart_in(" [!] CHUA LAN NAO du xac nhan ma da co lan dut => phoi troi\r\n"
2716 " QUA NHANH so voi ~350ms can de xac nhan 2 lan.\r\n"
2717 " Cach chua: ha thoi gian tich phan bang lenh 'n' (154ms\r\n"
2718 " -> 103ms), HOAC cho bang tai chay cham lai.\r\n");
2719 }
2720 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Da gat=%lu | Mau khac=%lu | Bo qua (dang khoa)=%lu | Servo=%u do (%uus)\r\n",
2721 (unsigned long)g_dem_gat, (unsigned long)g_dem_khac,
2722 (unsigned long)g_dem_bo_qua,
2723 (unsigned)Servo_GocDich(), (unsigned)Servo_XungHienTai());
2724 uart_in(b);
2725 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " XANH: %u->%u do -> cho %lums -> gat %u do -> giu %lums -> ve %u do -> nghi %lums\r\n",
2726 (unsigned)GOC_NGHI_DO, (unsigned)GOC_CHO_XANH_DO,
2727 (unsigned long)TRE_GAT_XANH_MS, (unsigned)GOC_GAT_DO,
2728 (unsigned long)GIU_XANH_MS, (unsigned)GOC_NGHI_DO,
2729 (unsigned long)NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS);
2730 uart_in(b);
2731 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " DO : %u->%u do ngay -> giu %lums -> ve %u do\r\n",
2732 (unsigned)GOC_NGHI_DO, (unsigned)GOC_BAO_DO,
2733 (unsigned long)GIU_BAO_DO_MS, (unsigned)GOC_NGHI_DO);
2734 uart_in(b);
2735 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " E18 (chi hien thi): %s | so lan vat di qua=%lu | BANG TAI=%s\r\n",
2736 g_ir_vat ? "CO VAT" : "TRONG",
2737 (unsigned long)g_ir_so_lan_vat,
2738 g_motor_chay ? "CHAY" : "DUNG");
2739 uart_in(b);
2740 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Cam bien: gain=%s | tich phan=%ums | tran dem=%u\r\n",
2741 TCS_TenGain(TCS_LayGain()), (unsigned)TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms(),
2742 (unsigned)TCS_TranDem());
2743 uart_in(b);
2744 {
2745 /* Suc khoe bus I2C - so lieu dinh luong de so voi ban TCS3200 */
2746 uint32_t so_loi, so_cuu;
2747 TCS_LayThongKeLoi(&so_loi, &so_cuu);
2748 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Bus I2C: %lu loi | %lu lan tu cuu bus | %lu lan doc\r\n",
2749 (unsigned long)so_loi, (unsigned long)so_cuu,
2750 (unsigned long)g_so_lan_doc);
2751 uart_in(b);
2752 if (so_cuu > 0u)
2753 {
2754 uart_in(" [!] Da phai cuu bus => hoac MCU phu GY-33 tranh bus,\r\n"
2755 " hoac nhieu tu motor 24V. Xem muc 9 tai lieu.\r\n");
2756 }
2757 }
2758 {
2759 int16_t nbr, nbg;
2760 TCS_LayNguongXanh(&nbr, &nbg);
2761 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " XANH khi (b-r)>=%+d va (b-g)>=%+d | NEN o b-r=%+d b-g=%+d\r\n",
2762 nbr, nbg, TCS_LayDbrNen(), TCS_LayDbgNen());
2763 uart_in(b);
2764 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Bam nen: %s (%u lan) | so mau loc=%u\r\n",
2765 TCS_LayBamNen() ? "BAT" : "tat",
2766 (unsigned)TCS_LaySoLanBamNen(), (unsigned)TCS_LaySoMauLoc());
2767 uart_in(b);
2768 }
2769 break;
2770 }
2771 
2772 case 'e': case 'E':
2773 {
2774 char b[150];
2775 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n>> ENCODER: xung=%ld | vong=%ld.%02ld | toc do=%ld rpm\r\n",
2776 (long)g_encoder_cnt, (long)(g_vong_x100 / 100),
2777 (long)(g_vong_x100 % 100), (long)g_rpm);
2778 uart_in(b);
2779 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Bang tai=%s | Nhan START=%lu lan | Nhan STOP=%lu lan\r\n",
2780 g_motor_chay ? "CHAY" : "DUNG",
2781 (unsigned long)g_so_lan_start, (unsigned long)g_so_lan_stop);
2782 uart_in(b);
2783 break;
2784 }
2785 
2786 case 'h': case 'H': case '?':
2787 in_tro_giup();
2788 break;
2789 
2790 default:
2791 break;
2792 }
2793 
2794 /* KHOI A - GY-33 QUET LIEN TUC, VO DIEU KIEN, MOI VONG LAP.
2795 Day la NGUON KICH DUY NHAT cua co cau gat: khong phu thuoc nut nhan,
2796 motor hay E18. Mot lan doc ton ~154ms (mot chu ky tich phan); trong
2797 suot thoi gian do driver goi phuc_vu_trong_khi_do() lien tuc nen servo
2798 van truot deu, encoder van dem, nut START/STOP van an lien tay. */
2799 if (gy33_quet() == 0u)
2800 {
2801 /* HAN CHE TAN SUAT IN. Nhanh nay chay MOI VONG LAP khi cam bien mat
2802 lien lac, in day du moi vong se do chu lien tuc (terminal khong doc
2803 noi - dung luc can no nhat de chan doan) va moi lan in ~130 ky tu o
2804 115200 chan ~11ms, lam cham han vong lap va do dai chu ky quet nut /
2805 IR / servo. Chi in LAN DAU va sau do moi MAT_LIEN_LAC_IN_MS mot lan. */
2806 uint32_t nay = HAL_GetTick();
2807 if ((g_mat_lien_lac == 0u) ||
2808 ((nay - g_moc_in_mat) >= MAT_LIEN_LAC_IN_MS))
2809 {
2810 g_mat_lien_lac = 1u;
2811 g_moc_in_mat = nay;
2812 uart_in("!! MAT LIEN LAC I2C voi GY-33 - driver da tu cuu bus nhung van\r\n"
2813 " khong doc duoc. Go 'p' de quet bus, 'x' de cuu bus bang tay.\r\n");
2814 }
2815 }
2816 else

Colour driver interface, thresholds and calibration constants

Device

1/**
2 ******************************************************************************
3 * @file tcs34725.h
4 * @brief Driver cam bien mau TCS34725 (I2C) cho STM32F103C8T6
5 * -> BAN "CHI NHAN XANH DUONG" (blue = co tin hieu)
6 *
7 * TCS34725 la cam bien SO: 4 bo dem 16-bit (Clear, Red, Green, Blue) dem so
8 * photon trong mot KHOANG TICH PHAN, het khoang do ca 4 kenh cung chot gia
9 * tri MOT LUC va MCU doc ra qua I2C.
10 *
11 * NGUYEN LY PHAN LOAI - CHI CO 3 KET LUAN (them TCS_KHONG_RO khi so lieu
12 * khong tin duoc):
13 * TCS_TRONG - khong co vat truoc cam bien
14 * TCS_XANH_DUONG - co vat va vat mau XANH DUONG => RA TIN HIEU
15 * TCS_MAU_KHAC - co vat nhung KHONG phai xanh duong => phan loai ra
16 *
17 * Cach nhan xanh duong: chuan hoa 3 kenh ve TONG = 255 (bo do sang di, chi
18 * con THANH PHAN MAU => khong phu thuoc khoang cach), roi doi HAI dieu kien
19 * d_br = b - r >= nguong_br (truc quyet dinh chinh)
20 * d_bg = b - g >= nguong_bg (chot chan vat XANH LA)
21 * Can dieu kien thu hai vi neu chi xet b-r thi vat XANH LA cung cho b-r
22 * duong nhe va se bi bao nham thanh xanh duong.
23 *
24 * Vi du van mau sau chuan hoa (r+g+b = 255):
25 * Xanh duong dam : r= 57 g= 78 b=120 -> d_br=+63 d_bg=+42 => XANH
26 * Xanh duong nhat: r= 73 g= 82 b=100 -> d_br=+27 d_bg=+18 => XANH
27 * Nen xanh la : r= 83 g= 84 b= 88 -> d_br= +5 d_bg= +4 => KHAC
28 * Do : r=140 g= 60 b= 55 -> d_br=-85 => KHAC
29 * Trang : r= 85 g= 85 b= 85 -> d_br= 0 => KHAC
30 *
31 * LAP DAT: cam bien DAT NGANG, vat cach 10-15 cm. Day la khoang cach rat xa
32 * voi module GY-33 (chi co 4 den LED trang nho) va anh sang phan xa giam
33 * theo ~1/d^2, nen mac dinh chay o DO NHAY TOI DA: GAIN 60x, ATIME 0xC0
34 * (154ms). Phoi di nhanh thi ha ATIME xuong 0xD5 (103ms).
35 * ⚠ Hai han che vat ly o khoang cach nay: (1) vat mau TOI phan xa rat it,
36 * kenh CLEAR co the khong vuot noi san toi va bi coi la "TRONG"; (2) anh
37 * sang phong cung bi tinh vao, anh sang doi thi phai do lai nen va hoc lai.
38 *
39 * DAU DAY - MODULE GY-33 ("TCS34725 I2C UART"):
40 * VCC -> 3.3V | GND -> GND chung (chan 'G' cung la GND)
41 * SCL -> PB10 (I2C2_SCL) SDA -> PB11 (I2C2_SDA)
42 * DR / CT / S0 / S1 / INT / NC -> DE TRONG
43 * Module khong co chan LED: 4 den trang sang thuong truc khi co dien 3.3V.
44 * Dia chi I2C: 0x29 (7-bit).
45 * ⚠ GY-33 CO HAI DUONG I2C RIENG: SCL/SDA noi THANG vao chip TCS34725 (dia
46 * chi 0x29, DUNG CAI NAY), con CT/DR noi vao MCU PHU tren module (0x5A,
47 * khong dung). Quet bus phai thay 0x29; thay 0x5A la dang cam nham.
48 ******************************************************************************
49 */
50 
51#ifndef __TCS34725_H
52#define __TCS34725_H
53 
54#ifdef __cplusplus
55extern "C" {
56#endif
57 
58#include "main.h"
59 
60/* ====================== THONG SO CAN HIEU CHINH ============================ */
61 
62/* Dia chi I2C 7-bit cua chip TCS34725 goc */
63#define TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT 0x29u
64 
65/* Dia chi cua MCU phu tren module GY-33 (cap chan CT/DR) - CHI de nhan dang
66 khi quet bus, driver KHONG giao tiep voi no. */
67#define TCS_DIA_CHI_GY33 0x5Au
68 
69/* Chan bus I2C - CHI dung cho thu tuc TU CUU BUS (tam bien thanh GPIO de danh
70 9 xung SCL). Doi bus I2C khac thi sua o day. */
71#define TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port GPIOB
72#define TCS_SCL_Pin GPIO_PIN_10
73#define TCS_SDA_GPIO_Port GPIOB
74#define TCS_SDA_Pin GPIO_PIN_11
75 
76/* THOI GIAN TICH PHAN: t = (256 - ATIME) x 2.4ms, tran dem = (256-ATIME)x1024
77 (chan tren 65535). Cang dai cang NHAY -> nhin duoc CANG XA.
78 0xFF = 2.4ms tran 1024 | 0xEB = 50ms tran 21504
79 0xD5 = 103ms tran 44032 | 0xC0 = 154ms tran 65535 <== MAC DINH
80 Chon 0xC0 vi o 2.4ms bang tai trong chi doc duoc C = 2 tren tran 1024
81 (0.2% thang do) - mot don vi dem thay doi la van mau lat hoan toan.
82 ⚠ Doi lai moi phep do ton 154ms, nen voi TCS_SO_MAU_LOC = 1 thi phoi phai
83 nam trong tam nhin >= ~350ms de dat XAC_NHAN_XANH = 2. */
84#define TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH 0xC0u /* 154ms */
85 
86/* DO KHUECH DAI (AGAIN): 0=1x 1=4x 2=16x 3=60x.
87 Vat cach 10-15cm + chi co den module => phai dung 60x: tu C = 2 len muc
88 dung duoc can nhan them ~3800 lan, ma ATIME chi cho 64 lan.
89 Ha cam bien xuong 2-3cm thi ha ve 16x cho khoi bao hoa.
90 ⚠ main.c goi TCS_ChongBaoHoa() ngay sau khi nap hai so nay, ham do TU HA
91 gain neu cham tran - nen 60x khong the ket o trang thai bao hoa. */
92#define TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH 3u /* 60x */
93 
94/* SAN TOI: kenh CLEAR duoi muc nay => KHONG co vat truoc cam bien.
95 Gia tri nay phu thuoc anh sang phong va phai do lai tai cho bang
96 TCS_HocSanToi() luc bang tai TRONG - firmware se tu tinh va ghi de. */
97#define TCS_SAN_TOI_MAC_DINH 0u /* 0 = khong co san toi, moi luc deu coi la
98 "co vat", viec phan loai do MAU quyet dinh */
99 
100/* He so an toan khi HOC san toi bang lenh 'b':
101 san_toi = C_trong * TCS_HS_SAN_TOI / 100 + TCS_CONG_SAN_TOI
102 Phai cao hon muc trong mot chut de nhieu khong lam bao "co vat" oan,
103 nhung dung cao qua keo theo vat mau toi bi bo sot. */
104#define TCS_HS_SAN_TOI 160u /* % cua muc do duoc luc trong */
105#define TCS_CONG_SAN_TOI 80u /* cong them mot luong tuyet doi */
106 
107/* NHAN "CO VAT" BANG DO SANG - CHIEU DAO NGUOC.
108 Code goc gia dinh vat SANG hon nen; rig nay NGUOC LAI: cam bien nhin vao
109 nen SANG, phoi CHE bot no di nen vat TOI hon (do that khi chua co man che:
110 NEN 313..566 | PHOI XANH 190 | PHOI DO 155..236).
111 Buoc nay khong dung truc MAU duoc vi phoi toi hon nen thi van mau cua phoi
112 bi nen ap dao - phoi do ra b-r = -10..-6 trong khi nen la -9..-10, HAI CUM
113 CHONG NHAU. Truc do sang thi tach bach.
114 Dung TI LE chu khong dung so tuyet doi vi anh sang phong co doi:
115 co_vat khi C < C_nen * TCS_PT_CO_VAT / 100
116 Chon 85%: so do that (gain 60x + 154ms, khong man che) NEN 14480 | XANH
117 10050 | DO 8865 => nguong 12308, can hai phia (cach nen 15.0%, cach xanh
118 18.3%, cach do 28.0%). Voi 70% thi nguong chi cach phoi XANH 0.85% - nen
119 troi xuong 0.9% la phoi xanh bi ket luan "TRONG" va bo sot hoan toan.
120 ⚠ Con so nay chi dung khi KHONG co man che; lam lai man che thi phai DO
121 LAI ca ba muc va tinh lai. */
122#define TCS_PT_CO_VAT 85u /* % cua C nen; duoi muc nay = CO VAT */
123 
124/* CONG TAC DAO CHIEU SANG/TOI - buoc "co vat hay khong" theo DO SANG gia
125 dinh mot chieu co dinh, ma chieu do phu thuoc cach chieu sang:
126 1 = VAT TOI HON NEN (nen la anh sang phong, phoi che bot) -> co vat khi C THAP
127 0 = VAT SANG HON NEN (nen o xa den, phoi phan xa den LED) -> co vat khi C CAO
128 CACH BIET PHAI DAT 0 HAY 1: dat phoi truoc cam bien ghi so C, bo phoi ra
129 ghi so C cua nen. C_phoi < C_nen -> de 1; C_phoi > C_nen -> de 0.
130 ⚠ Dat sai chieu thi cam bien bao TRONG VINH VIEN, khong bao gio nhan ra
131 phoi nao, log im lang hoan toan - khong phai loi nguong mau.
132 ⚠ Chi can doi DUNG MOT DONG nay; ba cho phu thuoc no deu tu dao theo bang
133 #if trong tcs34725.c. */
134#define TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN 1u
135 
136/* CONG TAC CHON TRUC NHAN DANG - buoc "co vat hay khong" co HAI cach lam,
137 chon truc nao la tuy dieu kien chieu sang cua rig:
138 1 = nhan bang DO SANG (kenh C so voi muc nen dang bam)
139 0 = nhan bang VAN MAU (b-r, b-g co bam sat diem nen khong)
140 Rig hien tai CO MAN CHE nen dang dat 0. So do that voi man che:
141 DO SANG: NEN C=7380 | DO C=7303 (-1.0%) | XANH C=6930 (-6.1%)
142 -> phoi DO chi toi hon nen 1.0%, khong nguong nao tach duoc
143 VAN MAU: DO +20 < NEN +28 < XANH +38, ba cum tach bach va nen on dinh
144 tuyet doi (20/20 lan doc dung +28/-15, dao dong 0)
145 Khi KHONG co man che thi nguoc lai - luc do phai dat 1.
146 ⚠ Chon 0 thi buoc do sang bi BO QUA hoan toan (TCS_PT_CO_VAT /
147 TCS_PT_CO_VAT_SANG / TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN / bo bam C nen van chay va van in
148 ra nhung khong tham gia quyet dinh), va HAI HANG SO QUYET DINH tro thanh
149 TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR va TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG. */
150#define TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG 0u
151 
152/* Nguong CO VAT cho CHIEU 0 (vat SANG hon nen): co vat khi
153 C > C_nen * TCS_PT_CO_VAT_SANG / 100
154 Phai LON HON 100 (vat sang hon nen bao nhieu % moi tinh la co vat).
155 200 = vat phai sang GAP DOI nen. Do that xong thi dat vao khoang giua
156 C_nen va C_phoi giong het cach da lam voi TCS_PT_CO_VAT o chieu 1.
157 ⚠ Chi co tac dung khi TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN = 0. */
158#define TCS_PT_CO_VAT_SANG 200u
159 
160/* ⚠ GIOI HAN DA BIET CUA CACH NHAN BANG DO SANG - doc truoc khi go loi "bo
161 sot phoi xanh": ti le vat/nen KHONG on dinh theo anh sang, vi VAT duoc
162 chieu bang LED cua module (gan nhu co dinh) con NEN la anh sang PHONG
163 (thay doi). Den phong toi di thi nen tut manh, vat gan nhu dung yen => ti
164 le trach LEN va phoi co the vuot nguong roi bi ket luan la "TRONG". Do
165 that cua cung mot phoi xanh: nen 528/xanh 190 -> 0.36 (den sang), nen
166 272/xanh 170 -> 0.625 (den toi hon).
167 ⚠ DAU HIEU: phoi xanh chay qua ma log im lang hoan toan. Luc do doc C cua
168 nen - neu C_nen da tut nhieu thi phai NANG TCS_PT_CO_VAT len. */
169 
170/* Muc C cua NEN luc khoi dong. Chi la DIEM XUAT PHAT - bo theo doi ben duoi
171 se tu keo ve muc that sau vai lan doc. Co y dat LECH VE PHIA XA de bo bam
172 luon phai di ve phia muc nen that: chieu 1 (nen SANG) xuat phat THAP roi
173 leo len, chieu 0 (nen TOI) xuat phat CAO roi tut xuong.
174 ⚠ 61 chu khong phai 60 vi TCS_C_NEN_TOI_THIEU = 60 la san chan duoi. */
175#if (TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN != 0u)
176#define TCS_C_NEN_MAC_DINH 61u /* nen SANG -> xuat phat thap, leo len */
177#else
178#define TCS_C_NEN_MAC_DINH 1024u /* nen TOI -> xuat phat cao, tut xuong */
179#endif
180 
181/* THEO DOI MUC NEN: LEN NGAY, XUONG CHAM.
182 Len ngay an toan tuyet doi vi vat luon TOI hon nen nen khong vat nao keo
183 muc nen LEN duoc; den sang len thi bat kip ngay.
184 Xuong cham (moi TCS_C_NEN_GIAM_MOI lan doc moi ha 1 don vi) de mot vat nam
185 lau truoc cam bien khong keo tut muc nen xuong ngang no. O ~50 lan doc/giay
186 va he so 16 => ha ~3 don vi/giay. */
187#define TCS_C_NEN_GIAM_MOI 16u
188#define TCS_C_NEN_TOI_THIEU 60u /* khong cho muc nen tut duoi day */
189 
190/* ============ NGUONG NHAN MAU XANH DUONG (tren van mau da chuan hoa) =======
191 d_br = b - r : TRUC QUYET DINH CHINH.
192 d_bg = b - g : CHI la CHOT CHAN, khong phai truc phan biet - kenh GREEN
193 cua chip co dai pho RONG, con dap ung phu o vung 450nm cua xanh duong, nen
194 vat XANH DUONG van lam kenh green dem rat nhieu. Do that: g luon dinh o
195 97-101 du vat dam hay nhat, b chi 91-108 => d_bg chi con +1..-9, bien do
196 qua nho de lam nguong; trong khi d_br rat khoe (+29..+61).
197 
198 Cung MOT vat xanh duong o cac khoang cach khac nhau (gain 60x, 154ms):
199 Rat gan : r=47 g= 98 b=108 -> d_br=+61 d_bg=+10
200 Gan : r=56 g= 98 b= 99 -> d_br=+43 d_bg= +1
201 XA : r=59 g=101 b= 94 -> d_br=+35 d_bg= -7
202 XA hon : r=62 g=100 b= 91 -> d_br=+29 d_bg= -9
203 Kenh g dung yen trong MOI truong hop => co mot THANH PHAN CONG NEN co dinh.
204 Cang xa thi phan xa that cang nho so voi nen do, van mau bi "bac" dan va b
205 chui xuong duoi g - vi vay chot chan phai la mot so AM, va cach chua trieu
206 de la TRU NEN (TCS_BatTruNen).
207 
208 ---- BO SO CHUAN, ba mau do trong CUNG MOT phien, cung khoang cach ----
209 TRONG : r=60 g=104 b=90 -> b-r=+30 b-g=-14
210 XANH : r=55 g=104 b=95 -> b-r=+40 b-g= -9
211 DO : r=75 g= 96 b=83 -> b-r= +8 b-g=-13
212 ⚠ TRAT TU TREN TRUC b-r: DO (+8) < TRONG (+30) < XANH (+40). TRONG NAM O
213 GIUA nen khong the dung mot nguong duy nhat, phai la BA VUNG:
214 b-r >= 35 => XANH | 26..34 => TRONG | <= 25 => MAU KHAC
215 Khoang cach nhan dien cua DO va XANH bang nhau vi ca hai deu phai roi khoi
216 nen dung 5 don vi (mot ben len, mot ben xuong).
217 
218 ⚠ SO DUOI DAY BI GHI DE NGAY LUC KHOI DONG: main.c goi TCS_HocSanToi() va
219 nguong duoc dat lai = d_br_cua_nen + TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN, nen no chi con tac
220 dung khi phep tu do nen THAT BAI. */
221#define TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAC_DINH 60
222 
223/* Bien dat TREN muc nen khi go lenh 'b' (hoc nen):
224 nguong_br = d_br_cua_nen + TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN
225 Do do 'b' luon cho nguong dung voi rig/anh sang hien tai. Nen do duoc
226 +30 => nguong ra +35, dung bang diem giua nen va vat xanh (+40). */
227#define TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN 5
228 
229/* ============ NHAN DIEN NEN (bang tai trong) ==============================
230 ⚠ NEN KHONG PHAI "mau khac can loai ra" - no la KHONG CO VAT. Cam bien dat
231 ngang nen luc trong van thay bang tai + khong khi (C ~ 9000, thua san toi),
232 nen neu chi xet do sang thi luc nao cung bao "co vat" va co cau gat se
233 chay oan khi bang tai trong.
234 
235 Cach nhan ra nen: van mau cua nen rat on dinh va nam o mot diem co dinh
236 tren hai truc; so doc bam sat diem do thi ket luan TRONG:
237 |d_br - d_br_nen| <= TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR VA
238 |d_bg - d_bg_nen| <= TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG
239 
240 ⚠ THU TU XET: XANH DUONG duoc xet TRUOC vung nen (xem tcs34725.c). Vung
241 nen trai ve ca hai phia cua diem nen nen xet nen truoc se nuot mat mep
242 duoi cua dai xanh duong.
243 ⚠ BIEN PHAI CAN HAI PHIA - yeu cau "khoang cach nhan dien cua cac mau khac
244 PHAI BANG xanh duong": xanh duoc nhan khi d_br vuot len +TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN,
245 mau khac duoc nhan khi d_br tut xuong -TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR-1. Hai so bang nhau
246 thi hai loai vat roi khoi nen o cung mot muc lech, tuc duoc phat hien tu
247 cung mot khoang cach. Vi vay TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR = TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN - 1.
248 
249 Hai so duoi la so do that tren rig CO MAN CHE, 20/20 lan doc lien tiep deu
250 dung mot gia tri: r=1846 g=3143 b=2705 -> 61/104/89 -> b-r=+28 b-g=-15.
251 Ba cum: PHOI DO +20 < NEN +28 < PHOI XANH +38.
252 ⚠ Man che lam ba cum sat vao nhau (khong man che la -7/+9/+42, trai rong
253 49 don vi). Van dung duoc vi nhieu bang khong, nhung bien rat mong - xem
254 canh bao o BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN trong main.c.
255 ⚠ Hai so nay VAN bi TCS_HocSanToi() ghi de luc khoi dong. */
256#define TCS_DBR_NEN_MAC_DINH 28
257#define TCS_DBG_NEN_MAC_DINH (-15)
258/* Nua rong vung TRONG tren hai truc.
259 ⚠ Voi cau hinh hien tai (TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG = 0) day la HAI HANG SO
260 QUYET DINH viec tach phoi khoi nen - siet hay noi deu doi hanh vi that.
261 Truc b-g khong tach duoc do voi nen (do -9..-11 so voi nen -11) nen de
262 rong hon; toan bo viec tach nam tren truc b-r. */
263#define TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR 4
264#define TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG 6
265 
266/* Chot chan chong vat XANH LA. Vat xanh la thuc su cho d_bg rat am (-40 tro
267 xuong), con vat xanh duong o XA tut toi -11 (do that). Dat -20 la diem
268 giua: vua cho vat xanh duong o xa lot, vua chan duoc vat xanh la.
269 ⚠ KHONG dat 0 hay -10: se chan mat vat xanh duong o xa.
270 ⚠ Khac voi nguong b-r, so nay KHONG bi TCS_HocSanToi() ghi de luc khoi
271 dong - no giu nguyen cho toi khi sua tay o day. */
272#define TCS_NGUONG_BG_MAC_DINH (-20)
273 
274/* TRE (hysteresis) tren truc b-r: khi DA ket luan xanh duong roi thi nguong
275 ha xuong (nguong_br - TCS_TRE_BR) moi thoi. Chong nhap nhay o ranh gioi.
276 ⚠ PHAI NHO HON khoang cach tu nguong xuong nen (28 - 24 = 4), neu khong
277 thi khi da bat BLUE mot lan, chinh cai NEN cung du giu tin hieu mai khong
278 nha. Do that cho thay so doc rat on dinh (lap lai y het nhieu chu ky) nen
279 khong can tre lon. */
280#define TCS_TRE_BR 2
281 
282/* ⚠ CHAN CUNG cho muc NHA tin hieu: du tre co lon den dau, muc nha khong bao
283 gio duoc tut xuong duoi (d_br_cua_nen + TCS_BIEN_NHA_TOI_THIEU).
284 Ly do: moc nen co the troi len ngang muc nha, luc do CHINH CAI NEN du giu
285 tin hieu BLUE mai khong nha - ket luan dinh luon o XANH DUONG ke ca khi
286 vat da ra khoi vung. */
287#define TCS_BIEN_NHA_TOI_THIEU 3
288 
289/* So lan doc LIEN TIEP khac voi ket luan dang chot thi BUOC phai doi theo,
290 ke ca khi chua co 2 lan doc lien tiep giong nhau. Chong ket luan bi ket
291 cung khi so doc dao dong qua lai giua hai nhom. */
292#define TCS_SO_LAN_EP_DOI 4u
293 
294/* Ti le giu lai cho truc CHINH khi HOC bang lenh 'c':
295 nguong_br = d_br_do_duoc * TCS_HS_HOC / 100
296 60% => vat xanh duong o goc xau / xa hon van lot (do thuc te d_br tut toi
297 30% khi doi goc, nen khong duoc de ti le nay cao qua). */
298#define TCS_HS_HOC 60u
299 
300/* Truc PHU khi hoc: nguong_bg = d_bg_do_duoc - TCS_BIEN_HOC_BG, chan duoi
301 TCS_NGUONG_BG_MAC_DINH. Tru mot bien RONG (25) vi d_bg lao doc theo khoang
302 cach: cung mot vat cho +10 luc gan va -9 luc xa. */
303#define TCS_BIEN_HOC_BG 25
304 
305/* ================== TRU NEN - CACH MUA THEM TAM XA ========================
306 Cam bien luon thu them mot luong anh sang NEN co dinh (den phong, tan xa,
307 dap ung nen cua chip) khong mang mau cua vat. Vat cang XA thi ti le
308 nen/tin hieu cang lon, van mau cang bi keo ve trung tinh => d_br teo dan.
309 Cach chua: do so dem THO cua canh TRONG, roi moi lan doc lay
310 r_hieu_qua = raw_r - nen_r (chan duoi 0)
311 tuong tu cho g va b, XONG MOI chuan hoa.
312 ⚠⚠ CHI DUNG DUOC KHI NEN THUC SU TOI - nen sang ngang vat thi tru nen an
313 gan het tin hieu, con lai vai don vi nhieu va van mau ra rac. Vi vay driver
314 TU DANH GIA nen truoc khi bat: C_trong < TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI thi BAT, tu
315 muc do tro len thi coi la nen sang va KHONG bat.
316 Con mot luoi an toan trong lua tinh: sau khi tru ma tong 3 kenh tut duoi
317 1/8 tong goc thi tu dong quay ve dung so lieu THO cho lan doc do. */
318#define TCS_HS_TRU_NEN 95u /* tru 95% muc nen, chua 5% cho nen troi nhe */
319 
320/* Muc C cua canh TRONG ma tren do coi la "NEN SANG" - khong duoc tru nen,
321 va do sang cung khong con dung de biet co vat hay khong. */
322#define TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI 0u /* 0 = LUON coi la nen sang -> KHONG BAO GIO tru
323 nen (an toan nhat). Muon dung tru nen thi phai
324 do C_trong that roi dat so nay tren no. */
325 
326/* Khi nen SANG: san toi chi con la muc chan NHIEU (C_trong / he so nay),
327 khong phai muc "phan biet co vat". Viec co vat hay khong se do MAU quyet
328 dinh. Chan duoi TCS_SAN_TOI_TOI_THIEU. */
329#define TCS_CHIA_SAN_TOI_NEN_SANG 8u
330#define TCS_SAN_TOI_TOI_THIEU 100u
331 
332/* ============ CHAN RAC KHI HOC NGUONG =====================================
333 Van mau chuan hoa co tong 255. Mot vat xanh duong THAT o rig nay cho
334 d_br = +14..+62. Neu do ra tren +120 nghia la so lieu da hong (thuong do
335 tru nen an mat hai kenh kia) => TU CHOI hoc, dung de nguong rac ghi de. */
336#define TCS_HOC_BR_TOI_DA 120
337 
338/* Chan tren/duoi cho nguong hoc duoc - khong cho hoc ra so vo ly.
339 ⚠ CHAN DUOI DE 0 LA CO Y: chan duoi qua cao se KEP NGUOC nguong tu hoc len
340 va vo hieu hoa am tham phep tu do nen (vd nen do duoc -8, nguong tu hoc -3,
341 bi kep len +8). Doi lai, de 0 thi nguong nam sat nen nen chi can nen troi
342 vai don vi la ket luan nham XANH - trieu chung "vua cap nguon, chua co
343 phoi ma servo gat lien tuc".
344 ⚠ Bien chong gat oan QUAN TRONG HON bien bat duoc phoi: gat oan lam hong
345 me phan loai va co the ket co cau, con bo sot phoi chi la mot phoi di
346 thang. */
347#define TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN 0
348#define TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX 60
349 
350/* ============ HAI CHOT CHAN SO LIEU RAC ===================================
351 Chan mot dong do rac kieu "C=2 | r=0 g=0 b=254 | b-r=+254" (da xay ra that,
352 lap lai 9 lan lien tiep khi bang tai TRONG). Hai cai sai rieng biet:
353 (1) TIN HIEU QUA YEU. Chuan hoa chia cho TONG 3 kenh nen khi tong be thi
354 MOT don vi dem tho bi phong dai thanh ca chuc don vi chuan hoa (tong=3
355 -> 1 dem = 85 don vi; tong=255 -> 1 dem = 1 don vi). Muon d_br dang tin
356 toi +-3 don vi thi can tong >= 85; lay tron 100.
357 (2) SO LIEU VO LY VE VAT LY. Kenh CLEAR khong co kinh loc nen luon thu
358 duoc NHIEU photon hon bat ky kenh mau nao - C=2 ma kenh B suy ra ~510
359 la khong the => chac chan BYTE BI HONG tren duong I2C (I2C khong co CRC
360 cho phan du lieu nen mot bit loi van duoc HAL bao HAL_OK).
361 Ca hai chot tra ve TCS_KHONG_RO - CO Y, vi TCS_KHONG_RO khong thuoc ca ba
362 chuoi dem trong gy33_quet() nen so lieu rac khong kich duoc chu trinh nao.
363 ⚠ Neu bo sot phoi TOI MAU that su vi chot (1): dung ha con so nay ma NANG
364 DO NHAY len (ATIME truoc, roi gain). */
365#define TCS_TONG_TIN_TOI_THIEU 100u /* tong R+G+B toi thieu de tin van mau */
366#define TCS_HS_C_HOP_LE 4u /* kenh mau > C x 4 => du lieu hong */
367 
368/* Muc bao hoa: kenh C HOAC bat ky kenh R/G/B nao vuot ti le nay cua tran dem
369 thi coi nhu chay het thang (phan xa guong cua NHUA BONG hay gap) => so
370 lieu mau khong con dung => phai ha gain hoac ha thoi gian tich phan. */
371#define TCS_MUC_BAO_HOA_PT 90u /* % cua tran dem */
372 
373/* SO LAN DOC LAY TRUNG VI cho moi phep do mau (chong phan xa GUONG cua nhua
374 bong va chong nhieu khi tin hieu yeu o xa). Phai la so LE (1,3,5); 1 = tat
375 loc. Moi lan +1 ton them mot chu ky tich phan (154ms).
376 ⚠ MAC DINH = 1 vi VAT CHAY LIEN TUC qua cam bien: 3 mau -> ~460ms/lan doc
377 la vat di qua truoc khi doc xong, con 1 mau -> ~160ms/lan doc.
378 Ha so mau KHONG lam giam do nhay (tich phan van 154ms), chi bot kha nang
379 chong dot bien - ma so lieu do that lap lai y het nhieu chu ky lien. */
380#define TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAC_DINH 1u
381#define TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX 5u
382 
383/* ================= NEN TU BAM (auto-track) ================================
384 Moc nen troi giua cac lan do (anh sang phong doi, cam bien xe dich) lam ca
385 ba nhom ket luan sai theo. Cach chua: firmware TU CAP NHAT moc nen khi so
386 doc DUNG YEN lien tuc mot khoang du dai - vat chay qua cam bien chi nam
387 trong tam nhin duoi 1 giay, dung yen nhieu giay lien nghia la bang tai
388 dang TRONG.
389 ⚠ BON CHAN AN TOAN chong "moc nen bo dan theo mot vat dung yen" (loi da
390 gap: giu vat do truoc cam bien lau thi moc bo +30 -> +22 -> +15 -> +8 va
391 vat DO bi ket luan la TRONG):
392 1) bo qua nhung lan doc ket luan la XANH DUONG;
393 2) CUA SO TUNG LAN DOC - chi tinh nhung lan doc trong +-
394 TCS_NEN_BAM_CUA_SO quanh moc HIEN TAI;
395 3) TRAN TONG - moc khong duoc di xa qua TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG so voi NEO, day
396 la chan cuoi cung chong kieu bo tung buoc nho;
397 4) khi doi moc, nguong xanh doi theo dung bang ay don vi nen moi hieu
398 chinh tay van duoc giu nguyen. */
399#define TCS_NEN_BAM_SO_LAN 30u /* so lan doc dung yen thi nhan moc moi
400 (~5 giay o 154ms/lan) */
401#define TCS_NEN_BAM_DAO_DONG 2 /* +-bao nhieu van coi la "dung yen" */
402/* Cua so va tran tong de 6 vi chinh cai NEN dich +4 don vi khi motor chay:
403 motor DUNG b-r = +28 | motor CHAY b-r = +32
404 (7/7 lan doc deu dung +32, dao dong 0 - mot trang thai on dinh khac chu
405 khong phai nhieu: motor keo dong lam sut nhe duong nguon, den LED tren
406 module mo di va pho dich ve phia xanh.) Moc nen luon duoc hoc luc KHOI
407 DONG khi motor con DUNG, nen cua so phai LON HON 4 thi bo bam moi di theo
408 duoc.
409 ⚠ Vi sao 6 chu khong phai 8: neo = +28 nen voi 6 thi moc chi chay trong
410 [+22, +34] ma PHOI DO nam o +20 - thap hon can duoi, khong bao gio bo theo
411 duoc. Voi 8 thi khoang la [+20, +36], CHAM DUNG phoi do. */
412#define TCS_NEN_BAM_CUA_SO 6
413#define TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG 6
414 
415/* ========================================================================== */
416 
417typedef enum
418{
419 TCS_TRONG = 0, /* khong co vat truoc cam bien (duoi san toi) */
420 TCS_XANH_DUONG, /* CO VAT + mau XANH DUONG => RA TIN HIEU */
421 TCS_MAU_KHAC, /* CO VAT nhung khong phai xanh duong => loai ra */
422 TCS_KHONG_RO /* bao hoa - so lieu khong tin duoc */
423} TCS_Mau_t;
424 
425typedef struct
426{
427 uint16_t raw_c; /* kenh CLEAR - do sang tong (so dem tho) */
428 uint16_t raw_r; /* kenh DO */
429 uint16_t raw_g; /* kenh XANH LA */
430 uint16_t raw_b; /* kenh XANH DUONG */
431 uint8_t r; /* ti le 0..255, chuan hoa theo TONG 3 kenh => r+g+b=255 */
432 uint8_t g;
433 uint8_t b;
434 int16_t d_br; /* b - r : truc chinh nhan xanh duong */
435 int16_t d_bg; /* b - g : truc phu (b phai la kenh lon nhat)*/
436 uint8_t co_vat; /* 1 = co vat truoc cam bien (C vuot san toi)*/
437 uint8_t la_xanh; /* 1 = ket luan XANH DUONG (chua qua bo loc) */
438 uint8_t bao_hoa; /* 1 = C hoac 1 kenh cham tran, so lieu khong tin */
439 TCS_Mau_t mau; /* ket luan cuoi (da qua bo loc on dinh 2 lan doc) */
440} TCS_KetQua_t;
441 
442/* Ket qua tu kiem tra ket noi - chay truoc khi quet mau */
443typedef struct
444{
445 uint8_t so_tb; /* so thiet bi tra loi tren bus I2C */
446 uint8_t dia_chi[8]; /* danh sach dia chi 7-bit tim duoc */
447 uint8_t id; /* thanh ghi ID doc duoc (0x44 / 0x4D) */
448 uint16_t c, r, g, b; /* mot lan doc thu */
449 uint16_t tran; /* tran dem theo ATIME hien tai */
450 uint8_t ok_bus; /* [1] co thiet bi ACK o 0x29 */
451 uint8_t ok_id; /* [2] ID dung cua TCS34725 */
452 uint8_t ok_doc; /* [3] bat PON+AEN va doc duoc so lieu moi */
453 uint8_t ok_sang; /* [4] du sang (C khong qua thap) */
454 uint8_t ok_khong_bh; /* [5] khong bao hoa */
455 uint8_t ok_tach_mau; /* [6] 3 kenh khong dinh nhau y het */
456 uint8_t du_dieu_kien; /* 1 = du dieu kien de bat dau quet mau */
457} TCS_ChanDoan_t;
458 
459/* Khoi tao: nho handle I2C, PON+AEN, nap ATIME/AGAIN mac dinh (60x/154ms).
460 Tra ve 1 = tim thay va cau hinh duoc cam bien, 0 = khong thay. */
461uint8_t TCS_Init(I2C_HandleTypeDef *hi2c);
462 
463/* HAM PHUC VU goi trong luc CHO CAM BIEN DO XONG.
464 Mot phep do ton tron mot chu ky tich phan (154ms o cau hinh mac dinh) va
465 driver chi ngoi cho co AVALID. Khong lam gi thi ca he dung hinh 154ms:
466 servo khong duoc truot tiep (GIAT rat ro), nut nhan co the bi bo sot, IR
467 khong duoc quet. Dang ky mot ham o day thi driver goi no lien tuc trong
468 luc cho. Ham nay PHAI ngan va KHONG duoc goi lai bat ky ham TCS_* nao. */
469typedef void (*TCS_HamPhucVu_t)(void);
470void TCS_DatHamPhucVu(TCS_HamPhucVu_t ham);
471 
472/* QUET TOAN BO BUS I2C (0x08..0x77) - in ra dia chi nao co thiet bi.
473 Cong cu so 1 khi cam bien "khong noi chuyen": biet ngay la dut day
474 (khong dia chi nao) hay cam nham module MCU phu (chi thay 0x5A).
475 Tra ve so thiet bi tim duoc, ket qua nam trong cd->dia_chi[]. */
476uint8_t TCS_QuetBus(TCS_ChanDoan_t *cd);
477 
478/* TU KIEM TRA KET NOI - chay truoc khi quet mau. Ton khoang 0.5 s.
479 Tra ve 1 = du dieu kien quet mau, 0 = con loi day / cau hinh. */
480uint8_t TCS_KiemTraKetNoi(TCS_ChanDoan_t *cd);
481 
482/* DOC NHANH CHI KENH CLEAR (do sang tong) - 1 chu ky, KHONG loc trung vi.
483 Dung de RINH nhanh / do muc sang nen. */
484uint16_t TCS_DocClear(void);
485 
486/* Doc 1 chu ky day du - CA 4 KENH CUNG MOT LUC (khong nhoe nhu TCS3200),
487 co LOC TRUNG VI TCS_SO_MAU_LOC lan de chong nhieu khi tin hieu yeu.
488 Tu tinh d_br, d_bg, co_vat, bao_hoa va ket luan mau.
489 Tra ve 1 = OK, 0 = loi I2C / khong co so lieu moi. */
490uint8_t TCS_Doc(TCS_KetQua_t *kq);
491 
492/* 1 = ket luan hien tai la XANH DUONG (tien loi cho phan dieu khien) */

495/* Ten ket luan dang chuoi de in ra UART */
496const char *TCS_TenMau(TCS_Mau_t m);

498/* HOC SAN TOI (lenh 'b') - chay khi KHONG co vat truoc cam bien, cam bien
499 dang nhin dung cai canh se thay luc chay that (khoang trong / toi).
500 Do muc CLEAR luc trong roi dat:
501 san_toi = C_trong * TCS_HS_SAN_TOI / 100 + TCS_CONG_SAN_TOI
502 ⚠ PHAI khong co vat nao truoc cam bien khi goi.
503 Tra ve 1 = OK, ket qua do nam trong *kq. */
504uint8_t TCS_HocSanToi(TCS_KetQua_t *kq);

523void TCS_DatCNen(uint16_t c_nen);

1/**
2 ******************************************************************************
3 * @file tcs34725.c
4 * @brief Driver TCS34725 - doc 4 kenh RGBC 16-bit qua I2C.
5 * BAN "CHI NHAN XANH DUONG": XANH DUONG => co tin hieu,
6 * moi mau khac => TCS_MAU_KHAC (phan loai ra), khong co vat => TRONG.
7 * Cau hinh cho cam bien DAT NGANG, vat cach 10-15 cm.
8 ******************************************************************************
9 */
10 
11#include "tcs34725.h"

13/* ------------------------- BAN DO THANH GHI ------------------------------- */
14/* Moi lenh gui toi TCS34725 phai co BIT 7 = 1 (bit COMMAND). Them bit 5 = 1
15 (kieu AUTO-INCREMENT) thi doc nhieu byte lien tiep duoc trong 1 lan. */
16#define CMD 0x80u
17#define CMD_INC 0xA0u /* CMD | auto-increment */
18 
19#define REG_ENABLE 0x00u
20#define REG_ATIME 0x01u
21#define REG_WTIME 0x03u
22#define REG_CONFIG 0x0Du
23#define REG_CONTROL 0x0Fu
24#define REG_ID 0x12u
25#define REG_STATUS 0x13u
26#define REG_CDATAL 0x14u /* C,R,G,B: 8 byte lien tiep tu day */
27 
28#define EN_PON 0x01u /* bat dao dong noi + bo nguon */
29#define EN_AEN 0x02u /* cho phep bo do ADC chay */
30#define ST_AVALID 0x01u /* 1 = da xong 1 chu ky tich phan */
31 
32#define ID_TCS34725 0x44u
33#define ID_TCS34727 0x4Du
34 
35#define I2C_TIMEOUT 100u
36 
37/* ----------------------------- BIEN NOI BO -------------------------------- */
38static I2C_HandleTypeDef *s_hi2c = NULL;
39 
40/* Ham ung dung dang ky de duoc goi trong luc cho cam bien do xong - xem
41 giai thich o TCS_DatHamPhucVu trong tcs34725.h. */
42static TCS_HamPhucVu_t s_ham_phuc_vu = NULL;
43static uint8_t s_atime = TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH;
44static uint8_t s_gain = TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH;
45static uint8_t s_xoa_loc = 0u;
46 
47/* --- SAN TOI: C duoi muc nay => khong co vat truoc cam bien.
48 Cam bien dat ngang, phia sau la khoang trong => luc trong C rat thap.
49 Hoc lai bang lenh 'b' (TCS_HocSanToi). */
50static uint16_t s_san_toi = (uint16_t)TCS_SAN_TOI_MAC_DINH;
51static uint16_t s_c_trong = 0u; /* muc CLEAR do duoc luc trong (de in) */
52 
53/* --- NHAN "CO VAT" BANG DO SANG, chieu dao nguoc (xem tcs34725.h).
54 Rig nay vat TOI hon nen, nen dieu kien la C < nguong => CO VAT.
55 s_c_nen bam theo muc nen: LEN ngay, XUONG cham. */
56static uint16_t s_c_nen = TCS_C_NEN_MAC_DINH;
57static uint8_t s_c_nen_le = 0u; /* dem le de ha 1 don vi moi N lan doc */
58/* Chong mot lan doc dot bien keo moc nen len: phai co HAI lan lien tiep. */
59static uint8_t s_c_len_dem = 0u;
60static uint16_t s_c_len_tam = 0u;
61 
62/* --- TRU NEN: so dem THO cua canh TRONG, hoc bang TCS_HocSanToi ('b').
63 Tru phan nay ra truoc khi chuan hoa => van mau cua vat o XA khong bi
64 "bac" nua. Xem giai thich day du trong tcs34725.h. */
65static uint16_t s_nen_tho_r = 0u;
66static uint16_t s_nen_tho_g = 0u;
67static uint16_t s_nen_tho_b = 0u;
68static uint8_t s_da_hoc_nen = 0u; /* 1 = da co so lieu nen hop le */
69static uint8_t s_tru_nen = 0u; /* 1 = dang tru nen */
70static uint8_t s_nen_sang = 0u; /* 1 = nen sang ngang vat (xem .h) */
71/* Van mau cua NEN tren hai truc quyet dinh. Mac dinh = so DO THAT tren rig
72 (bake trong tcs34725.h) => nhan dien duoc nen ngay khi cap nguon, khong
73 can go 'b'. Lenh 'b' cap nhat lai cho rig/anh sang khac. */
74static int16_t s_d_br_nen = (int16_t)TCS_DBR_NEN_MAC_DINH;
75static int16_t s_d_bg_nen = (int16_t)TCS_DBG_NEN_MAC_DINH;
76 
77/* NEO cua moc nen: gia tri bake, hoac gia tri do duoc lan go 'b' gan nhat.
78 Nen tu bam khong duoc phep di xa qua TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG so voi neo nay. */
79static int16_t s_d_br_neo = (int16_t)TCS_DBR_NEN_MAC_DINH;
80 
81/* --- so mau lay trung vi (chay duoc, doi bang lenh 'f') --- */
82static uint8_t s_so_mau = TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAC_DINH;
83 
84/* --- NEN TU BAM: theo doi mot "ung vien" moc nen. Khi so doc dung yen quanh
85 ung vien du TCS_NEN_BAM_SO_LAN lan lien tiep thi nhan lam moc moi. */
86static uint8_t s_bam_nen = 1u; /* 1 = bat tu bam (mac dinh) */
87static int16_t s_uv_br = 0; /* ung vien: b-r */
88static int16_t s_uv_bg = 0; /* ung vien: b-g */
89static uint8_t s_uv_dem = 0u; /* so lan doc da dung yen quanh uv */
90static uint8_t s_uv_co = 0u; /* 1 = dang co ung vien */
