93/* --- NGUONG NHAN XANH DUONG tren van mau da chuan hoa ---
94 s_nguong_br : truc quyet dinh chinh.
95 s_nguong_bg : chot chan (mac dinh 0 = chi doi b >= g) - xem giai thich
96 ve dai pho kenh green trong tcs34725.h. */
97static int16_t s_nguong_br = (int16_t)TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAC_DINH; /* b - r */
98static int16_t s_nguong_bg = (int16_t)TCS_NGUONG_BG_MAC_DINH; /* b - g */
99 
100/* Trang thai cho TRE (hysteresis) tren truc b-r: 1 = lan doc truoc da ket
101 luan xanh duong => lan nay duoc noi nguong ra TCS_TRE_BR don vi. */
102static uint8_t s_dang_xanh = 0u;
103 
104/* --- theo doi suc khoe bus --- */
105static uint32_t s_so_loi = 0; /* tong so giao dich I2C that bai */
106static uint32_t s_so_lan_cuu = 0; /* so lan phai chay thu tuc cuu bus */
107/* So lan cum 8 byte tra ve so vo ly ve vat ly (mot kenh mau lon hon kenh
108 CLEAR) va phai doc lai bang 8 giao dich rieng le. Con so nay LON nghia la
109 duong I2C dang hong that - xem tcs_doc_rgbc(). */
110static uint32_t s_so_lan_doc_lai = 0;
111static uint8_t s_dang_cuu = 0; /* chong goi de quy khi dang cuu */
112 
113static void tcs_nap_lai_cau_hinh(void);
114 
115/* ========================= TU CUU BUS I2C ================================= */
116 
117/* Cho 'ms' mili giay nhung VAN tra quyen dieu khien ve ung dung trong luc
118 cho, thay vi chan cung nhu HAL_Delay. Thu tuc cuu bus chan toi ~30ms (18
119 nhip SCL + 3ms + 8ms khoi tao lai) va no chay dung luc mat I2C, tuc luc
120 servo dang rut dong - phai nha quyen dieu khien de IR va nut bam van duoc
121 quet. */
122static void tcs_cho_phuc_vu(uint32_t ms)
123{
124 uint32_t moc = HAL_GetTick();
125 
126 while ((HAL_GetTick() - moc) < ms)
127 {
128 if (s_ham_phuc_vu != NULL) { s_ham_phuc_vu(); }
129 }
130}
131 
132/* Nghi 1ms giua hai muc SCL - cuu bus chay ~500Hz, cham nhung chac an. */
133static void tcs_nhip(void)
134{
135 tcs_cho_phuc_vu(1u);
136}
137 
138uint8_t TCS_CuuBus(void)
139{
140 GPIO_InitTypeDef gi = {0};
141 uint8_t i;
142 uint8_t id = 0u;
143 
144 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return 0u; }
145 
146 s_so_lan_cuu++;
147 
148 /* 1. Tra hai chan ve GPIO open-drain, ca hai o muc CAO (nha bus) */
149 HAL_I2C_DeInit(s_hi2c);
150 
151 gi.Pin = TCS_SCL_Pin | TCS_SDA_Pin;
152 gi.Mode = GPIO_MODE_OUTPUT_OD;
153 gi.Pull = GPIO_NOPULL;
154 gi.Speed = GPIO_SPEED_FREQ_HIGH;
155 HAL_GPIO_Init(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, &gi);
156 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, TCS_SCL_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
157 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SDA_GPIO_Port, TCS_SDA_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
158 tcs_nhip();
159 
160 /* 2. Danh 9 xung SCL de slave dang ket giua byte nha SDA ra. */
161 for (i = 0u; i < 9u; i++)
162 {
163 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, TCS_SCL_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
164 tcs_nhip();
165 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, TCS_SCL_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
166 tcs_nhip();
167 }
168 
169 /* 3. Tao dieu kien STOP. */
170 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SDA_GPIO_Port, TCS_SDA_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
171 tcs_nhip();
172 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, TCS_SCL_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
173 tcs_nhip();
174 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SDA_GPIO_Port, TCS_SDA_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
175 tcs_nhip();
176 
177 /* 4. Khoi tao lai khoi I2C (MSP tu tra hai chan ve AF open-drain). */
178 if (HAL_I2C_Init(s_hi2c) != HAL_OK)
179 {
180 return 0u;
181 }
182 
183 /* 5. Nap lai cau hinh cam bien - chip co the da mat trang thai. */
184 s_dang_cuu = 1u;
185 tcs_nap_lai_cau_hinh();
186 (void)HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
187 (uint16_t)(CMD | REG_ID), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
188 &id, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT);
189 s_dang_cuu = 0u;
190 
191 return ((id == ID_TCS34725) || (id == ID_TCS34727)) ? 1u : 0u;
192}
193 
194/* Goi khi mot giao dich that bai: dem loi, va neu chua o trong thu tuc cuu
195 thi chay cuu bus mot lan. Tra ve 1 = da cuu xong, dang goi co the thu lai. */
196static uint8_t tcs_xu_ly_loi(void)
197{
198 s_so_loi++;
199 
200 if (s_dang_cuu) /* dang cuu ma con loi -> khong de quy */
201 {
202 return 0u;
203 }
204 return TCS_CuuBus();
205}
206 
207/* ========================= LOP I2C MUC THAP ===============================
208 Ba ham duoi deu theo mot khuon: thu mot lan; that bai thi cuu bus roi
209 THU LAI DUNG MOT LAN nua. */
210 
211static uint8_t tcs_ghi8(uint8_t reg, uint8_t gt)
212{
213 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return 0u; }
214 
215 if (HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
216 (uint16_t)(CMD | reg), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
217 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT) == HAL_OK)
218 {
219 return 1u;
220 }
221 if (tcs_xu_ly_loi() == 0u) { return 0u; }
222 
223 return (HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
224 (uint16_t)(CMD | reg), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
225 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT) == HAL_OK) ? 1u : 0u;
226}
227 
228static uint8_t tcs_doc8(uint8_t reg, uint8_t *gt)
229{
230 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return 0u; }
231 
232 if (HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
233 (uint16_t)(CMD | reg), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
234 gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT) == HAL_OK)
235 {
236 return 1u;
237 }
238 if (tcs_xu_ly_loi() == 0u) { return 0u; }
239 
240 return (HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
241 (uint16_t)(CMD | reg), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
242 gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT) == HAL_OK) ? 1u : 0u;
243}
244 
245/* Chu ky cua loi truyen tren bus nay: byte cao == byte thap, tuc gia tri
246 chia het cho 0x0101 = 257. Xem tcs_doc_rgbc(). */
247static uint8_t hai_byte_giong_nhau(uint16_t v)
248{
249 return (uint8_t)(((v >> 8) == (v & 0x00FFu)) ? 1u : 0u);
250}
251 
252/* DUONG DU PHONG: doc 8 byte C,R,G,B bang 8 GIAO DICH RIENG LE.
253 Cham hon cum 8 byte nhung khong co pha "hai byte cuoi" nen khong dinh loi
254 lap byte. Xem giai thich trong tcs_doc_rgbc(). */
255static uint8_t tcs_doc_rgbc_tung_byte(uint16_t *c, uint16_t *r,
256 uint16_t *g, uint16_t *b)
257{
258 uint8_t d[8];
259 uint8_t i;
260 
261 for (i = 0u; i < 8u; i++)
262 {
263 if (tcs_doc8((uint8_t)(REG_CDATAL + i), &d[i]) == 0u)
264 {
265 return 0u;
266 }
267 }
268 *c = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[1] << 8) | d[0];
269 *r = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[3] << 8) | d[2];
270 *g = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[5] << 8) | d[4];
271 *b = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[7] << 8) | d[6];
272 return 1u;
273}
274 
275/* Doc lien 8 byte C,R,G,B (moi kenh 2 byte, byte THAP truoc) */
276static uint8_t tcs_doc_rgbc(uint16_t *c, uint16_t *r, uint16_t *g, uint16_t *b)
277{
278 uint8_t d[8];
279 
280 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return 0u; }
281 
282 if (HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
283 (uint16_t)(CMD_INC | REG_CDATAL), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
284 d, 8u, I2C_TIMEOUT) != HAL_OK)
285 {
286 if (tcs_xu_ly_loi() == 0u) { return 0u; }
287 
288 if (HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
289 (uint16_t)(CMD_INC | REG_CDATAL), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
290 d, 8u, I2C_TIMEOUT) != HAL_OK)
291 {
292 return 0u;
293 }
294 }
295 *c = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[1] << 8) | d[0];
296 *r = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[3] << 8) | d[2];
297 *g = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[5] << 8) | d[4];
298 *b = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[7] << 8) | d[6];
299 
300 /* ===== CHOT KIEM TRA + DUONG DOC DU PHONG =====
301 Cum 8 byte auto-increment thi thoang tra ve mot gia tri co BYTE CAO BANG
302 BYTE THAP (chia het cho 0x0101 = 257). Da do duoc that: 24/24 lan doc
303 lien tiep deu chia het 257, va b_tho (~50000) lon gap ~6.6 lan kenh
304 CLEAR (~7568) - vo ly vi CLEAR khong co kinh loc. Cac hang so trong file
305 nay da kiem tra va DUNG nen loi nam o TANG TRUYEN, khong o thanh ghi.
306 Nhan dien bang HAI dau hieu (kiem tra theo do lon mot minh khong du - co
307 lan gia tri rac roi dung vao vung "trong nhu that"): kenh mau lon hon
308 kenh CLEAR, HOAC hai byte giong nhau.
309 ⚠ Mot gia tri THAT cung co 1/256 co hoi trung hai byte nen KHONG loai
310 thang, ma DOC LAI bang duong tung byte (~0.5ms) roi dung so doc lai.
311 ⚠ DAY LA MIENG VA, KHONG PHAI THUOC CHUA: s_so_lan_doc_lai lon nghia la
312 duong I2C that su co van de va PHAI xu ly phan cung. */
313 if ((*r > *c) || (*g > *c) || (*b > *c) ||
314 hai_byte_giong_nhau(*c) || hai_byte_giong_nhau(*r) ||
315 hai_byte_giong_nhau(*g) || hai_byte_giong_nhau(*b))
316 {
317 uint16_t c2, r2, g2, b2;
318 
319 if (tcs_doc_rgbc_tung_byte(&c2, &r2, &g2, &b2) != 0u)
320 {
321 /* Chi dem khi so doc lai THUC SU KHAC so cua cum 8 byte, tuc da chung
322 minh duoc cum 8 byte sai. Dem ngay khi nghi ngo thi con so bi thoi
323 phong: chot "hai byte giong nhau" tu no bat nham lanh tinh ~1.6% so
324 lan doc (1/256 co hoi moi kenh, co 4 kenh duoc soi). */
325 if ((c2 != *c) || (r2 != *r) || (g2 != *g) || (b2 != *b))
326 {
327 s_so_lan_doc_lai++;
328 }
329 
330 /* Chi nhan so lieu doc lai khi no HOP LE VE VAT LY. Neu doc rieng cung
331 ra so vo ly thi van de nam o CHINH CAM BIEN / module (vi du MCU phu
332 cua GY-33 dang tranh bus), khong phai o cum 8 byte - luc do giu so
333 cu de hai chot chan trong tcs_phan_loai() bat duoc va bao KHONG RO. */
334 if ((r2 <= c2) && (g2 <= c2) && (b2 <= c2))
335 {
336 *c = c2; *r = r2; *g = g2; *b = b2;
337 }
338 }
339 }
340 return 1u;
341}
342 
343uint32_t TCS_LaySoLanDocLai(void)
344{
345 return s_so_lan_doc_lai;
346}
347 
348void TCS_LayThongKeLoi(uint32_t *so_loi, uint32_t *so_lan_cuu)
349{
350 if (so_loi != NULL) { *so_loi = s_so_loi; }
351 if (so_lan_cuu != NULL) { *so_lan_cuu = s_so_lan_cuu; }
352}
353 
354void TCS_XoaThongKeLoi(void)
355{
356 s_so_loi = 0u;
357 s_so_lan_cuu = 0u;
358}
359 
360/* Ep cam bien do LAI TU DAU roi cho ket qua MOI.
361 Tat AEN roi bat lai => AVALID bi xoa, chu ky tich phan bat dau lai tu thoi
362 diem nay. Neu khong, doc phai so lieu cu (con dang tren bang tai la doc
363 nham vi tri phoi truoc do). */
364static uint8_t tcs_do_moi(void)
365{
366 uint32_t t0, han;
367 uint8_t st = 0u;
368 
369 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ENABLE, EN_PON) == 0u) { return 0u; }
370 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ENABLE, (uint8_t)(EN_PON | EN_AEN)) == 0u) { return 0u; }
371 
372 /* Cho AVALID, han = 3 lan thoi gian tich phan + 10ms.
373 Tru theo HIEU so tick de khong sai khi bo dem tick tran. */
374 t0 = HAL_GetTick();
375 han = (uint32_t)TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms() * 3u + 10u;
376 while ((HAL_GetTick() - t0) < han)
377 {
378 /* Trong luc cho (toi 154ms), tra quyen dieu khien ve cho ung dung de no
379 truot servo / quet nut / quet IR. Khong co buoc nay thi servo dung
380 hinh giua hanh trinh moi lan do mau => GIAT thay ro. */
381 if (s_ham_phuc_vu != NULL) { s_ham_phuc_vu(); }
382 
383 if (tcs_doc8(REG_STATUS, &st) == 0u) { return 0u; }
384 if (st & ST_AVALID) { return 1u; }
385 }
386 return 0u; /* qua han - cam bien treo */
387}
388 
389/* Trung vi cua mot mang nho (n = 1,3,5) - sap xep chen roi lay phan tu giua. */
390static uint16_t tcs_trung_vi(uint16_t *v, uint8_t n)
391{
392 uint8_t i, j;
393 for (i = 1u; i < n; i++)
394 {
395 uint16_t x = v[i];
396 j = i;
397 while ((j > 0u) && (v[j - 1u] > x)) { v[j] = v[j - 1u]; j--; }
398 v[j] = x;
399 }
400 return v[n / 2u];
401}
402 
403/* Doc CA 4 KENH co LOC TRUNG VI TCS_SO_MAU_LOC lan. Chong phan xa GUONG cua
404 nhua bong: mot lan doc co the vot dot bien, trung vi loai duoc diem la. */
405static uint8_t tcs_doc_rgbc_loc(uint16_t *c, uint16_t *r, uint16_t *g, uint16_t *b)
406{
407 uint16_t vc[TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX], vr[TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX];
408 uint16_t vg[TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX], vb[TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX];
409 uint8_t n = 0u, i;
410 
411 for (i = 0u; i < s_so_mau; i++)
412 {
413 if (tcs_do_moi() == 0u) { break; }
414 if (tcs_doc_rgbc(&vc[n], &vr[n], &vg[n], &vb[n]) == 0u) { break; }
415 n++;
416 }
417 if (n == 0u) { return 0u; }
418 
419 *c = tcs_trung_vi(vc, n);
420 *r = tcs_trung_vi(vr, n);
421 *g = tcs_trung_vi(vg, n);
422 *b = tcs_trung_vi(vb, n);
423 return 1u;
424}
425 
426/* ====================== TINH TOAN / PHAN LOAI ============================= */
427 
428/* Chuan hoa 3 kenh ve thang 0..255 theo TONG 3 KENH (khong chia cho kenh
429 CLEAR vi CLEAR con thu ca hong ngoai). Ket qua luon thoa R+G+B = 255, mau
430 trung tinh cho 85/85/85, va khong phu thuoc do sang lan khoang cach. */
431/* Tru mot muc nen ra khoi so dem tho, chan duoi 0. Chi tru TCS_HS_TRU_NEN %
432 de con chua cho nen troi len mot chut - tru het 100% de bi am/ve 0. */
433static uint16_t tcs_tru(uint16_t tho, uint16_t nen)
434{
435 uint32_t n = ((uint32_t)nen * TCS_HS_TRU_NEN) / 100u;
436 
437 return (tho > (uint16_t)n) ? (uint16_t)(tho - (uint16_t)n) : 0u;
438}
439 
440static void tcs_chuan_hoa(TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
441{
442 uint16_t er = kq->raw_r, eg = kq->raw_g, eb = kq->raw_b;
443 uint32_t tong;
444 uint32_t tong_tho = (uint32_t)kq->raw_r + kq->raw_g + kq->raw_b;
445 
446 /* TRU NEN truoc khi chuan hoa - day la buoc mua them tam xa. Phan con lai
447 la anh sang do CHINH VAT phan xa ve, khong lan anh sang nen. */
448 if (s_tru_nen && s_da_hoc_nen)
449 {
450 uint32_t tong_tru;
451 
452 er = tcs_tru(er, s_nen_tho_r);
453 eg = tcs_tru(eg, s_nen_tho_g);
454 eb = tcs_tru(eb, s_nen_tho_b);
455 
456 /* ⚠ LUOI AN TOAN: neu tru xong ma chi con duoi 1/8 tin hieu goc thi nen
457 da an gan het phan huu ich (nen sang ngang vat, hoac nen do sai luc
458 con vat trong tam nhin). Van mau con lai chi la nhieu - se sinh ra
459 nhung so vo ly kieu r=0 g=0 b=0 hoac b-r=+249.
460 Truong hop do: BO qua tru nen cho lan doc nay, dung so lieu THO. */
461 tong_tru = (uint32_t)er + eg + eb;
462 if (tong_tru < (tong_tho / 8u))
463 {
464 er = kq->raw_r;
465 eg = kq->raw_g;
466 eb = kq->raw_b;
467 }
468 }
469 
470 tong = (uint32_t)er + eg + eb;
471 
472 if (tong == 0u)
473 {
474 kq->r = 0u; kq->g = 0u; kq->b = 0u;
475 kq->d_br = 0; kq->d_bg = 0;
476 return;
477 }
478 kq->r = (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)er * 255u) / tong);
479 kq->g = (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)eg * 255u) / tong);
480 kq->b = (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)eb * 255u) / tong);
481 
482 /* Hai truc quyet dinh. Chuan hoa theo TONG 3 kenh nen d_br/d_bg KHONG doi
483 khi vat xa dan (chi do sang giam), day la ly do chon cach nay thay vi
484 dung so dem tho. */
485 kq->d_br = (int16_t)kq->b - (int16_t)kq->r;
486 kq->d_bg = (int16_t)kq->b - (int16_t)kq->g;
487}
488 
489/* Nguong do sang de ket luan CO VAT: C DUOI muc nay = co vat.
490 Lay theo TI LE cua muc nen dang theo doi chu KHONG dung so tuyet doi, vi
491 anh sang phong co doi (do duoc nen 313 va 566 o hai thoi diem khac nhau). */
492static uint16_t tcs_nguong_co_vat(void)
493{
494 uint32_t ng;
495 
496 /* Hai chieu dung hai he so khac nhau - xem TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN.
497 chieu 1 (vat TOI hon nen) : he so < 100, co vat khi C DUOI nguong
498 chieu 0 (vat SANG hon nen): he so > 100, co vat khi C TREN nguong */
499#if (TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN != 0u)
500 ng = ((uint32_t)s_c_nen * (uint32_t)TCS_PT_CO_VAT) / 100u;
501#else
502 ng = ((uint32_t)s_c_nen * (uint32_t)TCS_PT_CO_VAT_SANG) / 100u;
503#endif
504 if (ng > 65535u) { ng = 65535u; }
505 return (uint16_t)ng;
506}
507 
508/* Bam theo muc sang cua NEN: LEN NGAY, XUONG CHAM.
509 - Len ngay an toan tuyet doi: vat luon TOI hon nen tren rig nay nen khong
510 co vat nao keo muc nen LEN duoc. Den sang len thi bat kip ngay.
511 - Xuong cham: moi TCS_C_NEN_GIAM_MOI lan doc moi ha 1 don vi, de mot vat
512 nam lau truoc cam bien khong keo tut muc nen xuong ngang chinh no. */
513static void tcs_bam_c_nen(uint16_t raw_c, uint8_t bao_hoa)
514{
515 uint16_t tran = TCS_TranDem();
516 
517 /* BA CHOT chong mot lan doc rac keo moc nen di va lam ket ca he:
518 neu s_c_nen bi keo vot len (vd nhay len 2000 -> nguong 1000 trong khi
519 nen that la 599) thi moi lan doc deu ra "CO VAT", khong bao gio ra TRONG
520 nua, may trang thai gat ket o KHOA va servo im han. Tu thoat mat gan 8
521 phut vi moc chi ha 3 don vi/giay.
522 1) Bo qua lan doc BAO HOA - so lieu khong tin duoc.
523 2) Bo qua raw_c > tran dem cua ATIME hien tai: vuot tran la vo ly ve
524 vat ly, chac chan la so rac tu bus loi.
525 3) Muon doi moc phai thay HAI lan doc LIEN TIEP, va chi doi toi gia
526 tri bao thu hon trong hai lan do. */
527 if (bao_hoa) { s_c_len_dem = 0u; return; }
528 if (raw_c > tran) { s_c_len_dem = 0u; return; }
529 
530 /* HAI CHIEU - xem TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN trong tcs34725.h.
531 Nguyen tac chung: bo bam duoc phep di NHANH ve phia KHONG CO VAT NAO, va
532 phai di CHAM ve phia co the la vat. Doi chieu sang/toi thi hai phia nay
533 doi cho nhau nen ca ham phai lat theo - chi lat phep so sanh o
534 tcs_phan_loai() thi bo bam se BO THEO VAT va moc nen chay mat. */
535#if (TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN != 0u)
536 /* --- Chieu 1: nen la cai SANG => LEN nhanh, XUONG cham --- */
537 if (raw_c > s_c_nen)
538 {
539 if (s_c_len_dem == 0u)
540 {
541 s_c_len_dem = 1u;
542 s_c_len_tam = raw_c; /* ung vien - cho lan doc thu hai xac nhan */
543 return;
544 }
545 /* Da co hai lan lien tiep: len toi gia tri NHO HON (bao thu) */
546 s_c_nen = (raw_c < s_c_len_tam) ? raw_c : s_c_len_tam;
547 s_c_len_dem = 0u;
548 s_c_nen_le = 0u;
549 return;
550 }
551 
552 s_c_len_dem = 0u; /* chuoi len bi dut */
553 
554 if (s_c_nen <= (uint16_t)TCS_C_NEN_TOI_THIEU) { return; }
555 
556 s_c_nen_le++;
557 if (s_c_nen_le >= (uint8_t)TCS_C_NEN_GIAM_MOI)
558 {
559 s_c_nen_le = 0u;
560 s_c_nen--;
561 }
562#else
563 /* --- Chieu 0: nen la cai TOI => XUONG nhanh, LEN cham ---
564 Guong doi xung cua nhanh tren: "xuong nhanh" cung phai doi HAI lan doc
565 lien tiep, con "len cham" bi chan tren boi TRAN DEM thay vi chan duoi
566 boi TCS_C_NEN_TOI_THIEU. */
567 if (raw_c < s_c_nen)
568 {
569 if (s_c_len_dem == 0u)
570 {
571 s_c_len_dem = 1u;
572 s_c_len_tam = raw_c; /* ung vien - cho lan doc thu hai xac nhan */
573 return;
574 }
575 s_c_nen = (raw_c > s_c_len_tam) ? raw_c : s_c_len_tam;
576 s_c_len_dem = 0u;
577 s_c_nen_le = 0u;
578 return;
579 }
580 
581 s_c_len_dem = 0u; /* chuoi xuong bi dut */
582 
583 if (s_c_nen >= tran) { return; } /* khong cho moc nen vuot tran dem */
584 
585 s_c_nen_le++;
586 if (s_c_nen_le >= (uint8_t)TCS_C_NEN_GIAM_MOI)
587 {
588 s_c_nen_le = 0u;
589 s_c_nen++;
590 }
591#endif
592}
593 
594/* Phan loai 3 muc: TRONG / XANH DUONG / MAU KHAC.
595 Buoc 1: co vat khong - do DO SANG quyet dinh (dao chieu, xem trong ham).
596 Buoc 2: neu co vat -> doi HAI dieu kien xanh duong.
597 Buoc 3: co vat ma khong xanh -> MAU KHAC. */
598static TCS_Mau_t tcs_phan_loai(TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
599{
600 /* Bao hoa: mot kenh cham tran => moi mau deu keo ve trang, khong ket luan. */
601 if (kq->bao_hoa)
602 {
603 kq->co_vat = 1u; /* chac chan co gi do rat sang truoc cam bien */
604 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
605 return TCS_KHONG_RO;
606 }
607 
608 /* BUOC 0 - HAI CHOT CHAN SO LIEU RAC.
609 Phai dat TRUOC MOI buoc khac: so lieu khong dang tin thi khong duoc phep
610 ket luan BAT CU dieu gi tu no - ke ca ket luan "TRONG".
611 Xem khoi giai thich day du o TCS_TONG_TIN_TOI_THIEU trong tcs34725.h. */
612 {
613 uint32_t tong_tho = (uint32_t)kq->raw_r + kq->raw_g + kq->raw_b;
614 uint16_t kenh_lon = kq->raw_r;
615 
616 if (kq->raw_g > kenh_lon) { kenh_lon = kq->raw_g; }
617 if (kq->raw_b > kenh_lon) { kenh_lon = kq->raw_b; }
618 
619 /* (1) Tin hieu qua yeu - chuan hoa se phong dai nhieu luong tu hoa. */
620 if (tong_tho < (uint32_t)TCS_TONG_TIN_TOI_THIEU)
621 {
622 kq->co_vat = 0u;
623 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
624 s_dang_xanh = 0u;
625 return TCS_KHONG_RO;
626 }
627 
628 /* (2) Kenh mau lon hon han kenh CLEAR => byte hong tren duong I2C.
629 CLEAR khong co kinh loc nen KHONG THE nho hon kenh mau nhieu lan. */
630 if ((uint32_t)kenh_lon > ((uint32_t)kq->raw_c * (uint32_t)TCS_HS_C_HOP_LE))
631 {
632 kq->co_vat = 0u;
633 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
634 s_dang_xanh = 0u;
635 return TCS_KHONG_RO;
636 }
637 }
638 
639 /* --- Buoc 1: CO VAT hay khong - QUYET DINH BANG DO SANG C ---
640 Nguong lay theo TI LE muc nen dang theo doi (xem TCS_PT_CO_VAT). Phep so
641 sanh LAT theo TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN: chieu 1 thi C >= nguong => TRONG,
642 chieu 0 thi C <= nguong => TRONG.
643 ⚠ CA KHOI NAY bi bo qua khi TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG = 0 (cau hinh hien
644 tai) - voi man che, truc do sang khong con tach duoc gi. */
645#if (TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG != 0u)
646#if (TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN != 0u)
647 if (kq->raw_c >= tcs_nguong_co_vat()) /* sang ngang nen => dang nhin nen */
648#else
649 if (kq->raw_c <= tcs_nguong_co_vat()) /* toi ngang nen => dang nhin nen */
650#endif
651 {
652 kq->co_vat = 0u;
653 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
654 s_dang_xanh = 0u; /* vat da di khoi tam nhin -> nha tre */
655 return TCS_TRONG;
656 }
657#endif /* TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG */
658 /* --- Buoc 2: co phai XANH DUONG khong --- (XET TRUOC KHI XET NEN!)
659 d_br = b - r >= nguong_br : TRUC QUYET DINH (hut manh anh sang do)
660 d_bg = b - g >= nguong_bg : CHOT CHAN, chi de loai vat XANH LA
661 ⚠ THU TU QUAN TRONG: vung nen trai ve ca hai phia cua diem nen, xet nen
662 truoc thi phan MEP DUOI cua dai xanh duong se bi nuot thanh TRONG.
663 TRE: lan doc truoc da la xanh duong thi noi nguong truc chinh ra
664 TCS_TRE_BR don vi, de vat nam sat ranh gioi khong nhap nhay. */
665 {
666 int16_t nguong_br_hd = s_dang_xanh
667 ? (int16_t)(s_nguong_br - TCS_TRE_BR)
668 : s_nguong_br;
669 int16_t muc_nha_toi_thieu = (int16_t)(s_d_br_nen + TCS_BIEN_NHA_TOI_THIEU);
670 
671 /* ⚠ CHAN CUNG: muc giu tin hieu khong bao gio duoc tut xuong ngang NEN.
672 Neu khong, khi moc nen troi len thi chinh cai nen se du giu BLUE mai
673 khong nha => vat ra khoi vung van khong quay ve TRONG. */
674 if (nguong_br_hd < muc_nha_toi_thieu)
675 {
676 nguong_br_hd = muc_nha_toi_thieu;
677 }
678 
679 if ((kq->d_br >= nguong_br_hd) && (kq->d_bg >= s_nguong_bg))
680 {
681 kq->co_vat = 1u;
682 kq->la_xanh = 1u;
683 s_dang_xanh = 1u;
684 return TCS_XANH_DUONG;
685 }
686 }
687 s_dang_xanh = 0u;
688 
689 /* --- Buoc 3: co vat nhung khong phai xanh duong => MAU KHAC ---
690 Truoc do con mot buoc nua: van mau bam sat diem NEN thi ket luan TRONG.
691 Buoc do CHI DUNG duoc khi ba cum tach bach tren truc mau nen no duoc
692 bat/tat theo TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG:
693 khong man che : DO va NEN chong nhau tren truc mau -> phai bo buoc nay
694 va dung truc DO SANG
695 co man che : DO +20 | NEN +28 | XANH +38 -> dung buoc nay
696 ⚠ Van giu THU TU: XANH DUONG da duoc xet O TREN, truoc buoc nay. */
697#if (TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG == 0u)
698 {
699 int16_t lech_br = (int16_t)(kq->d_br - s_d_br_nen);
700 int16_t lech_bg = (int16_t)(kq->d_bg - s_d_bg_nen);
701 
702 if (lech_br < 0) { lech_br = (int16_t)(-lech_br); }
703 if (lech_bg < 0) { lech_bg = (int16_t)(-lech_bg); }
704 
705 if ((lech_br <= (int16_t)TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR) &&
706 (lech_bg <= (int16_t)TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG))
707 {
708 kq->co_vat = 0u;
709 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
710 s_dang_xanh = 0u; /* vat da di khoi tam nhin -> nha tre */
711 return TCS_TRONG;
712 }
713 }
714#endif
715 
716 kq->co_vat = 1u;
717 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
718 return TCS_MAU_KHAC;
719}
720 
721/* ===================== NEN TU BAM (auto-track) ============================
722 Goi sau moi lan phan loai. Y tuong: vat CHAY LIEN TUC qua cam bien chi nam
723 trong tam nhin duoi 1 giay; con so doc DUNG YEN nhieu giay lien nghia la
724 bang tai dang TRONG => cai dang nhin chinh la nen.
725 Xem day du ba chan an toan trong tcs34725.h. */
726static void tcs_bam_nen(const TCS_KetQua_t *kq, TCS_Mau_t mau_moi)
727{
728 int16_t l_br, l_bg;
729 
730 if (s_bam_nen == 0u) { return; }
731 
732 /* Chan an toan 1: CHI hoc tu nhung lan doc da ket luan la TRONG. Neu chi
733 loai XANH_DUONG / KHONG_RO thi mot vat ket luan MAU KHAC van duoc phep
734 tro thanh ung vien moc nen - do chinh la duong ma PHOI DO da di de keo
735 moc nen lech di (loi da gap that). */
736 if (mau_moi != TCS_TRONG)
737 {
738 s_uv_co = 0u;
739 s_uv_dem = 0u;
740 return;
741 }
742 
743 /* Chan an toan 2 - CUA SO TUNG LAN DOC: chi xet nhung lan doc bam quanh
744 moc HIEN TAI. Vat do o +8 cach moc +30 toi 22 don vi => bi loai ngay,
745 khong bao gio tro thanh ung vien. Day la cho chan "bo dan theo vat". */
746 {
747 int16_t xa = (int16_t)(kq->d_br - s_d_br_nen);
748 if (xa < 0) { xa = (int16_t)-xa; }
749 if (xa > TCS_NEN_BAM_CUA_SO)
750 {
751 s_uv_co = 0u;
752 s_uv_dem = 0u;
753 return;
754 }
755 }
756 
757 /* Con dung yen quanh ung vien hien tai khong? */
758 if (s_uv_co)
759 {
760 l_br = (int16_t)(kq->d_br - s_uv_br);
761 l_bg = (int16_t)(kq->d_bg - s_uv_bg);
762 if (l_br < 0) { l_br = (int16_t)-l_br; }
763 if (l_bg < 0) { l_bg = (int16_t)-l_bg; }
764 
765 if ((l_br > TCS_NEN_BAM_DAO_DONG) || (l_bg > TCS_NEN_BAM_DAO_DONG))
766 {
767 s_uv_co = 0u; /* dong roi -> bo ung vien, lam lai */
768 }
769 }
770 
771 if (s_uv_co == 0u)
772 {
773 s_uv_br = kq->d_br;
774 s_uv_bg = kq->d_bg;
775 s_uv_co = 1u;
776 s_uv_dem = 1u;
777 return;
778 }
779 
780 s_uv_dem++;
781 if (s_uv_dem < TCS_NEN_BAM_SO_LAN) { return; }
782 
783 /* Da dung yen du lau => day la nen. */
784 s_uv_dem = 0u;
785 s_uv_co = 0u;
786 
787 if (s_uv_br == s_d_br_nen) { return; } /* khong doi gi -> thoi */
788 
789 /* Chan an toan 3 - TRAN TONG: moc khong duoc di xa qua TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG so
790 voi NEO (gia tri bake, hoac gia tri do duoc lan go 'b' gan nhat).
791 Day la chan CUOI CUNG chong kieu "bo dan": du bo tung buoc nho bao nhieu
792 lan, tong duong di van bi giu trong tran nay. Khong co no thi vat dung
793 yen lau truoc cam bien se keo moc nen di theo minh. */
794 l_br = (int16_t)(s_uv_br - s_d_br_neo);
795 if (l_br < 0) { l_br = (int16_t)-l_br; }
796 if (l_br > TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG) { return; }
797 
798 /* Chan an toan 3: doi HAI nguong theo dung luong moc dich, de moi hieu
799 chinh tay bang lenh 'e' van duoc giu nguyen. */
800 s_nguong_br = (int16_t)(s_nguong_br + (s_uv_br - s_d_br_nen));
801 s_d_br_nen = s_uv_br;
802 s_d_bg_nen = s_uv_bg;
803 s_so_lan_bam++;
804}
805 
806/* ================================ API ==================================== */
807 
808uint16_t TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms(void)
809{
810 uint32_t b = (uint32_t)(256u - s_atime);
811 return (uint16_t)(((b * 24u) + 9u) / 10u); /* (256-ATIME)x2.4ms, lam tron len */
812}
813 
814uint16_t TCS_TranDem(void)
815{
816 uint32_t tran = (uint32_t)(256u - s_atime) * 1024u;
817 return (tran > 65535u) ? 65535u : (uint16_t)tran;
818}
819 
820uint8_t TCS_LayGain(void) { return s_gain; }
821uint8_t TCS_LayTichPhan(void) { return s_atime; }
822 
823void TCS_DatSoMauLoc(uint8_t n)
824{
825 if (n < 1u) { n = 1u; }
826 if (n > TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX) { n = TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX; }
827 if ((n % 2u) == 0u) { n++; } /* phai la so LE */
828 if (n > TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX) { n = TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX; }
829 s_so_mau = n;
830}
831 
832uint8_t TCS_LaySoMauLoc(void) { return s_so_mau; }
833 
834void TCS_BatBamNen(uint8_t bat)
835{
836 s_bam_nen = (uint8_t)(bat != 0u);
837 s_uv_co = 0u;
838 s_uv_dem = 0u;
839}
840 
841uint8_t TCS_LayBamNen(void) { return s_bam_nen; }
842uint16_t TCS_LaySoLanBamNen(void) { return s_so_lan_bam; }
843 
844const char *TCS_TenGain(uint8_t gain)
845{
846 switch (gain & 0x03u)
847 {
848 case 0u: return "1x";
849 case 1u: return "4x";
850 case 2u: return "16x";
851 default: return "60x";
852 }
853}
854 
855uint8_t TCS_DatGain(uint8_t gain)
856{
857 gain &= 0x03u;
858 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_CONTROL, gain) == 0u) { return 0u; }
859 s_gain = gain;
860 return 1u;
861}
862 
863uint8_t TCS_DatTichPhan(uint8_t atime)
864{
865 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ATIME, atime) == 0u) { return 0u; }
866 s_atime = atime;
867 return 1u;
868}
869 
870/* Nap lai ATIME / AGAIN / ENABLE sau khi cuu bus. Ghi THANG bang HAL, khong
871 qua tcs_ghi8, de khong lam khoi dong lai chinh thu tuc cuu bus. */
872static void tcs_nap_lai_cau_hinh(void)
873{
874 uint8_t gt;
875 
876 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return; }
877 
878 gt = s_atime;
879 (void)HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
880 (uint16_t)(CMD | REG_ATIME), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
881 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT);
882 gt = s_gain;
883 (void)HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
884 (uint16_t)(CMD | REG_CONTROL), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
885 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT);
886 gt = (uint8_t)(EN_PON | EN_AEN);
887 (void)HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
888 (uint16_t)(CMD | REG_ENABLE), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
889 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT);
890}
891 
892void TCS_DatHamPhucVu(TCS_HamPhucVu_t ham)
893{
894 s_ham_phuc_vu = ham;
895}
896 
897uint8_t TCS_Init(I2C_HandleTypeDef *hi2c)
898{
899 uint8_t id = 0u;
900 
901 s_hi2c = hi2c;
902 
903 /* Bat nguon noi bo truoc, cho 3ms (yeu cau cua chip) roi bat bo do */
904 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ENABLE, EN_PON) == 0u) { return 0u; }
905 tcs_cho_phuc_vu(3u); /* khong dung HAL_Delay: phai nha quyen dieu
906 khien de servo/IR/nut van duoc phuc vu */
907 
908 if (tcs_doc8(REG_ID, &id) == 0u) { return 0u; }
909 if ((id != ID_TCS34725) && (id != ID_TCS34727)) { return 0u; }
910 
911 if (TCS_DatTichPhan(TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH) == 0u) { return 0u; }
912 if (TCS_DatGain(TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH) == 0u) { return 0u; }
913 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_WTIME, 0xFFu) == 0u) { return 0u; }
914 
915 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ENABLE, (uint8_t)(EN_PON | EN_AEN)) == 0u) { return 0u; }
916 tcs_cho_phuc_vu((uint32_t)TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms() + 5u);
917 
918 return 1u;
919}
920 
921uint8_t TCS_QuetBus(TCS_ChanDoan_t *cd)
922{
923 uint8_t addr;
924 
925 if ((s_hi2c == NULL) || (cd == NULL)) { return 0u; }
926 
927 cd->so_tb = 0u;
928 for (addr = 0x08u; addr <= 0x77u; addr++)
929 {
930 if (HAL_I2C_IsDeviceReady(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(addr << 1), 2u, 5u) == HAL_OK)
931 {
932 if (cd->so_tb < 8u)
933 {
934 cd->dia_chi[cd->so_tb] = addr;
935 }
936 cd->so_tb++;
937 }
938 }
939 return cd->so_tb;
940}
941 
942uint8_t TCS_KiemTraKetNoi(TCS_ChanDoan_t *cd)
943{
944 uint16_t lon, nho;
945 
946 if ((s_hi2c == NULL) || (cd == NULL)) { return 0u; }
947 
948 cd->id = 0u;
949 cd->c = 0u; cd->r = 0u; cd->g = 0u; cd->b = 0u;
950 cd->tran = TCS_TranDem();
951 cd->ok_id = 0u; cd->ok_doc = 0u; cd->ok_sang = 0u;
952 cd->ok_khong_bh = 0u; cd->ok_tach_mau = 0u;
953 
954 /* --- [1] Bus: co thiet bi nao ACK o 0x29 khong --- */
955 TCS_QuetBus(cd);
956 cd->ok_bus = (HAL_I2C_IsDeviceReady(s_hi2c,
957 (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1), 3u, 10u) == HAL_OK)
958 ? 1u : 0u;
959 if (cd->ok_bus == 0u)
960 {
961 cd->du_dieu_kien = 0u;
962 return 0u;
963 }
964 
965 /* --- [2] Dung chip TCS34725 khong (thanh ghi ID) --- */
966 if (tcs_doc8(REG_ID, &cd->id))
967 {
968 cd->ok_id = ((cd->id == ID_TCS34725) || (cd->id == ID_TCS34727)) ? 1u : 0u;
969 }
970 
971 /* --- [3] Bat bo do va doc duoc mot chu ky moi --- */
972 if (tcs_do_moi())
973 {
974 cd->ok_doc = tcs_doc_rgbc(&cd->c, &cd->r, &cd->g, &cd->b);
975 }
976 
977 if (cd->ok_doc)
978 {
979 /* --- [4] Du sang: C phai vuot han muc nhieu nen --- */
980 cd->ok_sang = (cd->c > 50u) ? 1u : 0u;
981 
982 /* --- [5] Khong bao hoa --- */
983 cd->ok_khong_bh =
984 (cd->c < (uint16_t)(((uint32_t)cd->tran * TCS_MUC_BAO_HOA_PT) / 100u))
985 ? 1u : 0u;
986 
987 /* --- [6] 3 kenh phai khac nhau it nhat mot chut --- */
988 lon = cd->r; nho = cd->r;
989 if (cd->g > lon) { lon = cd->g; } if (cd->g < nho) { nho = cd->g; }
990 if (cd->b > lon) { lon = cd->b; } if (cd->b < nho) { nho = cd->b; }
991 cd->ok_tach_mau = (lon > nho) ? 1u : 0u;
992 }
993 
994 /* Ba muc dau la bat buoc; 4-5-6 chi la canh bao. */
995 cd->du_dieu_kien = (uint8_t)(cd->ok_bus && cd->ok_id && cd->ok_doc);
996 return cd->du_dieu_kien;
997}
998 
999uint16_t TCS_DocClear(void)
1000{
1001 uint16_t c = 0u, r = 0u, g = 0u, b = 0u;
1002 
1003 if (tcs_do_moi() == 0u) { return 0u; }
1004 if (tcs_doc_rgbc(&c, &r, &g, &b) == 0u) { return 0u; }
1005 return c;
1006}
1007 
1008uint8_t TCS_Doc(TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
1009{
1010 static TCS_Mau_t mau_truoc = TCS_TRONG; /* bo loc on dinh 2 lan doc */
1011 static TCS_Mau_t mau_chot = TCS_TRONG;
1012 static uint8_t dem_khac = 0u; /* so lan lien tiep khac mau_chot */
1013 uint16_t nguong_bh, lon;
1014 TCS_Mau_t mau_moi;
1015 
1016 if (s_xoa_loc) /* TCS_XoaLoc() da duoc goi -> quen vat cu */
1017 {
1018 mau_truoc = TCS_TRONG;
1019 mau_chot = TCS_TRONG;
1020 dem_khac = 0u;
1021 s_dang_xanh = 0u; /* nha luon TRE de do vat moi cho sach */
1022 s_xoa_loc = 0u;
1023 }
1024 
1025 if ((s_hi2c == NULL) || (kq == NULL)) { return 0u; }
1026 
1027 if (tcs_doc_rgbc_loc(&kq->raw_c, &kq->raw_r, &kq->raw_g, &kq->raw_b) == 0u)
1028 {
1029 return 0u;
1030 }
1031 
1032 /* Bao hoa: xet CA kenh C LAN kenh mau lon nhat (nhua bong co the lam mot
1033 kenh cham tran truoc ca C). */
1034 nguong_bh = (uint16_t)(((uint32_t)TCS_TranDem() * TCS_MUC_BAO_HOA_PT) / 100u);
1035 lon = kq->raw_r;
1036 if (kq->raw_g > lon) { lon = kq->raw_g; }
1037 if (kq->raw_b > lon) { lon = kq->raw_b; }
1038 kq->bao_hoa = ((kq->raw_c >= nguong_bh) || (lon >= nguong_bh)) ? 1u : 0u;
1039 
1040 tcs_chuan_hoa(kq);
1041 
1042 /* Bam muc sang cua nen TRUOC khi phan loai: nguong "co vat" o buoc 1 duoc
1043 tinh tu muc nay. Vat toi hon nen nen no chi keo muc nen xuong, va rat
1044 cham - xem tcs_bam_c_nen(). */
1045 tcs_bam_c_nen(kq->raw_c, kq->bao_hoa);
1046 
1047 mau_moi = tcs_phan_loai(kq);
1048 tcs_bam_nen(kq, mau_moi); /* tu cap nhat moc nen khi bang tai trong */
1049 
1050 if (mau_moi == mau_chot)
1051 {
1052 dem_khac = 0u;
1053 }
1054 else
1055 {
1056 dem_khac++;
1057 /* Doi ket luan khi: 2 lan doc lien tiep giong nhau (nhanh, nhu cu), HOAC
1058 da co TCS_SO_LAN_EP_DOI lan lien tiep khac voi ket luan dang chot.
1059 Ve sau chong duoc truong hop so doc dao dong qua lai giua hai nhom lam
1060 ket luan ket cung mai o gia tri cu (vat da di khoi vung ma van bao
1061 XANH DUONG). */
1062 if ((mau_moi == mau_truoc) || (dem_khac >= TCS_SO_LAN_EP_DOI))
1063 {
1064 mau_chot = mau_moi;
1065 dem_khac = 0u;
1066 }
1067 }
1068 mau_truoc = mau_moi;
1069 kq->mau = mau_chot;
1070 
1071 return 1u;
1072}
1073 
1074uint8_t TCS_LaXanhDuong(const TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
1075{
1076 return ((kq != NULL) && (kq->mau == TCS_XANH_DUONG)) ? 1u : 0u;
1077}
1078 
1079const char *TCS_TenMau(TCS_Mau_t m)
1080{
1081 switch (m)
1082 {
1083 case TCS_XANH_DUONG: return "XANH DUONG (BLUE) <== TIN HIEU";
1084 case TCS_MAU_KHAC: return "MAU KHAC (co vat, loai ra) ";
1085 case TCS_KHONG_RO: return "KHONG RO (bao hoa) ";
1086 default: return "NEN/TRONG (khong co vat) ";
1087 }
1088}
1089 
1090void TCS_DoNhayToiDa(void)
1091{
1092 /* Vat cach 10-15cm + chi co 4 den nho tren module => luon can do nhay cao
1093 nhat. Khong "tu do" gain nhu ban cu: o day gain THAP dong nghia voi mat
1094 tin hieu o xa. */
1095 (void)TCS_DatTichPhan(TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH); /* 154 ms */
1096 (void)TCS_DatGain(3u); /* 60x */
1097}
1098 
1099uint8_t TCS_ChongBaoHoa(void)
1100{
1101 /* CHI ha gain khi thuc su cham tran (vat qua gan / phong qua sang).
1102 Khong bao gio tu tang gain. */
1103 uint32_t nguong_bh = ((uint32_t)TCS_TranDem() * TCS_MUC_BAO_HOA_PT) / 100u;
1104 uint8_t lan;
1105 
1106 for (lan = 0u; lan < 3u; lan++)
1107 {
1108 uint16_t c = TCS_DocClear();
1109 
1110 if ((c >= (uint16_t)nguong_bh) && (s_gain > 0u))
1111 {
1112 TCS_DatGain((uint8_t)(s_gain - 1u));
1113 continue;
1114 }
1115 break;
1116 }
1117 return s_gain;
1118}
1119 
1120uint8_t TCS_HocSanToi(TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
1121{
1122 uint32_t san;
1123 uint8_t tru_cu = s_tru_nen;
1124 
1125 if (kq == NULL) { return 0u; }
1126 
1127 /* Do canh "TRONG" that (khong co vat) - co loc trung vi cho on dinh.
1128 TAT tru nen trong luc do, neu khong se do canh trong SAU KHI da tru
1129 chinh no => ra so vo nghia. */
1130 s_tru_nen = 0u;
1131 TCS_XoaLoc();
1132 if (TCS_Doc(kq) == 0u)
1133 {
1134 s_tru_nen = tru_cu;
1135 return 0u;
1136 }
1137 
1138 s_c_trong = kq->raw_c;
1139 
1140 /* Ghi nho so dem THO cua nen -> dung cho tru nen. */
1141 s_nen_tho_r = kq->raw_r;
1142 s_nen_tho_g = kq->raw_g;
1143 s_nen_tho_b = kq->raw_b;
1144 s_da_hoc_nen = 1u;
1145 
1146 /* ⚠ DANH GIA NEN truoc khi quyet dinh lam gi voi no. */
1147#if (TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI == 0u)
1148 /* Nguong = 0 nghia la CHUA HIEU CHINH -> LUON coi la nen sang -> khong bao
1149 gio tru nen. Viet bang #if thay vi so sanh de tranh canh bao #186-D (so
1150 sanh unsigned voi 0 luon dung). Dat TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI khac 0 thi doan
1151 duoi tu dong duoc dung lai. */
1152 (void)s_c_trong;
1153 s_nen_sang = 1u;
1154#else
1155 s_nen_sang = (uint8_t)(s_c_trong >= TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI);
1156#endif
1157 
1158 /* QUAN TRONG NHAT: ghi nho d_br CUA CHINH CANH TRONG, roi dat nguong nam
1159 TREN no mot bien an toan. Nen trong o rig nay cho b-r duong han (cam bien
1160 co thien lech mau san), nguong ma nam duoi so do thi cam bien bao XANH
1161 DUONG ngay ca khi khong co gi truoc mat. Hoc theo cach nay thi nguong
1162 luon dung voi rig + anh sang hien tai, khong phu thuoc hang so bake. */
1163 s_d_br_nen = kq->d_br;
1164 s_d_bg_nen = kq->d_bg;
1165 s_d_br_neo = kq->d_br; /* dat lai NEO cho nen tu bam */
1166 s_uv_co = 0u; /* bo ung vien dang theo doi dang do */
1167 s_uv_dem = 0u;
1168 {
1169 int32_t ng = (int32_t)s_d_br_nen + (int32_t)TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN;
1170 
1171 if (ng < TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN) { ng = TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN; }
1172 if (ng > TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX) { ng = TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX; }
1173 s_nguong_br = (int16_t)ng;
1174 }
1175 
1176 if (s_nen_sang)
1177 {
1178 /* NEN SANG (vach/ban/nen phia sau phan xa manh, sang ngang vat).
1179 - KHONG tru nen: se an gan het tin hieu cua vat.
1180 - Do sang KHONG con phan biet duoc co vat hay khong (nen va vat cho C
1181 xap xi nhau). San toi ha xuong chi con lam muc chan NHIEU, viec co
1182 vat hay khong de cho MAU quyet dinh. */
1183 uint32_t san_nhieu = (uint32_t)s_c_trong / TCS_CHIA_SAN_TOI_NEN_SANG;
1184 
1185 if (san_nhieu < TCS_SAN_TOI_TOI_THIEU) { san_nhieu = TCS_SAN_TOI_TOI_THIEU; }
1186 s_san_toi = (uint16_t)san_nhieu;
1187 s_tru_nen = 0u;
1188 
1189 TCS_XoaLoc();
1190 TCS_Doc(kq);
1191 return 1u; /* khong chay tiep phan tinh san toi duoi */
1192 }
1193 
1194 s_tru_nen = 1u; /* nen toi that -> tru nen an toan, bat len */
1195 
1196 /* San toi phai NAM TREN muc trong mot bien an toan, nhung khong qua cao
1197 keo theo vat mau toi bi bo sot. */
1198 san = (((uint32_t)s_c_trong * TCS_HS_SAN_TOI) / 100u) + TCS_CONG_SAN_TOI;
1199 if (san > 65535u) { san = 65535u; }
1200 s_san_toi = (uint16_t)san;
1201 
1202 /* Doc lai chinh canh trong => phai ra TCS_TRONG. */
1203 TCS_XoaLoc();
1204 TCS_Doc(kq);
1205 
1206 return 1u;
1207}

1274void TCS_DatNguongXanh(int16_t nguong_br, int16_t nguong_bg)
1275{
1276 if (nguong_br < 1) { nguong_br = 1; }
1277 /* nguong_bg PHAI duoc phep AM: vat xanh duong o xa cho b-g = -7..-9 (do
1278 that). Chan duoi -60 vi duoi nua la bo han chot chan chong xanh la. */
1279 if (nguong_bg < -60) { nguong_bg = -60; }
1280 s_nguong_br = nguong_br;
1281 s_nguong_bg = nguong_bg;
1282}
1283 
1284uint8_t TCS_HocXanhDuong(int16_t d_br, int16_t d_bg)
1285{
1286 int32_t br, bg;
1287 
1288 /* Vat hoc phai THUC SU la xanh duong tren TRUC CHINH: b hon han r.
1289 KHONG doi d_bg duong - tren chip nay vat xanh duong o XA cho d_bg AM
1290 (-7..-11 do that) vi kenh green dem nhieu. Chi xet truc chinh. */
1291 if (d_br <= 0)
1292 {
1293 return 0u;
1294 }
1295 
1296 /* ⚠ CHAN RAC: vat xanh duong that o rig nay cho d_br = +14..+62. Tren
1297 +120 nghia la so lieu da hong (thuong do tru nen an mat hai kenh kia,
1298 con lai b gan het 255). Hoc cai do se ghi de nguong bang so vo ly va
1299 lam cam bien khong con nhan duoc gi. */
1300 if (d_br > TCS_HOC_BR_TOI_DA)
1301 {
1302 return 0u;
1303 }
1304 
1305 br = ((int32_t)d_br * (int32_t)TCS_HS_HOC) / 100;
1306 if (br < TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN) { br = TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN; }
1307 if (br > TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX) { br = TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX; }
1308 
1309 /* Truc phu chi la chot chan: lay d_bg do duoc tru mot bien RONG, vi d_bg
1310 lao doc theo khoang cach (+10 luc gan -> -9 luc xa tren cung mot vat). */
1311 bg = (int32_t)d_bg - (int32_t)TCS_BIEN_HOC_BG;
1312 if (bg > (int32_t)TCS_NGUONG_BG_MAC_DINH) { bg = (int32_t)TCS_NGUONG_BG_MAC_DINH; }
1313 if (bg < -60) { bg = -60; }
1314 
1315 s_nguong_br = (int16_t)br;
1316 s_nguong_bg = (int16_t)bg;
1317 return 1u;
1318}

1/**

3 * @file servo_mg996r.h
4 * @brief Driver servo RC MG996R cho STM32F103C8T6 (TIM3_CH1 = PA6)
5 *
6 * NGUYEN LY: servo RC nhan xung PWM chu ky 20ms (50Hz). Do rong xung quyet
7 * dinh goc truc: ~1000us = 0 do, ~1500us = 90 do, ~2000us = 180 do.
8 * TIM3 dat Prescaler=71 => 1 tick = 1us, Period=19999 => chu ky 20ms, nen
9 * thanh ghi CCR1 chinh la do rong xung tinh bang micro giay.
10 *
11 * CHONG GIAT DONG (SLEW): khong dat goc dot ngot. Moi khung PWM do rong
12 * xung chi doi toi da SERVO_BUOC_US, nho vay servo quay muot va khong rut
13 * dong dot bien lam sut nguon.
14 *
15 * ⚠ NGUON: MG996R co dong chet may ~2.5A - PHAI cap nguon rieng (LM2596 ha
16 * 24V xuong 6V), TUYET DOI khong lay 5V tu ST-Link. Chi noi chung GND.
17 ******************************************************************************
18 */
19

22

29/* ====================== THONG SO CAN HIEU CHINH ============================ */
30 
31/* Gioi han do rong xung (us) - dai RC chuan, anh xa tuyen tinh 0..180 do.
32 ⚠ KHONG nang so 2000 len: MG996R cham chan co khi o khoang 2000-2100us,
33 ep vao do la rut dong chet may lien tuc => sut ap 6V, servo rung giat roi
34 vao bao ve nhiet. Neu canh gat lech, chinh GOC_NGHI_DO trong main.c. */
35#define SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US 1000u
36#define SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US 2000u
37 
38/* Toc do truot MAC DINH: so us do rong xung doi moi khung PWM 20ms.
39 Viec truot chay trong NGAT UPDATE cua TIM3 (dung mot buoc moi khung) nen
40 khong bi anh huong khi vong lap chinh bi chan boi I2C.
41 
42 Quy doi cho ca dai 180 do (=1000us):
43 15us -> ~1.3s | 20us -> ~1.0s | 25us -> ~0.8s | 40us -> ~0.5s
44 Cang nhanh cang de giat va cang de sut ap (dong dinh cao hon).
45 ⚠ Phai chia het 1000 (10/20/25/40/50/100) thi buoc cuoi moi rot DUNG goc
46 dich. Tranh cac so nhu 30 hay 35.
47 
48 Day chi la gia tri KHOI DONG: tung chang gat dat toc do rieng bang
49 Servo_DatTocDo() - xem bang BUOC_* trong main.c. */
50#define SERVO_BUOC_US 25u
51 
52/* Gian cach cua duong DU PHONG trong Servo_Task(), dung khi ngat TIM3 vi ly
53 do nao do khong chay. Dat 20 = dung mot khung PWM de duong du phong chay
54 cung toc do voi duong chinh. */
55#define SERVO_CHU_KY_MS 20u
56 
57/* So buoc toi da don vao MOT lan goi Servo_Task khi vong lap chinh vua bi
58 chan (co che bu theo thoi gian thuc).
59 ⚠ PHAI NHO. Hanh trinh gat chi ~900us, neu de tran lon (vd 20 buoc =
60 400us) thi servo nhay gan nua quang duong trong mot nhip, nhin nhu quay
61 loan. 3 buoc = 60us: bu duoc khuc chan ngan ma mat khong thay nhay. */
62#define SERVO_BU_TOI_DA 3u
63 
64/* Cho servo di het hanh trinh ve goc nghi luc CAP NGUON (ms). Servo_Init nap
65 do rong xung TRUOC khi bat PWM nen xung dau tien phat ra da dung goc nghi,
66 servo di thang ve day. MG996R quay 180 do mat ~0.6s khong tai.
67 De 0 = khong cho. */
68#define SERVO_CHO_VE_MS 600u
69

81 dang do. 0 se bi kep len 1. Xem bang quy doi o SERVO_BUOC_US. */

A.6

servo_mg996r.c

source

Rate-limited ramp executed in the TIM3 update interrupt

Device
STM32F103C8T6
Clock
72 MHz
Lines
227
1/**
2 ******************************************************************************
3 * @file servo_mg996r.c
4 * @brief Driver servo RC MG996R - PWM 50Hz co gioi han toc do truot
5 ******************************************************************************
6 */
7 
8#include "servo_mg996r.h"
9 
10static TIM_HandleTypeDef *s_htim = NULL;
11static uint32_t s_channel = 0;
12static volatile uint16_t s_xung_ht = 1500; /* do rong xung dang phat (us) */
13static volatile uint16_t s_xung_dich = 1500; /* do rong xung muon toi (us) */
14static uint8_t s_goc_dich = 90;
15static uint32_t s_tick = 0;
16 
17/* Theo doi ngat TIM3. s_ngat_moc = tick cua lan ngat gan nhat; Servo_Task()
18 dua vao do de biet ngat con song khong. Neu ngat chet, Servo_Task tu quay
19 ve cach truot hop tac cu (duong DU PHONG) nen servo van di chuyen duoc. */
20static volatile uint32_t s_ngat_moc = 0u;
21static volatile uint8_t s_ngat_song = 0u;
22 
23/* Toc do truot cua CHANG dang chay (us moi khung PWM). Dat bang
24 Servo_DatTocDo(). volatile vi ngat TIM3 doc no, luong chinh ghi no.
25 Ghi 1 byte tren Cortex-M3 la nguyen tu nen khong can khoa ngat. */
26static volatile uint8_t s_buoc_us = SERVO_BUOC_US;
27 
28/* ---------- doi goc (0..180) sang do rong xung (us) ---------- */
29static uint16_t goc_sang_xung(uint8_t goc)
30{
31 if (goc > 180u)
32 {
33 goc = 180u;
34 }
35 return (uint16_t)(SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US +
36 (((uint32_t)goc * (SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US - SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US)) / 180u));
37}
38 
39/* ---------- day do rong xung ra thanh ghi so sanh ---------- */
40static void xuat_xung(uint16_t us)
41{
42 if (us < SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US) { us = SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US; }
43 if (us > SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US) { us = SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US; }
44 s_xung_ht = us;
45 __HAL_TIM_SET_COMPARE(s_htim, s_channel, us);
46}
47 
48/* ---------- truot DUNG MOT BUOC ve phia goc dich ----------
49 Goi tu ngat update TIM3 (moi khung PWM 20ms), va tu duong du phong trong
50 Servo_Task(). Ngan gon co y - day la than ham ngat. */
51static void truot_mot_buoc(void)
52{
53 uint16_t con_lai;
54 uint16_t buoc = (uint16_t)s_buoc_us; /* doc MOT LAN - luong chinh co the
55 doi giua chung */
56 
57 if (s_xung_ht == s_xung_dich)
58 {
59 return;
60 }
61 
62 if (s_xung_dich > s_xung_ht)
63 {
64 con_lai = (uint16_t)(s_xung_dich - s_xung_ht);
65 if (con_lai > buoc) { con_lai = buoc; }
66 xuat_xung((uint16_t)(s_xung_ht + con_lai));
67 }
68 else
69 {
70 con_lai = (uint16_t)(s_xung_ht - s_xung_dich);
71 if (con_lai > buoc) { con_lai = buoc; }
72 xuat_xung((uint16_t)(s_xung_ht - con_lai));
73 }
74}
75 
76/* ---------- NGAT UPDATE CUA TIM3 - moi khung PWM 20ms mot lan ----------
77 ⚠ Ham nay dat o day (trong driver servo) chu KHONG o stm32f1xx_it.c, vi
78 ngat TIM3 KHONG duoc bat trong .ioc nen CubeMX khong sinh ra no. Nho vay
79 sinh lai code tu CubeMX cung khong ghi de mat.
80 ⚠ Neu sau nay co ai bat "TIM3 global interrupt" trong CubeMX thi se co
81 HAI dinh nghia TIM3_IRQHandler -> loi linker "multiply defined". Luc do
82 XOA ham nay va goi truot_mot_buoc() tu handler cua CubeMX. */
83void TIM3_IRQHandler(void)
84{
85 if (s_htim == NULL)
86 {
87 return;
88 }
89 
90 if ((__HAL_TIM_GET_FLAG(s_htim, TIM_FLAG_UPDATE) != RESET) &&
91 (__HAL_TIM_GET_IT_SOURCE(s_htim, TIM_IT_UPDATE) != RESET))
92 {
93 __HAL_TIM_CLEAR_IT(s_htim, TIM_IT_UPDATE);
94 s_ngat_moc = HAL_GetTick();
95 s_ngat_song = 1u;
96 truot_mot_buoc();
97 }
98}
99 
100/* ================================ API ==================================== */
101 
102void Servo_Init(TIM_HandleTypeDef *htim, uint32_t channel, uint8_t goc_ban_dau)
103{
104 s_htim = htim;
105 s_channel = channel;
106 
107 /* Kep o day GIONG Servo_DatGoc(): goc_sang_xung() da kep ben trong nen do
108 rong xung luon dung, nhung khong kep s_goc_dich thi Servo_GocDich() se
109 bao ra goc khong khop voi xung dang phat. */
110 if (goc_ban_dau > 180u)
111 {
112 goc_ban_dau = 180u;
113 }
114 
115 s_goc_dich = goc_ban_dau;
116 s_xung_dich = goc_sang_xung(goc_ban_dau);
117 
118 /* Dat xung TRUOC khi bat PWM: xung dau tien phat ra da dung goc nghi,
119 servo khong quat manh luc cap nguon. */
120 xuat_xung(s_xung_dich);
121 HAL_TIM_PWM_Start(s_htim, s_channel);
122 
123 s_tick = HAL_GetTick();
124 
125 /* Bat ngat update TIM3 de viec truot chay trong ngat, khong phu thuoc vong
126 lap chinh. Lam bang code chu KHONG qua CubeMX nen .ioc giu nguyen.
127 Uu tien 5: thap hon cac ngat he thong nhung cao hon SysTick (15) - than
128 ngat chi vai chuc chu ky nen khong lam troi dong ho HAL. */
129 __HAL_TIM_CLEAR_IT(s_htim, TIM_IT_UPDATE);
130 __HAL_TIM_ENABLE_IT(s_htim, TIM_IT_UPDATE);
131 HAL_NVIC_SetPriority(TIM3_IRQn, 5u, 0u);
132 HAL_NVIC_EnableIRQ(TIM3_IRQn);
133}
134 
135void Servo_DatGoc(uint8_t goc)
136{
137 if (goc > 180u)
138 {
139 goc = 180u;
140 }
141 s_goc_dich = goc;
142 s_xung_dich = goc_sang_xung(goc);
143}
144 
145void Servo_DatTocDo(uint8_t buoc_us)
146{
147 s_buoc_us = (buoc_us == 0u) ? 1u : buoc_us; /* 0 se lam servo dung han */
148}
149 
150void Servo_Task(void)
151{
152 uint32_t now;
153 uint32_t buoc_lan_nay; /* luon duoc gan trong khoi tinh bu ben duoi */
154 
155 if (s_htim == NULL)
156 {
157 return;
158 }
159 
160 now = HAL_GetTick();
161 
162 /* DUONG CHINH gio la ngat TIM3 (xem TIM3_IRQHandler o tren). Neu ngat dang
163 song thi ham nay khong lam gi - de no truot tiep se thanh truot HAI LAN
164 moi khung, servo di nhanh gap doi thiet ke. "Con song" = co ngat trong
165 100ms gan day (khung PWM 20ms nen 100ms la rat rong rai).
166 Phan ben duoi CHI chay khi ngat chet, giu lai de mot loi cau hinh ngat
167 khong lam servo dung han. */
168 if (s_ngat_song && ((now - s_ngat_moc) < 100u))
169 {
170 return;
171 }
172 
173 if ((now - s_tick) < SERVO_CHU_KY_MS)
174 {
175 return;
176 }
177 
178 /* BU THEO THOI GIAN THUC: vong lap chinh co the bi chan hang tram ms
179 (doc cam bien mau ton 33-130ms). Neu moi lan goi chi truot 1 buoc thi
180 servo nhich roi dung, nhin nhu GIAT. Tinh du so buoc dang le phai di
181 trong khoang thoi gian da troi qua. */
182 {
183 uint32_t so_buoc = (now - s_tick) / SERVO_CHU_KY_MS;
184 s_tick += so_buoc * SERVO_CHU_KY_MS;
185 
186 /* ⚠ Tran nay PHAI NHO. Hanh trinh gat chi ~900us, neu don 20 buoc
187 (=400us) vao mot chu ky PWM thi servo nhay gan NUA quang duong trong
188 mot nhip => giat manh, nhin nhu quay loan. Voi tran 3 buoc (60us) thi
189 khi bi chan lau servo chi di CHAM hon chu khong nhay coc. */
190 if (so_buoc > SERVO_BU_TOI_DA) { so_buoc = SERVO_BU_TOI_DA; }
191 buoc_lan_nay = (uint32_t)s_buoc_us * so_buoc; /* theo toc do CHANG */
192 }
193 
194 if (s_xung_ht == s_xung_dich)
195 {
196 return;
197 }
198 
199 /* Truot toi da SERVO_BUOC_US moi chu ky - khong nhay mot phat toi dich */
200 if (s_xung_dich > s_xung_ht)
201 {
202 uint32_t con_lai = (uint32_t)(s_xung_dich - s_xung_ht);
203 xuat_xung((uint16_t)(s_xung_ht +
204 ((con_lai > buoc_lan_nay) ? buoc_lan_nay : con_lai)));
205 }
206 else
207 {
208 uint32_t con_lai = (uint32_t)(s_xung_ht - s_xung_dich);
209 xuat_xung((uint16_t)(s_xung_ht -
210 ((con_lai > buoc_lan_nay) ? buoc_lan_nay : con_lai)));
211 }
212}
213 
214uint8_t Servo_DenNoi(void)
215{
216 return (uint8_t)(s_xung_ht == s_xung_dich);
217}
218 
219uint8_t Servo_GocDich(void)
220{
221 return s_goc_dich;
222}
223 
224uint16_t Servo_XungHienTai(void)
225{
226 return s_xung_ht;
227}

1/**
2 ******************************************************************************
3 * @file led7seg_595.h
4 * @brief Hai bo dem 7 doan DOC LAP, moi board 74HC595 mot bo chan rieng
5 *
6 * Hai board KHONG noi tiep (daisy-chain) va khong dung chung chan nao; giua
7 * chung chi con day nguon J2/J3. Ton them 3 chan cua STM32 nhung tranh duoc
8 * soi day lech chan de cam nham, tranh sai thu tu byte, va mot xung nhieu
9 * chi lam sai MOT den thay vi ca hai.
10 *
11 * PHAN CHAN:
12 * Board #1 - dem phoi DO Board #2 - dem phoi XANH DUONG
13 * PB12 -> ST_CP (IC chan 12) PB3 -> ST_CP (IC chan 12)
14 * PB13 -> SH_CP (IC chan 11) PB4 -> SH_CP (IC chan 11)
15 * PB15 -> DS (IC chan 14) PB5 -> DS (IC chan 14)
16 * Ca hai board: MR (chan 10) len VCC, OE (chan 13) xuong GND bang hai
17 * jumper ngay tren J1. Chan Q7' (J1 chan 1) cua CA HAI board BO TRONG.
18 *
19 * ⚠ PB3/PB4/PB5 von la chan JTAG, duoc giai phong boi
20 * __HAL_AFIO_REMAP_SWJ_NOJTAG() trong HAL_MspInit (chay vo dieu kien trong
21 * HAL_Init nen luon co hieu luc). Nap/debug bang ST-Link khong bi anh
22 * huong vi SWD chi dung PA13/PA14.
23 *
24 * KHONG DUNG SPI2: hai kenh doc lap can hai xung clock rieng ma F103C8 chi
25 * co mot SPI2. Day 8 bit bang phan mem het ~16us - khong dang ke so voi cac
26 * moc thoi gian cua gat_task - lai tu chinh duoc do rong xung.
27 *
28 * ⚠ HAI BO DEM HOAN TOAN DOC LAP, KHONG phai mot so hai chu so: moi den
29 * chay 0 -> 9 roi quay ve 0, khong co nho sang den ben canh. Vi vay file

32 */
33 
34#ifndef __LED7SEG_595_H
35#define __LED7SEG_595_H
36 
37#ifdef __cplusplus
38extern "C" {
39#endif
40 
41#include "main.h"
42 
43/* ====================== THONG SO CAN HIEU CHINH ============================ */
44 
45/* CONG TAC KHOANH VUNG LOI.
46 1 = khoi hien thi hoat dong binh thuong (mac dinh).
47 0 = TAT HAN khoi hien thi: moi ham thanh rong, khong cham chan nao, khong
48 ton chu ky CPU nao - firmware quay ve y het ban goc.
49 Dung khi he co trieu chung la (nut khong an, servo lung tung...) va can
50 biet chac co phai do khoi hien thi gay ra khong. Do xong nho dat lai 1. */
51#define LED595_BAT 1u
52 
53/* CHIEU PHAN CUC CUA LED 7 DOAN - doi dung so nay neu den khong sang.
54 0 = chan chung dong vai tro CATHODE (di xuong GND qua R1, dung theo ban ve
55 Proteus/PCB). Doan sang khi dau ra 595 muc CAO. Ma so 0x3F, 0x06...
56 1 = LED ANODE CHUNG that (chan chung len +5V). Doan sang khi dau ra 595
57 muc THAP. Ma so la gia tri DAO BIT: 0xC0, 0xF9...
58 Cach thu truoc khi han: dong ho o thang DIODE, que DO vao chan chung, que
59 DEN cham cac chan doan - neu sang thi la ANODE CHUNG => dat 1. */
60#define LED595_ANODE_CHUNG 1u
61 
62/* ANH XA DOAN -> BIT. SUA O DAY KHI DEN HIEN "NET DUT".
63 Bang nay tra loi: "doan X duoc lai boi BIT nao cua byte gui ra". Can co vi
64 con LED that co the danh chan doan khac voi footprint tren PCB, lam moi
65 chu so bi xao thanh - rieng so 8 bat het 7 doan nen van ra dung, do la dau
66 hieu nhan biet.
67 CACH DO RA BANG NAY: nap ban TEST_LED595_2BOARD voi SOI_TUNG_DOAN = 1,
68 firmware bat lan luot bit0..bit7 moi bit 1.5 giay; ghi lai thanh nao thuc
69 su sang o moi buoc roi dien vao day.
70 ⚠ Tam gia tri 0..7 va KHONG duoc trung nhau. */
71#define LED595_BIT_A 0u
72#define LED595_BIT_B 1u
73#define LED595_BIT_C 2u
74#define LED595_BIT_D 3u
75#define LED595_BIT_E 4u
76/* Do tren board that: hai doan F va G bi TRAO NHAU so voi ban ve - Q5 chay
77 doan G, Q6 chay doan F. */
78#define LED595_BIT_F 6u
79#define LED595_BIT_G 5u
80#define LED595_BIT_DP 7u
81 
82/* Do rong nua chu ky xung SH_CP, tinh bang so lan lap NOP o 72MHz.
83 40 ~ 2.2us moi nua chu ky (1 bit ~4.4us, ca hai board xong ~70us). Xung
84 rong ra de moi dao dong con sot lai tren day breadboard dai kip tat han
85 truoc suon ke tiep - cung ly do voi viec ha toc do chan xuong 2MHz.
86 ⚠ Neu VAN dut net thi tang tiep len 100 roi 200; cham lai khong mat gi vi
87 vong lap chinh quay 174ms mot lan. */
88#define LED595_TRE_XUNG 40u
89 
90/* Chu ky day lai toan bo noi dung (ms), ke ca khi so khong doi.
91 Muc dich: tu chua khi ghi dich bi nhieu lam sai - bu cho viec 74HC595 chay
92 5V nhung nhan tin hieu 3.3V (V_IH = 3.5V). Dat 0 de tat. */
93#define LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS 500u
94 
95/* ============================ GIAO DIEN ==================================== */
96 
97/* Cau hinh 6 chan (PB12/PB13/PB15 va PB3/PB4/PB5) roi hien ngay "0" tren ca
98 hai den. PHAI goi SAU MX_GPIO_Init() va SAU MX_TIM4_Init(), tuc la trong
99 khoi USER CODE 2 cua main.c. */
100void LED595_Init(void);
101 
102/* Dat so cho hai den. Gia tri >9 se tu duoc lay phan du cho 10.
103 Ham TU SO SANH voi gia tri dang hien: chi day xung khi that su doi, nen
104 goi moi vong lap cung khong ton gi. */
105void LED595_DatSo(uint8_t so_do, uint8_t so_xanh);
106 
107/* Goi trong vong lap chinh. Cu moi LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS thi day lai mot lan. */
108void LED595_Task(uint32_t now);
109 
110/* Tat het ca hai den (khong doan nao sang). */
111void LED595_Tat(void);
112 
113/* Tu kiem tra phan cung: sang het 7 doan, roi chay 0..9 tren CA HAI den, roi
114 tra ve gia tri dang dem. Chay MOT LAN luc khoi dong.
115 ⚠ CHAN khoang 1.7 giay (dung HAL_Delay) nen CHI duoc goi trong USER CODE 2,
116 TUYET DOI khong goi trong vong lap chinh. */
117void LED595_TuKiemTra(void);
118 
119/* CHAN DOAN: bat thang cac doan theo mat na, bo qua bang ma 7 doan.
120 bit0=A bit1=B bit2=C bit3=D bit4=E bit5=F bit6=G bit7=DP, bit=1 la SANG.
121 Ham tu lo phan dao bit khi LED595_ANODE_CHUNG=1. Dung de soi TUNG DOAN
122 khi den hien "net dut", tu do biet anh xa Q0..Q6 -> A..G co dung khong.
123 ⚠ Noi dung tho chi ton tai toi lan LED595_Task() ke tiep. */
124void LED595_DatDoanTho(uint8_t doan_b1, uint8_t doan_b2);
125 
126/* Doc lai gia tri dang hien - de in ra UART khi chan doan. */
127uint8_t LED595_SoDangHienDo(void);
128uint8_t LED595_SoDangHienXanh(void);
129 
130#ifdef __cplusplus
131}
132#endif
133

led7seg_595.c

source

Bit-banged output to two independent shift registers

Device
STM32F103C8T6
Clock
72 MHz
Lines
331
1/**
2 ******************************************************************************
3 * @file led7seg_595.c
4 * @brief Hai bo dem 7 doan DOC LAP, moi board mot bo chan rieng
5 *
6 * Xem toan bo giai thich phan cung o led7seg_595.h.
7 ******************************************************************************
8 */
9 
10#include "led7seg_595.h"
11 
12#if (LED595_BAT == 0u)
13/* ===== KHOI HIEN THI DA BI TAT bang LED595_BAT = 0 (xem led7seg_595.h) =====
14 Toan bo than file duoc thay bang cac ham rong. Khong dong vao chan nao.
15 Cac tham so (void) de AC5 khong bao warning #177-D. */
16void LED595_Init(void) { }
17void LED595_DatSo(uint8_t d, uint8_t x) { (void)d; (void)x; }
18void LED595_Task(uint32_t now) { (void)now; }
19void LED595_Tat(void) { }
20void LED595_TuKiemTra(void) { }
21void LED595_DatDoanTho(uint8_t a, uint8_t b) { (void)a; (void)b; }
22uint8_t LED595_SoDangHienDo(void) { return 0u; }
23uint8_t LED595_SoDangHienXanh(void) { return 0u; }
24#else
25 
26/* ==================== BO CHAN CUA MOI BOARD ================================
27 Ca 6 chan deu nam tren GPIOB nen dat/xoa duoc bang mot lenh ghi BSRR.
28 ⚠ NEU DOI CHAN: chi sua hai dong khoi tao ben duoi, KHONG phai sua cho nao
29 khac trong file. Nhung chan moi BAT BUOC phai van thuoc GPIOB - toan bo
30 lop duoi cua file nay ghi thang vao GPIOB. */
31typedef struct
32{
33 uint8_t chot; /* ST_CP - IC chan 12 */
34 uint8_t sck; /* SH_CP - IC chan 11 */
35 uint8_t dat; /* DS - IC chan 14 */
36} LED595_Chan_t;
37 
38/* Board #1 = den dem phoi DO. Giu nguyen bo chan cu de khong phai thao day. */
39static const LED595_Chan_t s_board1 = { 12u, 13u, 15u };
40/* Board #2 = den dem phoi XANH DUONG. Ba chan lien nhau tren hang chan phai
41 cua Blue Pill, cam mot khoi 3 chan la xong. */
42static const LED595_Chan_t s_board2 = { 3u, 4u, 5u };
43 
44/* ==================== BANG MA 7 DOAN (Muc 5.3 cua tai lieu) ================
45 Anh xa bit theo phan cung: bit0=A(Q0) bit1=B(Q1) bit2=C(Q2) bit3=D(Q3)
46 bit4=E(Q4) bit5=F(Q5) bit6=G(Q6) bit7=DP(Q7).
47 Chan Q7 (dau cham DP) KHONG duoc dau tren board nen bit7 luon = 0. */
48static const uint8_t g_ma7[10] =
49{
50 0x3Fu, /* 0 : A B C D E F */
51 0x06u, /* 1 : B C */
52 0x5Bu, /* 2 : A B D E G */
53 0x4Fu, /* 3 : A B C D G */
54 0x66u, /* 4 : B C F G */
55 0x6Du, /* 5 : A C D F G */
56 0x7Du, /* 6 : A C D E F G */
57 0x07u, /* 7 : A B C */
58 0x7Fu, /* 8 : A B C D E F G */
59 0x6Fu /* 9 : A B C D F G */
60};
61 
62/* ==================== TRANG THAI ========================================== */
63static uint8_t s_da_init; /* 1 = da cau hinh xong 6 chan */
64static uint8_t s_so_do = 0u; /* so DANG hien tren den board #1 */
65static uint8_t s_so_xanh = 0u; /* so DANG hien tren den board #2 */
66static uint32_t s_moc_lam_tuoi; /* tick lan day du lieu gan nhat */
67 
68/* ==================== LOP DUOI: dieu khien chan ========================== */
69 
70static void dat_cao(uint8_t chan)
71{
72 GPIOB->BSRR = (uint32_t)(1uL << chan);
73}
74 
75static void dat_thap(uint8_t chan)
76{
77 GPIOB->BSRR = (uint32_t)(1uL << ((uint32_t)chan + 16u));
78}
79 
80/* Nua chu ky xung. Dung __NOP() (lenh assembly) de trinh dich khong toi uu
81 mat vong lap nay. */
82static void tre_ngan(void)
83{
84 uint32_t n;
85 
86 for (n = 0u; n < (uint32_t)LED595_TRE_XUNG; n++)
87 {
88 __NOP();
89 }
90}
91 
92/* Dat MOT chan cua GPIOB thanh Output push-pull 2MHz.
93 Chon 2MHz chu khong phai 10MHz: suon xung 10MHz rat doc (~5ns), di tren
94 day cam breadboard dai khong co GND chay kem thi bi DOI (ringing), moi cai
95 doi la mot suon len gia lam 74HC595 dich them nhip => bit nam sai cho, den
96 hien "net dut" (rieng so 8 = 0x7F toan bit 1 nen van dung). 2MHz cho suon
97 thoai hon (~25ns) ma toc do van thua xa nhu cau.
98 ⚠ CRL giu chan 0..7, CRH giu chan 8..15, moi chan 4 bit. Bat buoc doc -
99 sua - ghi CO MAT NA: CRL con giu PB6/PB7 (encoder TIM4) va CRH con giu
100 PB10/PB11 (I2C2 cam bien mau). */
101static void cau_hinh_1_chan(uint8_t chan)
102{
103 __IO uint32_t *reg;
104 uint32_t vt;
105 uint32_t v;
106 
107 reg = (chan < 8u) ? &GPIOB->CRL : &GPIOB->CRH;
108 vt = (uint32_t)((chan & 0x07u) * 4u);
109 
110 v = *reg;
111 v &= ~(0xFuL << vt);
112 v |= (0x2uL << vt); /* MODE=10 (2MHz), CNF=00 (GP push-pull) */
113 *reg = v;
114}
115 
116static void cau_hinh_bo_chan(const LED595_Chan_t *c)
117{
118 cau_hinh_1_chan(c->chot);
119 cau_hinh_1_chan(c->sck);
120 cau_hinh_1_chan(c->dat);
121 
122 dat_thap(c->chot); /* ba chan deu nghi o muc THAP */
123 dat_thap(c->sck);
124 dat_thap(c->dat);
125}
126 
127/* ==================== LOP TREN: day mot byte ra MOT board ================= */
128 
129/* HOAN VI DOAN -> BIT theo bang LED595_BIT_x trong led7seg_595.h.
130 Bang g_ma7 o tren luon viet theo quy uoc CHUAN (bit0=A ... bit6=G), ham
131 nay doi sang thu tu chan THAT cua con LED dang dung. Mac dinh la hoan vi
132 dong nhat nen khong doi gi.
133 Chay 8 vong, va chi chay khi so DOI hoac khi lam tuoi (moi 500ms). */
134static uint8_t hoan_vi_doan(uint8_t m)
135{
136 static const uint8_t vt[8] =
137 {
138 LED595_BIT_A, LED595_BIT_B, LED595_BIT_C, LED595_BIT_D,
139 LED595_BIT_E, LED595_BIT_F, LED595_BIT_G, LED595_BIT_DP
140 };
141 uint8_t r = 0u;
142 uint8_t i;
143 
144 for (i = 0u; i < 8u; i++)
145 {
146 if ((m & (uint8_t)(1u << i)) != 0u)
147 {
148 r |= (uint8_t)(1u << vt[i]);
149 }
150 }
151 return r;
152}
153 
154static uint8_t ma_cua(uint8_t so)
155{
156 uint8_t m = (so <= 9u) ? g_ma7[so] : 0x00u;
157 
158 m = hoan_vi_doan(m);
159 
160#if (LED595_ANODE_CHUNG != 0u)
161 m = (uint8_t)(~m); /* anode chung: sang o muc THAP => dao bit */
162#endif
163 
164 return m;
165}
166 
167/* Day 8 bit vao MOT board roi chot.
168 Gui MSB TRUOC: bit vao chan DS truoc thi bi day di xa nhat, nen sau 8 xung
169 bit7 dung o Q7 (DP) va bit0 dung o Q0 (doan A) - khop bang ma phia tren.
170 
171 Trinh tu moi bit: dat muc cho DS -> cho on dinh -> nhac SH_CP LEN (595 lay
172 mau dung o suon nay) -> cho -> ha SH_CP xuong.
173 Sau 8 bit moi nhac ST_CP mot lan: suon LEN cua ST_CP day noi dung ghi dich
174 ra cac chan Q. Chot giua chung se lam den hien so dang dich do dang. */
175static void day_1_byte(const LED595_Chan_t *c, uint8_t ma)
176{
177 uint8_t i;
178 
179 for (i = 0u; i < 8u; i++)
180 {
181 if ((ma & 0x80u) != 0u) { dat_cao(c->dat); }
182 else { dat_thap(c->dat); }
183 ma = (uint8_t)(ma << 1);
184 
185 tre_ngan();
186 dat_cao(c->sck); /* suon LEN - 595 lay mau bit */
187 tre_ngan();
188 dat_thap(c->sck);
189 }
190 
191 tre_ngan();
192 dat_cao(c->chot); /* suon LEN - day noi dung ra chan Q */
193 tre_ngan();
194 dat_thap(c->chot);
195}
196 
197/* Hai board hoan toan doc lap nen day lan luot, moi board mot byte tren bo
198 chan cua rieng no. Khong con thu tu byte nao de nham, va cung khong con
199 day tin hieu nao chay giua hai board. */
200static void day_ma(uint8_t ma_b1, uint8_t ma_b2)
201{
202 day_1_byte(&s_board1, ma_b1);
203 day_1_byte(&s_board2, ma_b2);
204}
205 
206static void day_ra(uint8_t so_do, uint8_t so_xanh)
207{
208 day_ma(ma_cua(so_do), ma_cua(so_xanh));
209}
210 
211/* ==================== GIAO DIEN CONG KHAI ================================= */
212 
213void LED595_Init(void)
214{
215 __HAL_RCC_GPIOB_CLK_ENABLE();
216 
217 cau_hinh_bo_chan(&s_board1);
218 cau_hinh_bo_chan(&s_board2);
219 
220 s_da_init = 1u;
221 s_so_do = 0u;
222 s_so_xanh = 0u;
223 s_moc_lam_tuoi = HAL_GetTick();
224 
225 day_ra(0u, 0u); /* ca hai den hien so 0 ngay khi cap nguon */
226}
227 
228void LED595_DatSo(uint8_t so_do, uint8_t so_xanh)
229{
230 uint8_t d, x;
231 
232 if (s_da_init == 0u)
233 {
234 return;
235 }
236 
237 /* CHI lay phan DU cho 10. Hai bo dem doc lap, khong co nho sang nhau, nen
238 o day KHONG duoc phep xuat hien phep / 10 de tinh hang chuc. */
239 d = (uint8_t)(so_do % 10u);
240 x = (uint8_t)(so_xanh % 10u);
241 
242 if ((d == s_so_do) && (x == s_so_xanh))
243 {
244 return; /* khong doi -> khong day mot xung nao */
245 }
246 
247 s_so_do = d;
248 s_so_xanh = x;
249 s_moc_lam_tuoi = HAL_GetTick();
250 
251 day_ra(d, x);
252}
253 
254void LED595_Task(uint32_t now)
255{
256#if (LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS > 0u)
257 if (s_da_init == 0u)
258 {
259 return;
260 }
261 
262 if ((now - s_moc_lam_tuoi) >= (uint32_t)LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS)
263 {
264 s_moc_lam_tuoi = now;
265 day_ra(s_so_do, s_so_xanh); /* day lai y nguyen - tu chua neu bi nhieu */
266 }
267#else
268 (void)now;
269#endif
270}
271 
272void LED595_Tat(void)
273{
274 if (s_da_init == 0u)
275 {
276 return;
277 }
278 day_ra(0xFFu, 0xFFu); /* >9 => ma_cua() tra ve "khong doan nao" */
279}
280 
281void LED595_TuKiemTra(void)
282{
283 uint8_t i;
284 
285 if (s_da_init == 0u)
286 {
287 return;
288 }
289 
290 day_ra(8u, 8u); /* sang het 7 doan tren ca hai den */
291 HAL_Delay(500u);
292 
293 for (i = 0u; i <= 9u; i++)
294 {
295 day_ra(i, i);
296 HAL_Delay(100u);
297 }
298 
299 LED595_Tat();
300 HAL_Delay(200u);
301 
302 day_ra(s_so_do, s_so_xanh); /* tra ve dung gia tri dang dem */
303 s_moc_lam_tuoi = HAL_GetTick();
304}
305 
306/* CHAN DOAN: bat thang cac doan theo mat na, khong qua bang ma 7 doan.
307 bit0=A bit1=B bit2=C bit3=D bit4=E bit5=F bit6=G bit7=DP, bit=1 la SANG
308 (ham tu lo phan dao bit khi LED595_ANODE_CHUNG=1).
309 Dung de soi TUNG DOAN khi den hien "net dut".
310 ⚠ Noi dung tho chi ton tai toi lan LED595_Task() ke tiep (toi da
311 LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS) - sau do den tu tro ve gia tri dang dem. */
312void LED595_DatDoanTho(uint8_t doan_b1, uint8_t doan_b2)
313{
314 if (s_da_init == 0u)
315 {
316 return;
317 }
318 
319#if (LED595_ANODE_CHUNG != 0u)
320 doan_b1 = (uint8_t)(~doan_b1);
321 doan_b2 = (uint8_t)(~doan_b2);
322#endif
323 
324 day_ma(doan_b1, doan_b2);
325 s_moc_lam_tuoi = HAL_GetTick(); /* hoan lan lam tuoi ke tiep lai */
326}
327 
328uint8_t LED595_SoDangHienDo(void) { return s_so_do; }
329uint8_t LED595_SoDangHienXanh(void) { return s_so_xanh; }
330 
331#endif /* LED595_BAT */
